### [1] [A Unified 2D Framework for DeepLesion Detection, Segmentation and Short Report Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02805)
*Ruida Cheng,Tejas S. Mathai,Benjamin Hou,Qingqing Zhu,Zhiyong Lu,Matthew McAuliffe,Ronald M. Summers*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究在原始DeepLesion数据集上开发了一个统一的2D病灶分析框架，集成了LLM推理、病灶边界框检测、分割和放射学报告生成，在分割性能上相较nnUNet提升了28.5%的Dice分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前工作基于ULS23 DeepLesion数据集整合了LLM与病灶分割模型，但未处理原始DeepLesion数据集中的分割挑战。本研究旨在开发一个统一的2D病灶分析框架，解决原始数据集中分割困难的问题，并整合空间及解剖背景信息到报告生成中。

Method: 基于原始DeepLesion数据集，构建统一的2D病灶分析框架，集成LLM推理、病灶边界框检测、病灶分割和放射学报告生成模块，并引入空间与解剖上下文信息用于短报告生成。

Result: 在测试阶段，病灶边界框检测mAP50为70.1%，mAP50-95为46.4%；病灶分割Dice分数为62.6%；短报告生成的BLEU_1为64.3%、BLEU_4为49.6%、METEOR为34.7%、ROUGE_L为60.1%。分割Dice分数较nnUNet模型提升了28.5%。

Conclusion: 该统一的2D病灶分析框架有效解决了原始DeepLeson数据集中的分割难题，实现了显著的性能提升，同时将空间与解剖背景整合进报告生成中，相关实现、数据集和模型已在GitHub上开源。

Abstract: In previous work, we integrated large language models (LLMs) into the lesion segmentation model based on the ULS23 DeepLesion dataset, using short-form findings from the reports. In this study, we developed a unified 2D lesion analysis framework that integrates LLM-based reasoning, lesion bounding box detection, segmentation, and radiology report generation from the original DeepLesion dataset. In the testing phase, we achieved relatively high lesion bounding box detection accuracy with mAP50 of 70.1%, mAP50-95 of 46.4%; Lesion segmentation performance with a Dice score of 62.6%; short report generation accuracy with BLEU_1 score of 64.3%, BLEU_4 score of 49.6%, METEOR of 34.7%, and ROUGE_L of 60.1%. In this work, we address the challenging issue of segmentation in the original DeepLesion dataset and achieve a 28.5% Dice score improvement over the nnUNet lesion segmentation model. We also integrated spatial and anatomical context into the DeepLesion short report generation. We released the implementation, dataset, and models on Github. https://github.com/ruida/2D_DeepLesion_Foundation

</details>


### [2] [Localize, Don't Beautify: Client-Side Control of Image-Editing APIs for Cosmetic Surgery Previews](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02841)
*Sukhrobbek Ilyosbekov*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 这篇论文研究了仅从客户端层面控制商业图像编辑器进行整形手术预览编辑的区域限制问题。通过在210次编辑实验中测试六种商业编辑配置和一种基于掩码的图像修复模型，发现使用地标衍生的掩码进行客户端合成（masked composite）可以显著改善编辑定位（中位改善0.446），且无需访问模型内部，但所有编辑器的结果在身份保留方面均未接近术后参考照片。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 商业图像编辑器在编辑面部区域时往往会改变超出用户请求范围的特征（如鼻子编辑也平滑皮肤或改变光照）。现有方法需要访问模型内部结构，但公开的编辑API不提供这种访问。作者希望探究在仅客户端侧的有限条件下能实现多少区域控制。

Method: 设计了包含210次编辑的试点基准测试，测试六种商业编辑配置和一种掩码修复模型。对三种客户侧控制级别进行比较：仅提示词、通过地标衍生掩码将编辑区域剪切回贴到原始照片（掩码合成）、以及模型自身在掩码内修复。使用ArcFace余弦相似度衡量身份保留，CIELAB像素变化比率衡量请求区域内的变化集中度。

Result: 196次编辑可评分。掩码合成相对于仅提示词输出，在定位方面中位改善0.446（95%置信区间0.421-0.457），同时对请求区域的改变程度相当。不同编辑器在编辑强度与身份保留之间表现不同，测试的修复模型并未优于简单合成方法。所有编辑器在身份嵌入距离上均未比输入到术后基线的更接近术后照片。

Conclusion: 将手术预览编辑限制在预期区域内不需要访问模型内部——客户端侧的掩码与合成方法在所有测试编辑器中都能实现该控制，且提供商成本低。但研究仅关注控制而非临床准确性，无外科医生评估且每种条件只生成一次。

Abstract: Ask a commercial image editor to preview a cosmetic procedure and it will often change more of the face than the request names: a nose edit can also smooth skin or alter lighting. Existing methods for confining an edit to one region require access to the model's internals, which a public editing API does not expose. We ask how much control is possible from the client side alone. In a pilot benchmark, six commercial editing configurations and one mask-based inpainting model perform facelift-style jaw-neck and rhinoplasty edits at three levels of client-side control: the prompt alone; cutting the edited region out of the response and pasting it back onto the original photograph through a landmark-derived mask (a masked composite); and asking the model itself to inpaint inside the mask where supported. Of 210 attempted edits, 196 could be scored. ArcFace cosine measures identity preservation; a CIELAB pixel-change ratio measures how much change lands inside the requested region rather than a protected facial zone. On the 12 frontal faces the regional metric could score, the masked composite improved localization over the paired prompt-only output by a median of 0.446 (95% face-clustered bootstrap interval 0.421-0.457) while changing the requested region about as much. Editors differed in edit strength versus identity retention, and the one inpainting model we tested did not beat the simple composite. Against each face's input-to-postoperative baseline, no editor moved its outputs closer to the postoperative photograph in identity-embedding terms. This is a study of control, not clinical accuracy: no surgeons rated the outputs, and each condition was generated once. Within that scope, keeping a surgical preview inside its intended region needs no access to the model; a mask and composite on the client enforce it across every editor tested, at low provider cost.

</details>


### [3] [Test Time Adaptation Methods for Point Cloud Registration in Laparoscopic Surgery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02883)
*Nina Bodelot,Soufiane Belharbi,Eric Granger*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统分析并修改了三类测试时自适应（TTA）方法用于3D点云配准，特别针对腹腔镜手术中术前术后点云之间的非对称域偏移问题。通过在合成和真实数据上的评估，发现输入自适应方法最具潜力，兼具低推理延迟和稳定的配准误差降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 腹腔镜手术中3D点云配准面临域偏移问题：监督网络在合成数据上训练，但真实术中重建数据噪声大、稀疏且有遮挡，导致对应关系估计退化。现有TTA方法主要针对分类任务（依赖logits、熵、类原型等），不适用于配准任务，且配准涉及术前与术中点云间的非对称偏移，需要专门分析和改进。

Method: 分析并修改来自三个TTA家族（模型、归一化、输入自适应）的四种代表性方法：基于辅助任务模型更新、无反向传播的token清理、特征对齐和层归一化校准。修改这些方法以处理术前与术中点云间的非对称偏移，并用基于对应的配准目标替代分类熵目标。在P2P和P2ILReg数据集上，对合成目标应用8种污染（含均匀噪声和全局密度减少）及5个严重等级进行评估。

Result: 所有方法在P2P数据集上均改善了配准效果，但归一化自适应在P2ILReg上性能下降。综合考虑反向传播计算开销，输入自适应方法是最有前景的方案，提供低推理延迟并在两个数据集上持续降低误差。

Conclusion: 输入自适应TTA方法最适合腹腔镜手术中的3D点云配准场景，能够有效平衡计算效率与配准精度，代码已开源供社区使用。

Abstract: 3D point cloud registration in laparoscopic surgery estimates the transformation between an intraoperative organ reconstructed from video and its preoperative mesh. Because ground-truth transformations are unavailable for real data, supervised networks are trained on synthetic organ pairs. At test time, real reconstructions differ from synthetic data and are noisy, sparse, and occluded, which degrades correspondence estimation. Test-time adaptation (TTA) can reduce this domain shift, but existing methods mainly rely on logits, entropy, class prototypes, or cache memories unavailable in registration. Registration also involves paired inputs with an asymmetric shift that primarily affects the intraoperative cloud. We analyse and modify state-of-the-art TTA methods from three families to 3D registration: model, normalization, and input adaptation. We analyze four representative approaches based on auxiliary-task model updates, backpropagation-free token purging, feature alignment, and layer-normalization calibration. We modify them to handle asymmetric shifts between preoperative and intraoperative point clouds and replace classification-based entropy objectives. Using a correspondence-based model trained on clean synthetic source data, we evaluate adaptation to corrupted synthetic and real target data on P2P and P2ILReg. For synthetic targets, we apply eight corruptions, including uniform noise and global density reduction, at five severity levels. All methods improve registration on P2P, whereas normalization adaptation degrades performance on P2ILReg. Considering the computational overhead of backpropagation-based adaptation, input adaptation is the most promising option for laparoscopic surgery, providing low inference latency and consistent error reductions across datasets. Code: https://github.com/ninaa-git/survey_pc_registration_tta

</details>


### [4] [Modeling Scientific Experiment Scenes: Dataset and Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02892)
*Minghao Zou,Qingtian Zeng,Shangkun Liu,Cong Liu,Paul L. Rosin,Guanghui Yue,Jun Liu,Wei Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了PhysScene，首个面向物理实验场景的场景图生成（SGG）数据集，并针对其暴露的长尾关系谓词分布和视觉-文本语义鸿沟两大挑战，提出了跨模态双路径生成器（CM-DPG）模型，在PhysScene和VG150上取得了具有竞争力的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SGG基准主要聚焦日常图像，忽视了科学实验场景中专业仪器、任务特定实验语义以及密集细粒度物理关系。这些场景对自动化实验分析和智能教育日益重要，因此需要专门的SGG数据集和算法。

Method: 提出CM-DPG模型：(1) 通过联合视觉-文本编码增强对象级语义表示；(2) 利用互补的视觉和几何线索改进关系推理；(3) 采用关系感知预训练、标题派生的伪监督和自适应加权来支持头部和尾部谓词的平衡学习。同时构建PhysScene数据集，提供密集标注的场景图，并支持多种监督和协议设置。

Result: 在PhysScene和VG150上的大量实验表明，CM-DPG在多种评估设置下取得了具有竞争力的性能，消融研究验证了每个组件的贡献。

Conclusion: PhysScene填补了科学实验场景SGG数据集的空白，CM-DPG有效解决了该领域的长尾分布和跨模态语义鸿沟问题，为科学实验场景的结构化视觉理解提供了新的基准和方法。

Abstract: Scene Graph Generation (SGG) is fundamental to structured visual understanding, yet existing benchmarks focus mainly on daily life images and overlook scientific experiment scenes with specialized instruments, task-specific experimental semantics, and dense, fine-grained physical relations. These scenes are increasingly important for automated experimental analysis and smart education. To bridge this gap, we introduce PhysScene, the first SGG dataset for physical experiment scenes, providing densely annotated scene graphs and benchmarks under multiple supervision and protocol settings. PhysScene further exposes two key algorithmic challenges for SGG: pronounced long-tail relational predicate distributions and a substantial visual-textual semantic gap. To address these challenges, we propose the Cross-Modal Dual-Path Generator (CM-DPG), a model for robust open-vocabulary SGG. The model enhances object-level semantic representations through joint visual-textual encoding and improves relational reasoning using complementary visual and geometric cues. We also incorporate relation-aware pre-training, caption-derived pseudo-supervision, and adaptive weighting to support balanced learning across head and tail predicates. Extensive experiments on PhysScene and VG150 show that CM-DPG achieves competitive performance across multiple evaluation settings, with ablation studies validating the contribution of each component. The dataset and code are publicly available at https://github.com/ZMH-SDUST/CM-DPG.

</details>


### [5] [Material-Segmented Per-Pixel Emissivity Correction for Thermographic Anomaly Detection in Cultural Heritage Digital Twins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02964)
*Jonathan Klingspon,Scott McAvoy,Maurizio Seracini,Falko Kuester*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种免训练的逐像素发射率校正管线，通过SAM 3.1对RGB图像进行开放词汇分割、映射到材料发射率查找表，进而在原始辐射数据上执行逐像素逆Planck求解，以消除文化遗产表面热成像中因发射率不均导致的表观温度伪影。在物理合成基准上，平均绝对误差从1.97K降至0.91K；但在典型的室外风化遗产表面上，校正增益较小，主要集中在对真实低发射率异常的修正上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统定量长波热成像在文化遗产表面温度反演中默认全局常数发射率，但异质表面在不同材料间发射率差异显著，导致表观温度伪影既像又掩盖地下异常，亟需逐像素发射率校正方法。

Method: 该方法无需训练，利用SAM 3.1开放词汇分割将RGB图像分割为材料段，映射到基于文献编译的材料键控LWIR发射率表，再将发射率场传播到原始辐射数据的逐像素逆Planck求解中。

Result: 在物理合成基准中，20K对比度下平均绝对误差从1.97K降至0.91K，且精确查找表在所有布局上优于最优拟合全局常数；但在遗产真实发射率分布下不优于全局常数。在四个真实数据集上验证，典型室外风化遗产表面发射率接近常规默认值，校正集中在真实的低发射率异常上。主要失效模式是分割匹配外观而非材料。

Conclusion: 逐像素发射率校正在低发射率异常突出的场景中有效，但对典型风化遗产表面增益有限；研究提供了量化的工作区域图谱和实施禁忌，指出发射率定义的异常可能被抑制的反对应区域。

Abstract: Quantitative longwave thermography of heritage surfaces is limited by the global-constant emissivity assumption in inverse-Planck temperature retrieval; on heterogeneous surfaces emissivity varies within one field of view, producing apparent-temperature artifacts that mimic and mask subsurface anomalies. We present a training-free pipeline that derives per-pixel emissivity by applying SAM 3.1 open-vocabulary segmentation to a colocated, co-calibrated RGB channel, mapping segments to a material-keyed LWIR emissivity table compiled from primary measurement literature, and propagating the field into a per-pixel inverse-Planck solve on raw radiometric data. Lacking any public dataset with raw radiometry, a temperature reference, and a colocated RGB camera, we evaluate on a physics-based synthetic benchmark and four real datasets. On the benchmark, under a palette spanning the low-emissivity exceptions, the correction cuts mean absolute error from 1.97 K to 0.91 K at 20 K contrast and, with an accurate table, beats the best fitted global constant on every layout; on a heritage-realistic emissivity distribution it does not. We contribute a quantified operating-regime map, and a measurement-backed finding that tempers the heritage claim: weathered outdoor heritage emissivities cluster near the conventional default, so the correction is small on typical surfaces and concentrated on genuine low-emissivity exceptions. We characterize the dominant failure mode, in which open-vocabulary segmentation matches appearance rather than material, and the contraindicated regime in which emissivity-defined anomalies are suppressed.

</details>


### [6] [Qwen-3D: A Generalist 3D Vision-Language Model for Spatial Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02980)
*Lucy Lin,Ayush Jain,Yifan Liu,Katerina Fragkiadaki*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Qwen-3D，一种基于几何感知的多模态大模型，通过3D旋转位置编码和查询式分割解码器，将视觉信息压缩到3D场景空间中，实现长视频高效推理、指称分割和视觉问答的统一，在多项基准上超越现有3D LMM和大型专有2D模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LMM在处理长视频时受限于帧级分词化和有限的上下文窗口；已有的3D LMM虽然利用几何感知表征提升了空间推理能力，但在grounding和分割任务上仍落后于专门化的3D感知系统。作者认为关键瓶颈在于几何感知解码：现有方法通过语言token、候选框选择或轻量级grounding查询来传达3D预测，在语言推理与稠密几何预测之间形成了瓶颈。

Method: Qwen-3D在Qwen骨干网络内部利用多视角几何线索压缩视觉信息，主要包含两个核心设计：(1) 用3D旋转位置编码（3D RoPE）增强视觉token，使注意力直接在3D场景空间而非独立图像帧上进行，实现可扩展的跨视角和时间推理；(2) 引入基于查询的分割解码器，将语言直接映射到底层3D场景表示中，统一指称grounding、实例分割和多模态问答。

Result: 在多个多样化基准上，Qwen-3D超越了现有3D LMM，并优于若干大型专有2D模型；同时通过在2D和3D数据上联合训练，在标准2D视觉语言基准上保持强性能。

Conclusion: Qwen-3D证明了以3D几何作为压缩机制并结合几何感知解码是突破LMM长视频推理瓶颈的有效路径，且联合2D/3D训练不会损害原有2D能力，为未来多模态模型的长视频理解和3D空间推理提供了新方向。

Abstract: Large Multimodal Models (LMMs) have achieved remarkable success on images and short videos, yet scaling them to long videos remains challenging due to frame-centric tokenization and limited context windows. 3D geometry provides a natural compression mechanism for visual streams: depth and camera pose enable observations from multiple views and time steps to be fused into a persistent, world-aligned representation. While recent 3D LMMs leverage geometry-aware representations to improve spatial reasoning, they continue to lag behind specialist 3D perception systems on grounding and segmentation tasks. We argue that a key limitation is geometry-aware decoding: existing methods communicate 3D predictions through language tokens, proposal selection, or lightweight grounding queries, creating a bottleneck between language reasoning and dense geometric prediction. Building on these insights, we introduce Qwen-3D, a geometry-aware LMM that compresses visual information within the Qwen backbone using multi-view geometric cues, enabling efficient long-horizon visual reasoning over static scenes. Qwen-3D augments visual tokens with 3D Rotary Positional Embeddings, allowing attention to operate directly in 3D scene space rather than across independent image frames and thereby facilitating scalable cross-view and temporal reasoning. To bridge language and geometry, Qwen-3D incorporates a query-based segmentation decoder that grounds language directly in the underlying 3D scene representation, unifying referential grounding, instance segmentation, and visual question answering across both images and videos. Across a diverse set of benchmarks, Qwen-3D surpasses existing 3D LMMs and outperforms several large proprietary 2D models. Notably, Qwen-3D achieves these improvements while maintaining strong performance on standard 2D vision-language benchmarks by jointly training on 2D and 3D data.

</details>


### [7] [V-FIND: Revealing the Intrinsic Forgery Knowledge Encoded in Video Forgery Detectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03008)
*Shichao Kan,Chengpeng Hong,Jingtong Dou,Chuancheng Shi,Yuhan Liu,Linrui Xu,Yixiong Liang,Yigang Cen,Yanpeng Sun,Fei Shen,Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出V-FIND框架，通过发现并激活视频伪造检测器中稀疏的功能性神经元来实现伪造视频检测，无需全模型重训练，仅冻结骨干网络并训练轻量线性分类器即可在多个外部基准上取得强检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频伪造检测方法通常将检测器作为黑盒整体优化，忽略了模型中潜在的伪造判别知识。作者质疑是否可以不必依赖资源密集的全模型重训练，而是通过发现和激活检测器内部稀疏的取证知识来实现视频伪造检测。

Method: V-FIND框架包含两个关键步骤：首先定位在真实与伪造视频之间表现出显著差异的关键层，然后识别持续携带伪造判别信号的潜在锚定神经元，并将其组织成紧凑的取证子空间。在冻结原始骨干网络的情况下，仅训练一个轻量线性分类器。

Result: 在多个外部生成视频基准上，该取证子空间配合轻量线性分类器展现出强检测性能。神经元干预实验进一步直接证明了所发现神经元的功能特异性。

Conclusion: 视频伪造检测器内部包含稀疏、可提取且可复用的伪造判别知识，为理解和利用检测器内在取证能力提供了新视角。

Abstract: As generated videos become increasingly realistic, reliable video forgery detection is increasingly important. Existing studies typically optimize and use video forgery detectors as black boxes, while the latent forgery-discriminative knowledge inside them remains largely unexplored. Instead of continuing to rely on resource-intensive full-model retraining to steadily improve detection performance, we ask whether video forgery detection can also be achieved by uncovering and activating sparse forensic knowledge within the detector. We find that forgery-discriminative knowledge is not uniformly distributed across the full representation space, but is concentrated in a sparse set of functionally specialized neurons. Based on this insight, we propose a video forgery-intrinsic neuron discovery (V-FIND) framework. V-FIND first localizes critical layers that exhibit pronounced discrepancies between real and forged videos, and then identifies latent anchor neurons that consistently carry forgery-discriminative signals, organizing them into a compact forensic subspace. With the original backbone frozen and only a lightweight linear classifier trained, this subspace still delivers strong detection performance across multiple external benchmarks for generated videos. Further neuron intervention experiments provide direct evidence for the functional specificity of the discovered neurons. Overall, these results suggest that video forgery detectors contain sparse, extractable, and reusable forgery-discriminative knowledge, offering a new perspective on understanding and exploiting their intrinsic forensic capability.

</details>


### [8] [Clinically-Grounded Hierarchical Classification for Consistent Chest X-ray Interpretation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03016)
*Jong Hak Moon,Minjun Kim,Minjun Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CHASE框架，通过三级别分类体系（9个解剖区域、17个亚区域、28个病理发现）在共享Vision Transformer骨干网络中联合优化多级监督、跨级概率对齐和层级违规惩罚，实现胸部X光片从粗到细的层级化解读，并优于扁平与层级基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 胸部X光解读本质上是层级化的——临床决策不仅取决于存在什么异常，还取决于异常的位置。然而现有自动化系统大多将其视为扁平分类问题，未能捕获层级间的依赖关系，也无法保证粗粒度与细粒度预测之间的一致性。

Method: 提出CHASE（分类与层级分析及结构化约束）框架，是一个统一的单阶段框架，包含：(1) 基于临床驱动的三层级分类法（9个解剖区域、17个亚区域、28个病理发现）；(2) 在共享Vision Transformer骨干网络中联合优化多级监督；(3) 跨级概率对齐机制；(4) 层级违规惩罚项，确保细粒度发现由粗粒度上下文提供解剖支持。

Result: 实验表明CHASE在所有层级上均优于扁平基线和层级基线方法，同时实现了更优的概率层级一致性，层级级注意力图证实了预测具有解剖学依据。

Conclusion: 通过结构化层级约束和跨级对齐，CHASE有效模拟了放射科医师从粗到细的推理过程，证明了层级建模对医学影像分类的重要性，并为层级化医学影像诊断提供了新的思路。

Abstract: Accurate chest X-ray interpretation is inherently hierarchical. Clinical decisions depend not only on what abnormality is present but where it is situated, requiring reasoning from broad anatomical systems down to specific pathological findings. Yet existing automated systems largely treat this as a flat classification problem, failing to capture inter-level dependencies or enforce coherence between coarse and fine predictions. We propose CHASE (Classification with Hierarchical Analysis and Structured Enforcement), a unified single-stage framework that mirrors radiologists' coarse-to-fine reasoning through a clinically driven three-level taxonomy of 9 anatomical regions, 17 sub-regions, and 28 pathological findings. CHASE jointly optimizes multi-level supervision, cross-level probability alignment, and a hierarchy-violation penalty within a shared Vision Transformer backbone. This ensures that fine-grained findings are anatomically supported by their coarser-level context rather than predicted in isolation. Experiments demonstrate that CHASE outperforms flat and hierarchical baselines across all levels while achieving superior probabilistic hierarchy consistency, with level-wise attention maps confirming anatomically grounded predictions. Code is available at: https://github.com/yejix-ai/CHASE.

</details>


### [9] [Standalone DINOv3 for Training-Free Open-Vocabulary Semantic Segmentation in Remote Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03023)
*Changhao Zhao,Haoxiang Li,Yuke Li,Hai Liu,LingLin Zeng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DinoSplat-OV，一个无需训练的开放词汇遥感语义分割框架，基于DINOv3模型，通过文本感知拉普拉斯传播模块和高斯泼溅上采样模块处理遥感图像的大尺寸、多尺度与密集分布特性，在UDD5、DOTA和LoveDA数据集上取得了优于现有训练免方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遥感语义分割受限于昂贵的像素级标注成本，促使研究者探索无需训练的开放词汇方法。DINOv3（DINO.txt）首次赋予DINO骨干网络图文对比学习能力，为开放词汇分割提供了可能，但尚未有工作将其应用于遥感场景。

Method: 提出DinoSplat-OV免训练框架，包含两个核心模块：1）文本感知拉普拉斯传播模块，通过结合文本语义亲和力与局部视觉相似性去噪patch级预测，保持区域一致性的同时保留边界；2）高斯泼溅上采样模块，通过RGB引导的各向异性聚合和测试时优化重建像素级特征。此外采用全局锚点滑动窗口策略支持大尺寸遥感图像。

Result: 在UDD5、DOTA和LoveDA三个遥感数据集上，DinoSplat-OV展现出与现有免训练方法相比具有竞争力或更优的性能，填补了DINO系列模型在免训练开放词汇分割方面的空白。

Conclusion: DinoSplat-OV有效验证了DINOv3在遥感开放词汇分割中的潜力，为免训练开放词汇分割方向提供了新的可行路径。

Abstract: Remote sensing semantic segmentation is hindered by costly pixel-level annotations, motivating training-free open-vocabulary methods. Recently, the recent release of DINOv3 brings DINO.txt, which equips the standalone DINO backbone with image-text contrastive learning and thus opens up the possibility of open-vocabulary segmentation. We propose DinoSplat-OV, a training-free framework that adapts DINOv3 to remote sensing without fine-tuning or additional pretraining. Targeting the dense distribution, multi-scale nature, and large size of remote sensing imagery, we design two core modules. Its Text-aware Laplacian Propagation module de-noises patch-level predictions by combining textual semantic affinities with local visual similarity, improving regional consistency while preserving boundaries. Its Gaussian Splatting Upsampling module reconstructs pixel-level features through RGB-guided anisotropic aggregation and test-time optimization. A global-anchor sliding-window strategy further supports large-scale imagery. Experiments on UDD5, DOTA, and LoveDA demonstrate competitive or superior performance over existing training-free methods, effectively filling the gap of DINO-series models in training-free open-vocabulary segmentation and providing a viable new path for further advances in this direction.

</details>


### [10] [CAPE-T2V: Captioner-Anchored Prompt Enhancement toward Two-Sided Conditioning Alignment in Text-to-Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03046)
*Yizhuo Jia,Jingyun Hua,Yuanxing Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CAPE-T2V框架，通过两阶段"字幕锚定提示增强"策略（先微调提示增强器PE，再用锚定PE重写视频字幕并微调DiT），缩小文本生成视频中推理时PE输出与训练时详细字幕之间的"PE-字幕差距"（PE-Caption gap），在多个基准上取得更优效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本生成视频扩散Transformer（T2V DiTs）在训练时使用详细视频字幕，但推理时依赖提示增强器（PE）对用户提示进行改写，导致训练与推理之间存在分布不匹配。现有方法虽尝试通过共享schema缩小差距，但推理时PE输出与训练字幕在细节选择、信息组织、描述粒度等方面仍有残余差异，作者将这种残余不匹配定义为PE-Caption gap。

Method: CAPE-T2V采用两步框架实现双侧条件对齐：（1）构造三类PE训练样本——将简洁源字幕、详细源字幕和由目标派生出的伪用户提示分别与"字幕生成器产生的目标"配对，微调PE使其将各输入映射到对应目标；（2）使用锚定PE（Anchored PE）重写视频派生字幕，并据此微调DiT；推理时同一PE负责改写用户提示。

Result: 与使用相同字幕schema的基线相比，CAPE-T2V在StoryEval、VBench-2.0和T2V-CompBench上于Wan2.2和LTX-2.3模型中取得更高的综合得分。此外，通过固定嵌入空间中的平方最大均值差异（MMD²）衡量，CAPE-T2V的DiT微调字幕与推理时PE输出在分布上更接近，即PE-Caption gap更小。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明CAPE-T2V是缓解PE-Caption gap的有效方法，通过PE与DiT的两侧对齐训练，能够提升T2V生成质量并缩小训练-推理分布差异。

Abstract: Text-to-video (T2V) diffusion transformers (DiTs) are trained with detailed video captions, whereas inference often relies on user prompts rewritten by a prompt enhancer (PE). Prior work has improved generation by optimizing the PE, the DiT, or both; some methods have also sought to narrow the training-inference mismatch through shared schemas. Yet even within a shared schema, inference-time PE outputs and DiT training captions may still differ in detail selection, information organization, descriptive granularity, and phrasing. We refer to this residual mismatch as the PE-Caption gap and introduce CAPE-T2V, a two-step Captioner-Anchored Prompt Enhancement framework toward two-sided conditioning alignment in T2V generation. First, CAPE-T2V constructs three types of PE training examples, pairing captioner-generated targets with concise source captions, detailed source captions, or pseudo user prompts derived from those targets. It then fine-tunes the PE to map each input to its paired target. Second, CAPE-T2V fine-tunes the DiT on video-derived captions rewritten by the Anchored PE; the same PE rewrites user prompts at inference. Relative to a baseline using the same caption schema, CAPE-T2V achieves higher aggregate scores on StoryEval, VBench-2.0, and T2V-CompBench across Wan2.2 and LTX-2.3. Further, CAPE-T2V exhibits a smaller PE-Caption gap than the baseline: its DiT fine-tuning captions are closer in distribution to inference-time PE outputs, as measured by squared maximum mean discrepancy in a fixed embedding space. Overall, these results support CAPE-T2V as an effective approach to mitigating the PE-Caption gap. The project is available at https://github.com/yizzz927/CAPE-T2V.

</details>


### [11] [AIDE: Automated Instruction via Distilled Expertise for Reference-Free Motor Skill Coaching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03047)
*Yoshiki Ito*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出AIDE框架，利用专家参考仅在训练阶段蒸馏知识，推理时仅需学习者姿态序列即可生成运动技能指导反馈，性能优于无参考基线，与需专家示范的方法相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 运动技能的自然语言教练反馈能加速学习，但专家教练稀缺昂贵。现有基于参考的方法在训练和推理时都需要专家示范，限制了实际部署。因此需要一种仅在训练时利用专家参考、推理时无需参考的方法。

Method: 分为教师和学生两个模型。教师模型通过冻结语言模型从配对的学习者-专家姿态中学习生成反馈，分别产生学习者token和编码差异的差异token；学生模型继承教师的编码器和权重初始化，用一个辅助模块从学习者单独的姿态中生成互补token，替代显式的专家比较。

Result: 在ExpertAF数据集上，AIDE在大多数指标上优于无参考基线，性能与需要在训练和推理时都使用专家示范的方法相当，LLM评估也支持这些发现。

Conclusion: AIDE实现了仅需学习者在推理时姿态序列即可生成高质量教练反馈的目标，通过训练阶段的专家知识蒸馏克服了参考方法的部署局限性，在性能与实用性之间取得了良好平衡。

Abstract: Generating natural-language coaching feedback on motor skills can accelerate learning, yet expert coaches are scarce and expensive. Existing reference-based methods require expert demonstrations at both training and inference time, limiting practical deployment. We propose AIDE (Automated Instruction via Distilled Expertise), a framework that exploits expert references only during training and generates feedback from a learner's pose sequence alone at inference. A teacher model first learns to generate feedback from paired learner-expert poses via a frozen language model, producing separate learner tokens and difference tokens that encode the learner-expert difference. A student model then inherits the teacher's encoder and weight initialization, replacing the explicit expert comparison with an auxiliary module that produces complementary tokens from the learner's pose alone. On the ExpertAF dataset, AIDE outperforms reference-free baselines on most metrics and performs comparably to methods requiring expert demonstrations at both training and inference, with LLM-based evaluation supporting these findings.

</details>


### [12] [PDD-RRG: Posterior Diagnostic Decision for Study-level Radiology Report Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03055)
*Yang Yu,Yiming Ji,Bin Dai,Dong Zhang,Zhiyong Zhou,Shoushan Li,Yakang Dai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种后验诊断决策框架（PDD-RRG），在报告生成之后引入决策阶段，通过贝叶斯后验概率和学习的阈值整合多个视角生成的报告，从而提升现有放射学报告生成模型的临床效果，且无需重新训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的放射学报告生成（RRG）方法未能充分利用检查相关的全部信息，尽管有些工作尝试引入多视图图像和历史数据，但这些额外输入有时反而会导致可避免的诊断错误。

Method: 首次在报告生成之后引入决策阶段：创建多种输入数据子集，利用现有RRG模型从不同视角生成报告；然后计算每个临床观察的贝叶斯后验概率和学习到的阈值，获得聚合的诊断结论；最后用该结论来精炼生成的报告。

Result: 在MIMIC-CXR数据集上的实验表明，PDD-RRG能够有效提升现有RRG模型的临床效能，且无需任何重新训练。

Conclusion: 通过引入报告生成后的决策阶段，利用贝叶斯后验概率整合潜在冲突的诊断，可以在不重新训练现有模型的情况下提升放射学报告生成的临床效果。

Abstract: Automatic radiology report generation (RRG) aims to simulate the workflow of radiologists, assisting them in clinical diagnosis. However, existing methods often fall short in utilizing all information relevant to the examination, as is typically done in clinical practice. Although some works attempt to incorporate multi-view images and historical data, these additional inputs may sometimes lead to avoidable diagnostic errors on the contrary. To address these challenges, we introduce a decision-making stage after report generation for the first time and propose a Posterior Diagnostic Decision framework (PDD-RRG) to integrate potentially conflicting diagnoses. Specifically, we create various subsets of input data and utilize an existing RRG model to generate reports from different perspectives. Then the Bayesian posterior probability and the learned thresholds for each clinical observation are calculated to obtain an aggregated diagnostic conclusion, which is subsequently used to refine the generated report. Experiments on MIMIC-CXR demonstrate that our proposed PDD-RRG can effectively enhance the clinical efficacy of existing RRG models without any retraining.

</details>


### [13] [TASQ: Temporal-Adaptive Bit Sparsification Quantization for Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03057)
*Seokho Han,Dongwei Wang,Jinhee Kim,Yiran Chen,Kang Eun Jeon,Huanrui Yang,Jong Hwan Ko*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TASQ提出了一种时间自适应比特稀疏量化方法，通过共享最大精度权重缓冲区并学习一个时间-空间LSB掩码，在不同去噪阶段为每层选择不同的有效精度，将存储成本与计算成本分离，在不牺牲模型质量的前提下显著降低计算量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 静态量化对所有去噪步骤统一使用相同的权重精度，必须迁就最敏感的步骤，导致许多不敏感步骤也浪费算力。现有方法无法在保持存储固定的同时降低不同去噪阶段的计算量。

Method: TASQ维护一个共享的最大精度权重缓冲区，通过学习时间-空间LSB掩码（Temporal-Spatial LSB Mask）在每个去噪阶段和层中截断最低有效位来选择较低的有效精度。配合Temporal-Precision Engine将学习到的调度映射到位串行执行，使得周期数与有效精度成比例，且切换精度无周期开销。

Result: 在PixArt-Sigma、SANA-1.6B和SDXL-Turbo上，TASQ达到与静态量化相当的质量但计算量更低；结合Temporal-Precision Engine相比静态量化减少25%-50%的执行周期，相比朴素静态8位位串行执行加速6.1-7.5倍。

Conclusion: TASQ有效解耦了扩散模型量化中的存储成本与计算成本，证明在不同去噪阶段动态调整精度可以显著减少计算开销且不损失质量，为扩散模型的量化提供了一种新的高效范式。

Abstract: Static quantization assigns one weight precision to every denoising step. To preserve quality, that precision must accommodate the most quantization-sensitive step, even though many other steps can tolerate fewer bits. The resulting model may satisfy its memory budget, but it repeatedly pays worst-case arithmetic throughout the denoising trajectory. We introduce Temporal-Adaptive Bit Sparsification Quantization (TASQ) to separate these two costs. TASQ stores one shared maximum-precision weight buffer and learns a Temporal-Spatial LSB Mask that selects a lower effective precision for each layer and denoising stage by truncating least-significant bits. Storage therefore remains fixed by the worst case, while BitOPs decrease at less sensitive stages without per-stage weight copies or runtime search. A Temporal-Precision Engine maps the learned schedule to bit-serial execution, where cycles scale with effective precision and switching precision has no measured cycle overhead. On PixArt-Sigma, SANA-1.6B, and SDXL-Turbo, TASQ achieves quality comparable to static quantization with less computation. Together with the Temporal-Precision Engine, it reduces execution cycles by 25 to 50 percent over static quantization and by 6.1 to 7.5x over a naive static 8-bit bit-serial execution. Code is available at https://github.com/seokho-han/tasq.

</details>


### [14] [RIDGE: Re-Noising with Internal Dynamic Guidance for Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03059)
*Ruiliang Gong,Zhen Wang,Yanghao Wang,Long Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RIDGE是一种免反转、免训练的流式图像编辑方法，通过引入"内部动态引导"机制，将编辑状态作为不可用干净目标状态的渐进近似，并用相同噪声水平重新加噪，使噪声位移随噪声水平自然收缩，从而避免等位移构造在高层噪声下更新过于激进的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的免反转流式图像编辑方法采用等位移构造，即保持源状态与目标侧状态之间的位移在不同噪声水平下不变。然而这与加噪过程不一致——两个干净状态在相同噪声水平和噪声样本下加噪后，其位移应随噪声水平增加而收缩。等位移构造会导致高层噪声下更新过于激进，影响编辑质量。

Method: RIDGE将编辑状态视为不可用干净目标状态的渐进近似，使用与干净源状态相同的噪声水平和噪声样本对该近似重新加噪，使噪声位移随噪声增加自然减小。由于初始编辑状态包含的目标语义有限，RIDGE在早期高噪声步骤中应用内部动态引导：利用干净目标状态预测通过模型内部生成的软动态掩码来引导临时编辑状态，将引导集中于需要修改的区域，无需外部分割或检测模型。

Result: 在两个基准上使用两个骨干模型（SD3 Medium和FLUX.1-dev）进行实验，结果表明RIDGE在源保真度、目标对齐和感知质量之间取得了有利的综合权衡。

Conclusion: RIDGE通过重新加噪策略和内部动态引导机制解决了等位移构造在高噪声水平下的缺陷，在无需训练和无需外部模型的情况下实现了更好的图像编辑效果。

Abstract: Inversion-free flow-based image editing avoids latent inversion, but still requires a target-side state at every editing step. The widely used equal-displacement construction keeps the displacement between the noisy source state and the target-side state unchanged across noise levels. This is inconsistent with noising, under which the displacement between two clean states noised with the same noise level and noise sample should contract as the noise level increases. Thus, it can lead to overly aggressive updates at high noise levels. We introduce RIDGE: Re-Noising with Internal Dynamic Guidance for Image Editing, an inversion-free and training-free method that maintains the edited state as an evolving approximation to the unavailable clean target state. RIDGE re-noises this approximation using the same noise level and noise sample as the clean source state, allowing their noisy displacement to decrease naturally with increasing noise. Since the edited state initially contains limited target semantics, RIDGE further applies internal dynamic guidance during the early high-noise steps. A clean target state prediction guides the provisional edited state through a soft dynamic mask derived internally from the model, focusing guidance on regions that require modification without external segmentation or detection models. Experiments on two benchmarks using two backbones, SD3 Medium and FLUX.1-dev, show that RIDGE offers a favorable aggregate trade-off among source preservation, target alignment, and perceptual quality.

</details>


### [15] [Global Graph-Validated Optimization for VLM-based 3D Indoor Scene Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03064)
*Jialu Huang,Yingxuan You,Fei Wang,Zheng Dang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种开放词汇3D室内布局生成方法，通过图结构中间表示将语义一致性与物理可行性分离，结合全局语义验证（GSV）和全局物理可行性搜索（GPFS）两阶段策略，在语义一致性和物理合理性上达到最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM/VLM的3D室内布局生成方法大多隐式建模资产间关系或依赖局部成对约束与局部优化，这与全局、高度非凸的布局空间不匹配，往往产生局部合理但全局不一致或物理上不可行的场景。

Method: 方法包含两个核心模块：(1) 全局语义验证（GSV）：将场景表示为结构化图，通过基于规则的验证强化语义约束，移除矛盾配置，生成全局一致的语义脚手架；(2) 全局物理可行性搜索（GPFS）：结合进化搜索进行全局探索与梯度优化进行局部利用，减少对VLM初始化提议的依赖，增强在非凸和不连续可行空间中的鲁棒性。

Result: 实验表明该方法在开放词汇3D室内布局生成任务上达到最先进性能，同时改善了语义一致性和物理合理性。

Conclusion: GSV与GPFS将布局生成从局部关系建模和初始化敏感的优化，推向全局一致的推理与搜索，有效解决了局部合理但全局不一致的难题。

Abstract: We study open-vocabulary 3D indoor layout generation, which synthesizes diverse and physically plausible scenes from unlabeled 3D assets using free-form language instructions. Recent methods leverage large language models (LLMs) and vision-language models (VLMs) to generate structured scenes from text. However, most model inter-asset relations implicitly or rely on local pairwise constraints and local optimization. These formulations are poorly aligned with the global, highly non-convex layout space, often yielding locally plausible yet globally inconsistent or physically infeasible scenes. We address this problem with a graph-based intermediate representation that separates semantic coherence from physical feasibility, together with a hybrid search-and-refinement strategy. First, Global Semantic Verification (GSV) represents scenes as structured graphs and enforces semantic constraints through rule-based verification. This explicit validation removes contradictory configurations and produces a globally consistent semantic scaffold. Second, Global Physical Feasibility Search (GPFS) combines evolutionary search for global exploration with gradient-based refinement for local exploitation. It reduces dependence on VLM-proposed initialization and improves robustness in non-convex and discontinuous feasible spaces. Together, GSV and GPFS move layout generation beyond local relational modeling and initialization-sensitive optimization toward globally consistent reasoning and search. Experiments show that our method achieves state-of-the-art performance in open-vocabulary 3D indoor layout generation, improving both semantic consistency and physical plausibility.

</details>


### [16] [LDU-Bench: Multimodal LLM Evaluation for Lithography Defect Understanding under Layout-Varying Circuit Backgrounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03078)
*Huanglong Ji,Botong Zhao,Shujing Lv,Yue Lv*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出LDU-Bench，一个面向光刻缺陷理解的多任务多模态基准，将审查流程分解为缺陷分诊、形态识别、粗定位和图像条件原因分析四个任务，并采用任务级指标、诊断读数和LCS评分系统评估模型，实验发现现有MLLM在分诊阶段表现尚可，但在后续环节存在跨语义层级结构化理解不足的瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业光刻审查中，仅判断是否存在缺陷远不能满足工程检测需求，模型还需理解缺陷形态、空间位置及有可见证据支撑的潜在成因，但现有基准未能系统评估这些能力。

Method: 构建LDU-Bench多任务多模态基准，基于真实光刻和集成电路审查图像，将审查流程分解为缺陷分诊、形态识别、粗定位和图像条件原因分析四个独立任务，使用任务级指标、诊断读数和Lithography Closure Score（LCS）进行系统评估。

Result: 实验表明现有MLLM能较可靠地完成缺陷分诊，但该能力不能稳定迁移到下游审查阶段；形态对齐、有效定位和证据到原因的映射是主要瓶颈，且能力断裂并非单一指标波动，而是跨语义层级结构化理解不足的反映。

Conclusion: LDU-Bench为工业MLLM在光刻审查链中的可用性、失败点和能力边界提供了可量化且具诊断性的统一评估平台。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models have demonstrated strong defect recognition capability in industrial anomaly detection. However, in lithography review, merely determining whether an image contains a defect is insufficient for engineering inspection; models must also understand defect morphology, spatial location, and the potential causes supported by visible evidence. To this end, this paper proposes LDU-Bench, a multi-task multimodal benchmark for lithography defect understanding. Constructed from real lithography and integrated-circuit review images, LDU-Bench decomposes the review workflow into four independent tasks: defect triage, morphology recognition, coarse localization, and image-conditioned cause analysis. It systematically evaluates models using task-level metrics, diagnostic readouts, and the Lithography Closure Score (LCS). Experimental results show that although existing MLLMs can perform defect triage relatively reliably, this ability does not stably transfer to downstream review stages. Morphology alignment, effective localization, and evidence-to-cause mapping remain the major bottlenecks. Further diagnostics indicate that this capability break is not a fluctuation of a single metric, but reflects insufficient structured understanding across semantic levels. Overall, LDU-Bench provides a quantifiable and diagnostic unified platform for evaluating the usability, failure points, and capability boundaries of industrial MLLMs in lithography review chains.

</details>

*Ting Yin,Danning Li,Chen Shu,Xiaoxia Yao,Boyu Fu,Yujing Chang,Tianyu Shi,Mengna Feng,Jie Chen,Jing Fu,Xiuli Xiao,Tianlin Li,Mumin Shao,Jiaxin Bi,Wenchuan Zhang,Xiaoyan Wu,Xiao Han,Zhang Zhang,Yuhao Yi,Hong Bu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍了CorePath，一个基于PRISM微调的乳腺专科多模态病理基础模型，利用7901对配对的核心针穿刺活检全切片图像和诊断报告训练而成。在六个CNB队列和两个公开基准上，CorePath在癌症检测、浸润评估和组织学分型方面持续优于PRISM，并显著降低非乳腺幻觉（30.1%降至2.8%）。此外，CorePath-CRG通过置信度门控和风险控制实现了选择性报告发布，达到零非乳腺幻觉。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 乳腺核心针穿刺活检（CNB）对乳腺癌诊断至关重要，但由于组织取样有限、病灶异质性和形态学重叠等问题，亚型区分面临挑战，需要更精准的诊断工具。

Method: 从PRISM基础模型微调得到乳腺专科多模态病理基础模型CorePath，使用两家医疗中心的7901对配对CNB全切片图像和诊断报告进行训练。在此基础上构建CorePath-CRG，结合共形亚型置信度门控与Learn-Then-Test风险控制，实现选择性报告发布、亚型级回退和延迟机制。

Result: 在六个CNB队列中，CorePath的加权AUC达到0.9526-0.9735（五类组织学分型）；在公开基准上取得BCNB浸润癌亚型0.7780、BRACS病变分层0.8178和细粒度分类0.8252的最高加权AUC；非乳腺幻觉从30.1%降至2.8%；CorePath-CRG在发布输出中实现零非乳腺幻觉。

Conclusion: 领域特化的基础模型结合统计风险控制，为准确的乳腺CNB诊断和可靠的报告生成提供了一种有前景的解决方案。

Abstract: Breast core needle biopsy (CNB) is central to breast cancer diagnosis yet remains challenging because limited tissue sampling, lesion heterogeneity, and subtle morphologic overlap can obscure subtype distinctions. We developed CorePath, a breast-specialized multimodal pathology foundation model fine-tuned from PRISM using 7901 paired CNB whole-slide images and diagnostic reports from two centers. Evaluated across six CNB cohorts and two public breast pathology benchmarks without task-specific retraining, CorePath consistently outperformed PRISM across cancer detection, invasion assessment, and histological subtyping. It achieved weighted area under the receiver operating characteristic curves (AUCs) of 0.9526-0.9735 for five-class CNB histological subtyping across private centers. On public benchmarks, CorePath outperformed leading pathology foundation models, achieving the highest weighted AUCs of 0.7780 for BCNB invasive carcinoma subtyping, 0.8178 for BRACS lesion stratification, and 0.8252 for BRACS fine-grained classification. In report generation, CorePath reduced the overall non-breast hallucinations from 30.1% to 2.8%, demonstrating improved domain fidelity after breast-specific adaptation. CorePath-CRG further combined conformal subtype-confidence gating with Learn-Then-Test risk control to enable selective report release, subtype-level fallback, and deferral. CorePath-CRG achieved zero non-breast hallucinations among released outputs and showed the strongest overall performance in pathologist-validated LLM-based Evaluation Scores and quantitative report-generation metrics across most centers. These results demonstrate that domain-specialized foundation models with statistical risk control offer a promising approach for accurate breast CNB diagnosis and reliable report generation.

</details>


### [18] [DiverseDiT++: Quantifying, Analyzing, and Promoting Representation Diversity in Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03082)
*Binglei Li,Mengping Yang,Zhiyu Tan,Xiaomeng Yang,Zhizhong Huang,Junping Zhang,Hao Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了Diffusion Transformers (DiTs)内部表征的动态变化，提出了一个新的度量指标——加权多样性分数(WDS)来量化不同块间表征的差异性，并发现表征多样性是DiTs有效学习的关键因素。基于此发现，作者提出DiverseDiT++框架，通过长残差连接和表征多样性损失来促进多样化的表征学习，在ImageNet数据集上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然DiTs在视觉合成领域取得了显著进展（得益于其出色的可扩展性），且已有工作如REPA通过引入外部预训练编码器来对齐表征，但DiTs内部表征学习的基本机制仍未被社区充分理解。理解这些机制对于进一步优化模型性能至关重要。

Method: 1) 提出加权多样性分数(WDS)指标，用于量化DiTs中不同块间表征的差异；2) 对多种设置下块级表征的演化和影响进行全面研究，揭示表征多样性的重要性；3) 基于研究发现提出DiverseDiT++框架，包含两个核心组件：长残差连接（多样化输入表征）和表征多样性损失（鼓励块学习不同特征）。

Result: WDS与合成质量在多种设置、模型规模和训练阶段均表现出强相关性（Pearson相关系数r=-0.869，与log(FID)相关）。在ImageNet 256×256和512×512上的大量实验表明，DiverseDiT++在不同规模和不同骨干网络上均取得了一致的性能提升和收敛加速。

Conclusion: 表征多样性是DiTs有效学习的关键因素，WDS可作为反映模型性能的指标并指导模型优化。DiverseDiT++通过显式促进多样化的表征学习，验证了该发现的有效性。

Abstract: Recent advances in Diffusion Transformers (DiTs) have enabled remarkable progress in visual synthesis, benefiting from their superior scalability. To facilitate DiTs' capability of capturing meaningful internal representations, recent works such as REPA incorporate external pretrained encoders for representation alignment. However, the underlying mechanisms governing representation learning within DiTs remain poorly understood in the community. To this end, this paper first presents a systematic analysis of the representation dynamics of DiTs via quantifying the diversity of block-wise representations. Specifically, we introduce a novel metric, termed the Weighted Diversity Score (WDS), to measure the representational discrepancies across different blocks. Through extensive investigations on the evolution and influence of internal representations under various settings, we reveal that representation diversity across blocks is a critical factor for effective representation learning in DiTs. More importantly, WDS exhibits a strong correlation with synthesis quality across diverse settings, model scales, and training stages (Pearson's $r=-0.869$ with $\log(\text{FID})$), suggesting its potential as an indicator to reflect model performance and a principled guide for model optimization. Based on this key finding, we propose DiverseDiT++, a novel framework that explicitly promotes diverse representation learning. Concretely, our method incorporates long residual connections to diversify input representations across blocks and a representation diversity loss to encourage blocks to learn distinct features. Extensive experiments on ImageNet $256\times256$ and $512\times512$ demonstrate that our DiverseDiT++ yields consistent performance gains and convergence acceleration when applied to different backbones with various sizes,...

</details>


### [19] [GSTEP: Global Spatio-Temporal Density-Driven Visual Token Pruning for Efficient Video Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03083)
*Mengjie Zhang,Qihui Zhu,Tao Zhang,Shuangwu Chen,Huihuang Qin,Yu Guo,Shenghao Ye,Zijian Wen,Yunpeng Hou,Dong Jin,Xiaobin Tan,Huasen He,Jian Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出GSTEP，一种全局时空密度剪枝框架，将视频建模为连续时空信息流，通过联合时间密度和空间密度进行全局token采样，在保持性能的同时显著降低VideoLLM推理成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VideoLLM推理成本高昂，因为长视频中存在大量冗余的时空视觉token。现有token剪枝方法大多采用分段级局部剪枝，将视频切分为孤立片段并在每个片段内独立选择token，可能导致两个问题：1）对短但语义密集的片段保留不足；2）丢弃了局部看似不显著但在全局视角下关键的token。

Method: GSTEP将视频建模为连续的时空信息流。具体而言：1）构建token级别的时空密度，包括从平滑的中心帧级变化信号获得连续时间密度，以及帧内空间密度；2）通过联合平衡信息密度与覆盖率进行全局token采样。该方法为即插即用框架，可适配多种VideoLLM。

Result: 在多个VideoLLM和公开基准上验证了有效性。在LLaVA-OneVision-7B上，GSTEP剪枝75%的视觉token，保持了原平均性能的100.2%，并实现了1.17倍的端到端加速。

Conclusion: GSTEP在准确率与效率之间取得了良好的平衡，并且能够跨模型架构和评估设置泛化，证明了全局式剪枝相对于局部式剪枝的优越性。

Abstract: Video large language models (VideoLLMs) achieve strong video understanding performance, but their inference remains expensive due to the large number of redundant spatio-temporal visual tokens in long videos. Existing token pruning methods alleviate this cost by reducing redundant tokens, yet most of them rely on segment-level local pruning, where videos are partitioned into isolated segments and tokens are selected independently within each segment. Such designs may under-preserve short but semantically dense segments and discard tokens that appear non-salient locally but remain critical from a global perspective. To address this issue, we propose GSTEP (Global Spatio-Temporal Density Pruning), a plug-and-play pruning framework that models video as a continuous spatio-temporal information flow. GSTEP constructs a token-level spatio-temporal density by combining a continuous temporal density, obtained from a smoothed centered frame-level change signal, with intra-frame spatial density, and then performs global token sampling by jointly balancing information density and coverage. Extensive experiments on multiple VideoLLMs and public benchmarks demonstrate that GSTEP consistently achieves strong accuracy-efficiency trade-offs and generalizes well across model architectures and evaluation settings. On LLaVA-OneVision-7B, GSTEP prunes 75% of visual tokens, preserves up to 100.2% of the original average performance across benchmarks, and achieves a 1.17 end-to-end speedup.

</details>


### [20] [SUV: Future Scene Understanding as Video Generation for End-to-End Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03084)
*Yibo Yuan,Jiacheng Fu,Jiangtong Zhu,Yi Li,Jianhua Han,Meng Tian,Zhuohan Liu,Zhiwei Xiong,Hang Xu,Jianwu Fang,Jianru Xue*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SUV是一个统一的端到端驾驶框架，利用预训练的视频基础模型将未来场景理解建模为视频生成任务，同时预测未来外观、语义、相对深度和实例级动态，并通过联合视频-动作注意力生成车辆轨迹，在多个基准测试中取得领先性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的端到端驾驶方法使用任务特定的头部和输出格式来建模未来场景，扩展性有限。作者希望探究视频生成能否作为共享预测器来解决这一问题。

Method: SUV将未来场景理解转化为视频生成任务，使用预训练的视频基础模型作为共享视频专家，同时建模未来外观、语义、相对深度和实例级动态四种视频流。通过联合视频-动作注意力机制，动作专家关注所有未来流的潜在表示并生成自我车辆轨迹。

Result: SUV仅使用单个前置摄像头且无需候选轨迹选择，在NAVSIM-v2两个划分上均超越多种最新方法，navtest上达到91.0 EPDMS，navhard上达到36.9；在长尾WOD-E2E基准上取得竞争力RFS 7.94。受控消融实验表明结构化未来监督和直接未来流访问能提升轨迹规划得分。

Conclusion: SUV证明了视频生成可以作为端到端驾驶中未来场景理解的统一预测范式，无需任务特定的视觉预测头部即可同时建模多种未来流并提升轨迹规划性能。

Abstract: End-to-end driving requires a coherent understanding of future scenes, yet existing methods model these scenes using task-specific heads and output formats, with limited scalability. Can video generation instead provide a shared predictor? We introduce SUV, a unified end-to-end driving framework that casts future Scene Understanding as Video generation using a pretrained video foundation model. SUV models future appearance, semantics, relative depth, and instance-level dynamics as video streams with a shared video expert, without stream-specific visual prediction heads. Through joint video-action attention, the action expert attends to the latent representations of all future streams and generates the ego trajectory. Experiments show that SUV directly predicts all four future streams, while controlled ablations show that structured future supervision and direct future-stream access yield higher trajectory planning scores. With only a single front camera and no candidate-trajectory selection, SUV outperforms a broad set of recent state-of-the-art methods on both NAVSIM-v2 splits, achieving 91.0 EPDMS on navtest and 36.9 on navhard. On the long-tail WOD-E2E benchmark, SUV achieves a competitive RFS of 7.94.

</details>


### [21] [Channel-wise Dynamic Knowledge Distillation via Adaptive Sample Generation for Action Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03100)
*Ping Li,Chenhao Ping,Jie Song,Mingli Song*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种自适应的样本感知通道动态知识蒸馏（ASCD KD）方法，通过两阶段流程解决动作识别模型压缩中的两个关键问题：固定输入样本导致的特征对齐次优，以及统一蒸馏强度无法捕捉不同通道在不同训练时期的重要性差异。方法在多个视频和图像基准上取得了最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有知识蒸馏方法在动作识别模型压缩中面临两个局限：1）依赖固定输入样本导致教师与学生模型间的特征对齐不是最优；2）对所有通道施加统一的蒸馏强度，未能考虑不同通道在捕获不同知识（如运动速度或幅度）时的重要性差异。

Method: 提出ASCD KD方法，包含两个阶段：第一阶段使用自适应样本生成模块，通过最小化由通道质心频率差异加权的特征损失来获取样本梯度，并利用这些梯度创建更新样本，同时使用高斯掩码作用于频率特征以保留关键运动相关细节；第二阶段采用通道动态蒸馏模块，在生成样本上训练学生模型，由样本梯度和特征频率引导。为提升效率，样本周期性更新而非每轮更新。

Result: 在三个视频基准（UCF101、Kinetics-400、Something-Something-v2）和两个图像数据集（CIFAR-100、ImageNet）上的大量实验证明了该方法达到了最先进的性能。

Conclusion: ASCD KD通过自适应样本生成和通道级动态蒸馏，有效解决了现有KD方法在动作识别中的局限性，在多个数据集上验证了其优越性。

Abstract: Knowledge Distillation (KD) offers a promising yet underexplored path for compressing large action recognition models. However, existing KD methods suffer from two key limitations: 1) reliance on fixed input samples leads to suboptimal feature alignment between the frozen teacher (larger model) and the learnable student (smaller model), and 2) applying a uniform distillation strength for all channels fails to account for their varying importance in capturing distinct knowledge (e.g., motion tempo or magnitude) across training epochs. This motivates us to develop an Adaptive Sample-aware Channel-wise Dynamic (ASCD) KD approach, which operates in two stages. First, we use an adaptive sample generation module to create updated samples by incorporating semantics from sample gradients, which are derived by minimizing a feature loss weighted by channel centroid frequency differences at each layer. Meanwhile, crucial motion-related details are preserved by applying a Gaussian mask to frequency features. Second, we employ a channel-wise dynamic distillation module to train student on these generated samples, guided by sample gradients and feature frequencies. For efficiency, samples are updated periodically rather than per epoch. Extensive experiments on three video benchmarks (UCF101, Kinetics-400, Something-Something-v2) and two image datasets (CIFAR-100, ImageNet) demonstrate the state-of-the-art performance of our method. Code is available at https://github.com/mlvccn/ASCD_KD_Action.

</details>


### [22] [Double Down on Defense: Strengthening Deep Perceptual Hashes against Evasion Attacks without Retraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03101)
*Bangjie Sun,Nayoung Kim,Mun Choon Chan,Jun Han*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DualShield是一个即插即用的防御方法，通过匹配时的随机平滑和发布时的参考图像加固，在不重新训练底层模型的情况下提升深度感知哈希的鲁棒性，抵御对抗性扰动导致的近重复图像匹配绕过攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度感知哈希被广泛应用于信任与安全、版权保护等场景，但对抗性扰动可导致近重复图像绕过匹配，而现有方法通常需要昂贵的重新训练来提升鲁棒性。

Method: DualShield结合两种机制：(1) 匹配时的随机平滑，对扰动后的参考-查询对进行决策聚合；(2) 发布时的加固，在参考图像发布前添加优化的不可感知扰动，从而提供认证和经验鲁棒性。

Result: DualShield实现了约0.3的认证l2半径，保证该半径内的查询扰动无法绕过匹配；在8种深度感知哈希和3个数据集上，显著降低自适应白盒、黑盒及图像变换攻击的成功率，同时保持低碰撞率。

Conclusion: 深度感知哈希可以通过改进匹配流程和在发布前加固参考图像来增强鲁棒性，无需昂贵且耗时的模型重新训练，证明了即插即用防御方案的可行性和有效性。

Abstract: Near-duplicate image matching is crucial for trust and safety, provenance verification, copyright enforcement, and large-scale visual search. Modern platforms increasingly rely on deep perceptual hashes, which map visually similar images to nearby representations despite common image transformations. However, adversarial perturbations can cause near-duplicates to evade matching. We present DualShield, a plug-in defense that improves the robustness of existing deep perceptual hashes without retraining or modifying their underlying models. DualShield combines matching-time randomized smoothing, which aggregates decisions over perturbed reference-query pairs, with publication-time hardening, which adds an optimized imperceptible perturbation to each reference image before publication. Together, these mechanisms provide certified and empirical robustness. DualShield achieves a certified $\ell_2$ radius of approximately 0.3, guaranteeing that query perturbations within this radius cannot evade matching. We further evaluate it against adaptive white-box, black-box, and image-transformation attacks. Across eight deep perceptual hashes and three datasets, DualShield substantially reduces attack success rates while preserving low collision rates. These results show that deep perceptual hashes can be strengthened without costly retraining by improving the matching procedure and hardening reference images before publication.

</details>


### [23] [FaithIR: Rethinking Infrared Image Super-Resolution from Perceptual Sharpness to Task Relevant Fidelity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03106)
*Axi Niu,Zhenguo Wu,Kang Zhang,Qingsen Yan,Jinqiu Sun,Yanning Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出FaithIR框架，用于可靠的红外图像超分辨率重建，通过补丁级条件分支捕获全局热/结构信息，像素级恢复分支在结构引导下进行密集局部重建，显著提升下游感知任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有红外超分辨率方法常产生伪纹理、过度锐化边缘和虚假高频细节，扭曲真实热结构和语义信息，影响下游机器感知任务的可靠性。

Method: FaithIR包含两个分支：补丁级条件分支负责捕获全局热学和结构信息；像素级恢复分支在结构引导下执行密集局部重建，整个过程直接在像素域进行以保留红外特有结构和任务相关信息。

Result: 在FLIR-IISR、M3FD和FMB数据集上的大量实验表明，该方法在重建保真度、跨数据集泛化能力以及目标检测和语义分割任务中均表现优异。

Conclusion: 保留忠实的红外结构比单纯追求感知锐度对可靠机器感知更为重要。

Abstract: Infrared image super-resolution (IISR) is important for downstream tasks such as object detection and semantic segmentation. Existing IISR methods often produce artificial textures, over-sharpened edges, and spurious high-frequency details that distort authentic thermal structures and semantic information. To address this issue, we propose FaithIR, a faithful infrared super-resolution framework for reliable machine perception. FaithIR consists of a patch-level conditioning branch that captures global thermal and structural information and a pixel-level restoration branch that performs dense local reconstruction under structural guidance. The entire restoration process is performed directly in the pixel domain to preserve infrared-specific structures and task-relevant information. Extensive experiments on FLIR-IISR, M3FD, and FMB demonstrate strong reconstruction fidelity, cross-dataset generalization, and superior performance in object detection and semantic segmentation. These results show that demonstrate that preserving faithful infrared structure preservations is more important for reliable machine perception than merely pursuing perceptual sharpness alone.

</details>


### [24] [A Unified Resolution-Conditioned Framework for Orthogonal Line-Scanning Image Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03107)
*Yiming Gong,Kai Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于Rank增强线性注意力（RELA）的统一分辨率条件融合框架，通过特征级线性调制（FiLM）使单一模型能够适应不同狭缝宽度下的激光线扫描显微成像，实现近各向同性分辨率的恢复，并跨配置取得34-40 dB的PSNR效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激光线扫描显微镜能够实现快速体成像，但产生各向异性的横向分辨率。正交线扫描可提供互补的方向信息以恢复近各向同性分辨率，然而现有的深度学习方法需要针对每种光学配置单独训练模型，缺乏跨配置的泛化能力。

Method: 提出统一的分辨率条件融合框架：(1) 基于Rank增强线性注意力（RELA）构建网络；(2) 使用特征级线性调制（FiLM）根据分辨能力比率连续调节网络；(3) 引入自适应RELA，用比率条件的多尺度深度卷积替代固定核秩增强，并采用可学习的注意力温度来调节选择性；(4) 利用基于物理的可分离点扩散函数模型生成多狭缝配置的训练数据。

Result: 模型在多种配置下达到34-40 dB PSNR；无条件多狭缝训练仅达24.3 dB；针对特定狭缝的专业模型在训练配置外损失4-9 dB。模型对未见过的中间配置也能平滑泛化，无插值伪影。消融实验表明FiLM解决了配置歧义，全局线性注意力捕获长距离方向对应关系，自适应温度在近各向同性区域额外提升2 dB。

Conclusion: 该框架通过分辨率条件化设计实现了单一模型跨多种光学配置的自适应融合，有效恢复了近各向同性分辨率，并在不同狭缝宽度下均表现出优异的泛化性能与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Laser line-scanning microscopy enables fast volumetric imaging but produces anisotropic lateral resolution. Orthogonal line scans provide complementary directional information that can recover near-isotropic resolution, yet existing deep-learning methods require a separate model for each optical configuration. We present a unified, resolution-conditioned fusion framework based on Rank Enhanced Linear Attention (RELA). Feature-wise Linear Modulation (FiLM) conditions the network continuously on the resolving-power ratio, enabling one model to adapt across slit widths. We further introduce Adaptive RELA, which replaces fixed-kernel rank enhancement with ratio-conditioned multi-scale depthwise convolutions and uses a learnable attention temperature to adjust selectivity with degradation severity. Training data spanning multiple slit configurations are generated using a physics-grounded separable point-spread-function model verified against measured optical data at 48.3 dB accuracy. The resulting model achieves 34-40 dB PSNR across configurations, whereas unconditioned multi-slit training collapses to 24.3 dB and per-slit specialists lose 4-9 dB outside their training setting. It also generalizes smoothly to unseen intermediate configurations without interpolation artifacts. Ablations show that FiLM resolves configuration ambiguity, global linear attention captures long-range directional correspondences, and adaptive temperature yields an additional 2 dB in the challenging near-isotropic regime, where complementary signals are weak.

</details>


### [25] [Multimodal Plant Root Phenotyping with Integration of 3D Skeleton Extraction and Language Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03109)
*Jiakai Lin,Zijun Li,Guoyu Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个多模态机器人AI框架，将3D骨架提取与语言引导推理相结合，用于可解释且数据高效的植物根系表型分析。通过无监督骨架提取网络生成高保真结构表征，并利用Evidence-First语言建模框架微调GPT作为交互式分析聊天机器人，实现了跨12种植物物种的稳健可解释推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 植物根系表型分析对于理解地下结构、优化作物管理和提升农业可持续性至关重要，但现有的根系分析方法缺乏可解释性和数据效率，难以将定量形态分析与语义理解有效结合。

Method: 方法包含两个核心部分：(1) 基于加权拉普拉斯收缩（W-LBC）的无监督骨架提取网络，从密集点云生成高保真结构表征，并计算根数、长度、分支角度和密度等定量形态描述符；(2) Evidence-First语言建模框架，通过自动生成的指令-响应对微调GPT，使模型在自然语言推理之前提供可测量的证据，将数值结构与语义意义关联。

Result: 实验表明该结构引导框架在12种具有不同根系构型的植物物种上实现了稳健且可解释的推理，验证了框架的通用性和有效性。

Conclusion: 通过将无监督3D几何感知与大规模语言理解相结合，该方法弥合了定量分析与语义解释之间的鸿沟，为可解释的机器人植物根系表型分析建立了统一范式。

Abstract: Plant root phenotyping is fundamental to understanding below-ground structures, optimizing crop management, and improving agricultural sustainability. This paper presents a multimodal robotic AI framework that integrates 3D skeleton extraction with language-guided reasoning for interpretable and data-efficient root analysis. We develop an unsupervised skeleton extraction network based on Weighted Laplacian Contraction (W-LBC) to generate high-fidelity structural representations from dense point clouds captured by robotic 3D sensing platforms. Quantitative morphological descriptors, including root count, length, branching angle, and density, are computed from the reconstructed skeleton graph to capture geometric and topological characteristics. Building on these features, we introduce an Evidence-First language modeling framework that fine-tunes GPT as an interactive analytical chatbot using automatically generated instruction--response pairs. Each training sample provides measurable evidence before natural-language reasoning, enabling the model to ground interpretation in quantitative morphology. Through supervised fine-tuning, GPT associates numerical structure with semantic meaning, producing biologically consistent explanations of growth patterns and adaptive traits. Experiments show that the structure-guided framework achieves robust, interpretable reasoning across 12 plant species with diverse root architectures. By integrating unsupervised 3D geometric perception with large-scale language understanding, our approach bridges quantitative analysis and semantic interpretation, establishing a unified paradigm for explainable robotic plant root phenotyping.

</details>


### [26] [Adaptive Two-Stage Visual Token Pruning for Efficient Inference in Video-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03112)
*Paribesh Regmi,Qingshuang Chen,Chi Zhang,Heba Aly,Yelin Kim,Hongda Mao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向视频处理的两阶段自适应token剪枝策略：第一阶段剪除冗余帧，第二阶段根据视频内容自适应地确定token剪枝比例，通过分析token嵌入的相关性结构来量化冗余度。该方法无需额外训练，在10% token保留率下将视频字幕任务准确率提升7%，同时减少95%的计算量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言模型在图像和视频理解中表现出色，但处理每张图像数千个token导致推理延迟高，限制了在边缘设备和实时监控中的部署。现有token缩减方法主要针对单张图像设计，未考虑视频序列中的时间冗余和帧间冗余；且这些方法使用固定的统一剪枝比例，无法适应不同视频之间冗余度的差异。

Method: 提出两阶段自适应token剪枝策略：第一阶段剪除冗余的视频帧；第二阶段在保留帧内进行token级别剪枝，剪枝比例基于视频内容自适应确定。通过分析token嵌入的相关性结构来量化内容冗余度，从而决定剪枝比例。该方法完全事后(post-hoc)应用，无需额外训练或微调。

Result: 在视频字幕基准上，10% token保留率下准确率提升7%，计算量(TFLOPs)减少95%。

Conclusion: 该两阶段自适应剪枝方法有效解决了视频场景下token冗余问题，通过内容自适应的剪枝策略在保持甚至提升准确率的同时大幅降低计算开销，且无需训练，具有良好的实用性和部署前景。

Abstract: Vision-language models excel at image and video understanding but suffer from high inference latency due to the need to process thousands of tokens per image, limiting their deployment on resource-constrained edge devices and in real-time surveillance applications. This challenge is further amplified in video processing, where multiple frames must be analyzed simultaneously. Existing token reduction techniques are largely developed for single-image inputs and therefore fail to account for the temporal and inter-frame redundancies present in video sequences. In addition, these methods generally rely on a fixed, uniform pruning ratio applied across all inputs, which is suboptimal because the degree of redundancy can vary significantly between different videos, necessitating content-dependent pruning levels to preserve critical information. To address these limitations, we propose a two-stage adaptive token pruning strategy specifically designed for video processing. In the first stage, we prune out the redundant frames, and in the second stage, token-level pruning is applied within the retained frames. Crucially, the pruning ratio in the second stage is determined adaptively based on the content of each video. This is achieved by analyzing the correlation structure of token embeddings to quantify redundancy, which is used to determine the ratio. Importantly, our method is entirely post-hoc and requires no additional training or fine-tuning, while achieving strong empirical gains; notably, it improves accuracy by +7\% on a video captioning benchmark at 10\% token retention, while reducing computation TFLOPs by 95\%.

</details>


### [27] [Non-Destructive Quantification of Urea Adulteration in Bovine Milk Using Transmittance Multispectral Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03113)
*Sharukshan Niranjan,Iresha Ranaweera,Tharindu Chandrarathne,Kalana Dissanayaka,Roshan Godaliyadda,Vijitha Herath,Parakrama Ekanayake,Janak Vidanarachchi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种基于多光谱成像（MSI）的回归框架，用于在受控密度条件下定量检测牛奶中的尿素掺假。该系统使用12个光谱波段（365-940 nm）采集图像，结合多元线性回归和前馈神经网络进行建模，验证R²分别达到0.9599和0.9773，展示了快速、无损、低成本筛查牛奶质量的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 牛奶中掺入尿素是严重的食品安全和健康问题，现有检测方法（实验室分析法和光谱技术）多依赖专用仪器、样品前处理、化学试剂或实验室操作，难以满足快速、低成本、常规筛查的需求，因此亟需开发一种更实用的定量检测方法。

Method: 采用自建的多光谱成像系统，在365-940 nm范围内12个离散光谱波段采集牛奶样品图像；样品通过在新鲜牛奶中控制添加尿素并用蒸馏水调节密度（比重1.032，20°C）制备；使用多元线性回归（MLR）建立初始映射，并以前馈神经网络（FFNN）进一步提升预测性能。

Result: MLR模型的验证R²为0.9599，FFNN模型将验证R²提升至0.9773，均展现出高预测精度，证明透射式多光谱成像在受控密度条件下可准确实现尿素定量检测。

Conclusion: 该研究证明透射式多光谱成像结合回归建模是可行、准确且非破坏性的尿素定量方法，在受控密度平衡条件下表现优异，有望作为牛奶质量快速筛查的有效工具。

Abstract: Adulteration of bovine milk using urea remains a major food quality and health concern, motivating the development of rapid and quantitative screening tools. Conventional approaches, including laboratory-based analytical methods and spectroscopic techniques, have been used for urea detection; however, many remain less suitable for rapid, low-cost routine screening due to requirements such as specialized instrumentation, sample preparation, chemical reagents, or laboratory operation. This study introduces a pragmatic, cost-effective, accurate, and laboratory-validated MSI-based method for quantitative urea estimation under controlled density conditions using a multispectral-imaging-based regression framework. An in-house-built multispectral imaging system operating in twelve discrete spectral bands (365--940~nm) was used to acquire multispectral images of milk samples prepared with controlled urea addition and water for density balancing. Fresh milk was obtained on the day of image acquisition, and the specific gravity of the milk was verified to be 1.032 at 20°C using a hydrometer. Multiple linear regression provided an initial mapping with a high validation $R^2$ of 0.9599, while a feed-forward neural network further improved predictive performance with a validation $R^2$ of 0.9773. These results demonstrate the feasibility of transmittance multispectral imaging for accurate, non-destructive urea quantification under controlled density-balanced conditions, supporting its potential as a rapid screening approach for milk-quality assessment.

</details>


### [28] [SeCo-SBIR: Semantically Consistent Prompt Learning for Zero-Shot Sketch-Based Image Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03120)
*Long Hoang Dang,Tuan Nguyen Huu,Nguyen Minh Hieu,Tu Minh Phuong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SeCo-SBIR，一种用于零样本草图画检索（ZS-SBIR）的语义一致提示学习框架，通过文本引导的多模态提示策略和基于扰动的一致性约束，在适应草图-照片域差距的同时保持CLIP的零样本泛化能力，在三个标准基准上取得最先进结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在ZS-SBIR中，基于CLIP的提示学习面临一个根本矛盾：模型需要通过任务特定适应来弥合草图与照片之间的域差距，但额外的灵活性可能导致对已见训练类别的过拟合，从而损害CLIP的零样本泛化能力。

Method: 1) 文本引导的多模态提示策略：通过CLIP文本编码器路由可学习的提示向量，并利用可学习的耦合函数将中间表示逐层注入视觉编码器，从而将文本编码器的可迁移语义知识注入视觉通路。2) 基于扰动的一致性约束：采用非对称InfoNCE目标函数，将增强输入送入冻结的CLIP参考分支，清洁输入送入可训练分支，将学习表示锚定到CLIP的通用特征空间。3) 结合轻量适配器和多目标损失（三元组、NT-Xent和分类损失）。

Result: 在三个标准ZS-SBIR基准上，涵盖类别级、广义级和跨数据集设置，SeCo-SBIR均取得了最先进的结果。

Conclusion: SeCo-SBIR通过双管齐下的策略（注入可迁移语义知识+一致性约束锚定泛化空间），成功化解了任务适应与零样本泛化之间的张力，验证了语义一致的提示学习框架在ZS-SBIR任务上的有效性。

Abstract: Adapting CLIP for zero-shot sketch-based image retrieval (ZS-SBIR) via prompt learning faces a fundamental tension: the model must bridge the sketch-photo domain gap through task-specific adaptation, yet the added flexibility risks overfitting to seen training categories and eroding CLIP's zero-shot generalization. We present SeCo-SBIR, a semantically consistent prompt learning framework that resolves this tension from both sides. First, a text-guided multi-modal prompting strategy routes learnable prompt vectors through CLIP's text encoder and projects the resulting intermediate representations into the visual encoder at every layer via learnable coupling functions. Because the text encoder has already learned robust, abstract category-level semantics from large-scale language supervision, this mechanism injects transferable semantic knowledge directly into the visual pathway - adapting the model to the sketch-photo domain while inherently favoring generalization to unseen classes. Second, a perturbation-based consistency constraint addresses the residual overfitting risk from the learnable coupling functions by aligning the adapted model with a frozen CLIP reference branch using an asymmetric InfoNCE objective - augmented inputs feed the frozen branch while clean inputs feed the trainable branch - anchoring the learned representations to CLIP's generalizable feature space. Together with lightweight adapters and a multi-objective loss combining triplet, NT-Xent, and classification terms, SeCo-SBIR achieves state-of-the-art results on all three standard ZS-SBIR benchmarks across categorical, generalized, and across-dataset settings.

</details>


### [29] [Rectify Then Diffuse: Disentangling Concepts Before Denoising Trajectory Unfolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03135)
*Ning Zhu,An Chen,Mengfei Zhao,Juntao Xu,Jingze Liang,Boyuan Gu,Liang-Jian Deng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Rectify-then-Diffuse (RTD)，一种无需训练的文本到图像扩散模型组合生成框架。RTD通过Soft-Overlap Disentanglement和Isotropic Gradient Rectification在去噪前一次性修正初始潜在空间的注意力分配，将组合生成视为边界条件问题，避免了传统方法对迭代轨迹的反复控制。在AE-Bench对象对子集上，RTD相比CO3将BLIP-VQA提升45.8%，ImageReward提升19.6%，且运行速度快2.3倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本到图像扩散模型在生成单个概念时表现良好，但在处理多个概念时经常出现概念遗漏或错误合并的问题。作者追踪到这些失败源于去噪开始前的早期协调瓶颈：提示条件注意力可能将不同概念分配到高度重叠的空间区域，导致后续去噪过程中这些概念的注意力持续耦合。

Method: RTD包含两个核心组件：(1) Soft-Overlap Disentanglement (SOD)：将标准化的概念图之间的重叠转化为可微且不依赖布局的分离目标；(2) Isotropic Gradient Rectification (IGR)：对SOD梯度进行归一化，并施加跨提示和初始化具有一致尺度的有界潜在位移。整体流程是在标准去噪前对初始分配进行一次修正，将组合生成视为边界条件问题。

Result: 在AE-Bench对象对子集上，RTD相比CO3将BLIP-VQA提升45.8%，ImageReward提升19.6%，同时运行速度快2.3倍。RTD实现了最先进的组合保真度和稳健的性能提升。

Conclusion: RTD通过将组合生成重新框定为边界条件问题，并在去噪前一次性修正初始潜在空间分配，有效地解决了多概念组合生成中的概念遗漏和错误合并问题。该方法无需训练，性能优越且计算效率高。

Abstract: Text-to-image diffusion models can generate individual concepts well, but they often omit or merge concepts incorrectly with multiple concepts. We trace these failures to an early coordination bottleneck: before denoising begins, prompt-conditioned attention may allocate different concepts to strongly overlapping spatial support, which can keep their attention coupled as denoising proceeds. This observation motivates treating compositional generation as a boundary-condition problem rather than repeatedly controlling the evolving trajectory. To this end, we propose Rectify-then-Diffuse (RTD), a training-free framework that rectifies the initial allocation once before standard denoising. Firstly, we propose Soft-Overlap Disentanglement (SOD), which converts normalized overlap between pilot concept maps into a differentiable and layout-agnostic separation objective. Secondly, we introduce Isotropic Gradient Rectification (IGR), which normalizes the SOD gradient and applies a bounded latent displacement with a consistent scale across prompts and initializations. Extensive experiments show that RTD achieves state-of-the-art compositional fidelity and robust gains. On the AE-Bench object pair subset, RTD improves BLIP-VQA by 45.8% and ImageReward by 19.6% over CO3 while running 2.3$\times$ faster. Code will be released at https://github.com/Z-yiwei/rectify-then-diffuse

</details>


### [30] [Frozen High-Resolution Inference for Cross-City Object Detection: An AI City Challenge 2026 Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03136)
*Jaeuk Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文探索了在气隙Training-as-a-Service平台中，单一RF-DETR-Large检测器在跨城市目标检测任务上的冻结推理配置。研究发现，在1120×1120分辨率下冻结推理（不更新任何参数）相比704×704训练时的配置取得了最高的聚合AP提升（0.3272→0.3654，+0.0382），且在小目标上有最大相对增益、中目标上有最大绝对增益。作者同时揭示了一个重要警示：在仅有聚合跨城市反馈条件下，域内验证AP上升（0.767→0.789）并不代表模型在目标城市的表现更好，域内验证是不可靠的模型选择信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨城市目标检测面临目标城市无标注数据的挑战，而AI City Challenge 2026 Track 6提供了独特的限制条件：平台为气隙环境，服务器仅返回源城市与目标城市混合图像的聚合COCO风格AP，无法获得逐图或逐类的细粒度反馈。在此受限反馈条件下，作者希望系统性地审计和理解不同配置（如输入分辨率、训练方式、归一化策略）对聚合性能的影响，并探讨在这种未知混合分布下可靠的模型选择信号是否存在。

Method: 作者在气隙TaaS平台上对单一RF-DETR-Large检测器进行了系统性的配置审计。核心方法包括：（1）冻结推理测试：在1120×1120分辨率下使用704×704训练的checkpoint进行推理，不更新任何参数，对比原始分辨率配置；（2）热启动微调：在1120px分辨率下进行fine-tuning，并对比其域内验证AP与聚合AP的变化；（3）消融实验：测试Gray-world归一化的影响，以及矩形输入配置的效果（后在审计中发现该矩形运行意外使用了竖屏方向）；（4）严格的实验规范：每个配置仅提交一次，最佳配置在隐藏服务器上选定，所有配置根据置信度阈值差异进行分级处理（0.01 vs 0.05），并发布了可验证的审计证据。

Result: 冻结1120×1120推理取得了最高聚合AP（0.3272→0.3654，+0.0382），是2.53倍输入像素所带来的增益，其中小目标相对增益最大、中目标绝对增益最大。热启动1120px微调达到0.3470，但低于冻结推理，且其域内验证AP上升而聚合AP低于冻结方案，表明域内验证不可靠。Gray-world归一化对冻结1120结果无显著影响。矩形运行因审计发现使用了非预期的竖屏方向而被标记为无效。

Conclusion: 在仅有聚合AP反馈的跨城市检测场景中，提高冻结推理分辨率是一种简单且有效的性能提升手段，甚至优于微调。更重要的是，本研究揭示了域内验证AP与聚合跨城市AP之间的脱节现象，警告研究者不应以域内验证作为模型选择的唯一依据。所有结果均为探索性审计发现，局限于混合数据分布，不构成目标城市特定改进的证据。作者通过公开全部平台命令、配置快照和证据边界，为同类受限反馈场景下的研究建立了可重复、可审计的实验范式。

Abstract: Cross-city object detection requires a detector trained in one city to generalize to an unlabeled target city. In AI City Challenge 2026 Track 6, we analyze archived configurations of a single RF-DETR-Large detector inside an air-gapped Training-as-a-Service platform whose server returns only an aggregate COCO-style AP over a hidden mixture of source- and target-city images. Frozen 1120 x 1120 inference of a checkpoint trained at 704 x 704 achieved the highest aggregate AP among the evaluated configurations (0.3272 -> 0.3654, +0.0382) without any parameter update, with the largest relative gain on small objects and the largest absolute gain on medium objects, at 2.53x the input pixels. A warm-start 1120px fine-tuning recipe reached 0.3470 while its in-domain validation AP rose (0.767 -> 0.789), a caution that in-domain validation is an unreliable model-selection signal under aggregate-only cross-city feedback. Because that run's evaluation used a higher confidence threshold than the inference-only runs (0.05 vs. 0.01), we treat its score as a descriptive archived outcome rather than a controlled verdict on fine-tuning. Gray-world normalization did not meaningfully change the frozen-1120 result, and a rectangular run was found by audit to have used an unintended portrait orientation. We release verbatim platform commands, configuration snapshots, and an explicit evidence boundary for every claim. Each configuration was submitted once and the best was selected on the hidden server, so these are exploratory, audited findings about the aggregate mixture; they do not establish target-city-specific improvement.

</details>


### [31] [From Routes to Steps: Separating Semantic Progress from Local Execution in Vision-and-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03143)
*Xiangyun Huang,Xiangchen Wang,Runfeng Lin,Yihao Xu,Kangyu Huang,Jiang Hengchen,Xiwang Dong,Lin Jiarong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Route2Step是一种将语义进度跟踪与动作生成解耦的框架，通过显式的步骤级接口解决VLN中进度跟踪错误与执行错误难以区分的歧义问题。在R2R-CE上，SR从48.1%提升至55.3%，SPL从43.3%提升至48.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的VLM导航器仅通过下一步动作预测来监督能力，导致进度跟踪错误和动作执行错误难以区分。当代理偏离路线时，纠正动作标签无法指示是选择了错误的子指令还是未能正确执行当前指令，代理可能在错误的进度状态下持续做决策。

Method: 提出Route2Step框架，包含两个模块：(1) Instruction Analysis Module (M_IA) 从全局指令和视觉历史中预测进度状态；(2) Action Generation Module (M_AG) 基于预测状态和最近观察生成局部动作块。通过E-SPA步骤对齐程序，在没有手动时间标签的情况下将子指令与路线级演示的对应部分关联起来，实现对错误进度估计的状态监督，同时对在正确活动子指令下反复失败的滚动组保留直接动作监督。

Result: 在R2R-CE基准上，Route2Step将成功率从48.1%提升至55.3%，SPL从43.3%提升至48.2%，仅使用190K状态级纠正样本和11.5K直接动作监督状态。在真实世界室内和室外环境的实验也验证了其实用性。

Conclusion: 通过解耦进度跟踪与动作生成并提供显式的步骤级接口，Route2Step有效解决了VLN中进度跟踪歧义问题，在模拟和真实环境中均取得了显著性能提升。

Abstract: Vision-and-Language Navigation (VLN) requires an agent to follow a route-level instruction by executing its constituent steps from egocentric visual observations. Existing VLM-based navigators typically supervise both capabilities through next-action prediction alone, making progress-tracking errors difficult to distinguish from execution errors. When an agent deviates from the route, a corrective action label may recover the next movement but does not indicate whether the agent selected the wrong sub-instruction or failed to execute the correct one. Consequently, the agent may continue making decisions from an erroneous progress state. To resolve this ambiguity, we propose \textbf{Route2Step}, a framework that decouples semantic progress tracking from action generation through an explicit step-level interface. The Instruction Analysis Module ($\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{IA}}$) predicts this state from the global instruction and visual history. Conditioned on the predicted state and recent observations, the Action Generation Module ($\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{AG}}$) generates local action chunks. To supervise the progress state without manual temporal labels, E-SPA, a step-alignment procedure, associates sub-instructions with their corresponding portions of route-level demonstrations. These alignments enable state supervision for incorrect progress estimates, while direct action supervision is reserved for rollout groups that repeatedly fail under the correct active sub-instruction. On R2R-CE, Route2Step improves SR from 48.1\% to 55.3\% and SPL from 43.3\% to 48.2\%, using 190K state-level corrective samples while requiring only 11.5K directly action-supervised states. Experiments in real-world indoor and outdoor environments further demonstrate the practical applicability of Route2Step. The project page is: https://sisyphus-hxy.github.io/Route2Step/.

</details>


### [32] [CROSS: Cascaded Distillation and Dual-Constraint Grounding for Remote Sensing Referring Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03147)
*Tingzhang Luo,Ruizhong Liu,Yichao Liu,Cheng Fan,Yu Liu,Jianyuan Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 这篇论文提出CROSS框架，针对遥感图像指代分割（RRSIS）中两个核心问题——架构弱耦合和目标中心语义偏差，通过语言引导级联蒸馏（LGCD）与视角-空间对比学习（PSCL）两个创新模块，实现SOTA性能并提升在空间描述扰动下的定位鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RRSIS方法虽借助VLM和SAM取得进展，但过度依赖预训练能力，存在两大未充分解决的局限：(1)架构弱耦合——单向信息流使模型依赖粗糙的VLM提示，浪费SAM的像素级结构指导，导致定位漂移；(2)目标中心语义偏差——模型过度关注主导目标语义，对RRSIS至关重要的空间推理能力不敏感。

Method: 提出紧密集成范式CROSS，包含两个核心模块：1) 语言引导级联蒸馏（LGCD），将SAM的几何亲和度作为软正则化器蒸馏进VLM中间层，注入稠密结构先验以细化定位；2) 视角-空间对比学习（PSCL），通过挖掘掩膜过滤的欺骗性干扰项和空间-语言反事实作为硬负样本，施加跨锚定约束，打破语义捷径以实现真正的逻辑一致性。

Result: 在多个RRSIS基准数据集上，CROSS取得SOTA性能，并且在严重空间描述扰动下仍能保持精确定位。

Conclusion: CROSS作为一种鲁棒的新范式，有效解决了RRSIS中架构耦合不足和语义偏差两大问题，为遥感图像指代分割提供了更可靠的方案。

Abstract: Referring Remote Sensing Image Segmentation (RRSIS) has achieved significant progress through the integration of VLMs and the Segment Anything Model (SAM). However, this progress largely relies on strong pre-trained capabilities, while leaving two fundamental limitations insufficiently addressed: (1) Architectural Weak-Coupling, where the unidirectional flow forces reliance on coarse VLM prompts and wastes SAM's pixel-level structural guidance, causing localization drift; and (2) Object-Centric Semantic Bias, where models overemphasize dominant object semantics while remaining insensitive to spatial reasoning crucial for RRSIS. Motivated by these observations, we propose CROSS, a tightly integrated paradigm for RRSIS. First, we introduce Linguistic-Guided Cascaded Distillation (LGCD) to bridge the architectural gap, which distills SAM's geometric affinities as soft regularizers into VLM intermediate layers, injecting dense structural priors to refine localization. Second, Perspective-Spatial Contrastive Learning (PSCL) imposes cross-anchored constraints by mining mask-filtered deceptive distractors and spatial-linguistic counterfactuals as hard negatives, explicitly shattering semantic shortcuts to enforce genuine logical consistency. Extensive experiments on RRSIS benchmarks demonstrate that CROSS achieves state-of-the-art performance and maintains precise localization even under severe spatial description perturbations, standing as a robust new paradigm for RRSIS.

</details>


### [33] [Surface Keypoint Representation for Multi-Object and Articulated Human-Object Interaction Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03158)
*Xiaogang Peng,Zeyu Han,Zichong Meng,Yiming Xie,Jihua Zhu,Gang Hua,Huaizu Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于表面关键点轨迹的物体运动表示方法，用于生成包含多物体和关节物体的全身人-物交互运动，通过三阶段分解（物体运动生成、接触距离场预测、接触引导的全身运动合成）在多个数据集上取得了优于或媲美现有方法的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的人-物交互（HOI）生成方法大多假设与单一刚体物体交互，难以推广到可变数量物体或具有多种关节机制的关节物体场景。这限制了模型在真实世界复杂交互任务中的适用性。

Method: 提出表面关键点轨迹作为物体运动表示，对每个刚性组件（独立物体或关节装配的一部分）跟踪少量非共线表面点；引入时空接触距离场来建模身体各区域何时何地与各物体接触；将HOI生成分解为三个阶段：从文本或路径点生成物体运动、预测接触距离场、通过接触引导的优化合成全身运动。

Result: 在ParaHome、HIMO、ARCTIC和OMOMO四个数据集上的实验表明，该方法在单物体、多物体和关节交互场景下均取得了优于或与现有方法相当的性能。

Conclusion: 表面关键点轨迹表示能够有效统一处理多物体协调和多样化关节机制，无需显式的关节类型说明；三阶段生成框架结合接触距离场能够生成高质量的全身人-物交互运动。

Abstract: Daily activities require humans to coordinate whole-body motion with the motion of surrounding objects. Despite recent progress in human-object interaction (HOI) generation, most existing methods assume interactions with a single rigid object and do not extend well to scenarios involving a variable number of objects or articulated objects with diverse joint mechanisms. We propose surface keypoint trajectories as an object motion representation: for each rigid component, whether a standalone object or one part of an articulated assembly, we track a small set of non-collinear surface points over time.
