### [101] [MuRA: Multi-Rank Adaptation for Efficient and Effective Test-Time Vision-Language Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03885)
*Gengyuan Liu,Nanzhou Wang,Chang Liu,Qinwen Wu,Zhenhao Wang,Jiacong Wang,Bokui Chen,Xiangyang Ji*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MuRA框架，通过动态选择与融合不同秩的适配模块来应对视觉输入信息密度的变化，解决现有测试时自适应方法中静态秩配置导致的过拟合与欠拟合问题，在领域泛化和跨数据集基准上达到SOTA精度并降低计算与内存开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型在分布偏移下性能显著下降，虽然基于低秩适配的测试时自适应提供了参数高效的解决方案，但现有方法依赖静态秩配置。由于视觉输入天然具有不同的信息密度，固定秩会导致优化上的妥协——复杂场景欠拟合、简单场景过拟合，这是一个根本性的瓶颈。

Method: MuRA框架包含三个核心组件：(1) 多秩正交分解（Multi-Rank Orthogonal Decomposition）提供知识保持的初始化；(2) 统一组件融合与连续路由器更新（Unified Component Fusion with Continuous Router Updating）学习语义到秩的映射；(3) 基于token级视觉复杂度动态选择并融合不同容量的适配模块。同时提供了自适应机制必要性和梯度稳定性的数学证明。

Result: MuRA在广泛的领域泛化和跨数据集基准上取得了最先进的精度，同时显著降低了计算和内存开销。其动态设计在视觉最深层的表现尤为突出，利用最短的梯度反向传播路径获得最佳效果。

Conclusion: MuRA通过动态秩选择机制有效解决了静态秩配置的固有局限，在保持参数效率的同时显著提升了视觉语言模型在分布偏移下的测试时自适应性能，并通过理论证明与实验验证了该框架的有效性与高效性。

Abstract: Vision-language models exhibit remarkable zero-shot capabilities but suffer significant performance degradation under distribution shifts. While test-time adaptation (TTA) via Low-Rank Adaptation offers a parameter-efficient solution, we identify a fundamental bottleneck in current methods: the reliance on static rank configurations. Because visual inputs inherently possess varying information densities, a fixed rank forces an inevitable optimization compromise, leading to underfitting on complex scenes and overfitting on simple ones. To bridge this gap, we propose Multi-Rank Adaptation (MuRA), a novel framework that dynamically selects and fuses adaptation modules of varying capacities based on token-level visual complexity. MuRA synergizes Multi-Rank Orthogonal Decomposition to provide a superior, knowledge-preserving initialization, and Unified Component Fusion with Continuous Router Updating to sustainably learn semantic-to-rank mappings. Furthermore, we provide rigorous theoretical justifications mathematically proving the necessity and gradient stability of this adaptive mechanism. Crucially, MuRA's dynamic design uniquely thrives at the deepest visual layer, capitalizing on the shortest gradient backpropagation path. Extensive experiments demonstrate that MuRA achieves state-of-the-art accuracy across extensive domain generalization and cross-dataset benchmarks while significantly reducing both computational and memory overhead.

</details>


### [102] [CARE-X: Towards Clinically Useful Radiology VLMs with Auxiliary Supervision, Reward-Aligned Learning, and Tool-Augmented Measurement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03890)
*Mercy Prasanna Ranjit,Anirban Porya,Sathvik Joel,Niharika Vadlamudi,Nikhilesh Chowdary Eathamukkala,Prasanth V,Abhyuday Kumara Swamy,Pranay Narhari Umredkar,Pradeep Narayan,Vivek Rajagopal,Tanuja Ganu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CARE-X是一个胸部X射线视觉语言模型，通过统一辅助判别监督（焦点损失分类和复合损失定位头）与奖励对齐生成（DAPO策略优化），同时结合Qwen3-VL-4B-Instruct的原生工具调用能力实现确定性测量，在报告生成、VQA、空间定位和测量依赖诊断等多个任务上达到或超越现有最佳性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有胸部X射线视觉语言模型仅擅长流畅的报告生成，但缺乏临床所需的判别性发现分类（可调阈值）、空间定位和基于解剖测量的诊断能力。这些任务被当作独立问题处理，导致生成模型与放射科医生的实际需求之间存在明显差距。

Method: CARE-X采用多任务联合训练框架：1) 在生成骨干网络上增加焦点损失分类头和复合损失定位头，与语言建模目标共同训练，实现辅助判别监督；2) 提出解耦裁剪与动态采样策略优化（DAPO），利用任务特定的奖励信号优化报告生成、视觉问答和空间定位；3) 将Qwen3-VL-4B-Instruct与原生工具调用能力结合，实现确定性测量工具调用并保留完整图像视觉访问。

Result: 在四个报告生成基准上多数指标达到最先进水平；ReXVQA上VQA准确率达94.0%（比次优基线高6.0个百分点）；生成式空间解码接近专用检测头的性能；在五个测量依赖条件下，混合推理相较仅感知基线平均F1提升43.6个百分点。

Conclusion: 结构化判别预测与文本生成可以相互增强而非相互矛盾，将辅助判别监督、奖励对齐生成和确定性工具调用统一到一个框架中是可行的，且能显著缩小生成式VLMs与临床实际需求之间的差距。

Abstract: A clinically useful chest X-ray system must go beyond fluent report generation: it should classify findings with tunable decision thresholds, localize them spatially, and derive the anatomical measurements upon which many diagnoses depend. Today's Vision-Language Models (VLMs) treat these as separate problems, if they address them at all, leaving a gap between what radiologists need and what generative models provide. We introduce CARE-X, a chest X-ray VLM that narrows this gap by unifying auxiliary discriminative supervision with reward-aligned generation. CARE-X augments its generative backbone with focal-loss classification and composite-loss grounding heads, co-trained alongside the language-modeling objective. This auxiliary supervision produces discriminative diagnostic predictions with tunable decision thresholds and precise spatial localization while also improving report quality, providing evidence that structured prediction and generation reinforce one another. Building on this foundation, Decoupled Clip and Dynamic Sampling Policy Optimization (DAPO) leverages task-specific reward signals for report generation, visual question answering (VQA), and spatial grounding, directly optimizing the clinical quality metrics that matter in practice. The result is state-of-the-art performance on the majority of metrics across four report-generation benchmarks, 94.0% VQA accuracy on ReXVQA (+6.0 pp over the next-best baseline), and generative spatial decoding that reaches near parity with dedicated detection heads. Separately, to address measurement-dependent diagnoses, we couple Qwen3-VL-4B-Instruct with native tool-calling capabilities for invoking deterministic measurement tools, while retaining full visual access to the image. This hybrid inference yields +43.6 pp average F1 over perception-only baselines across five measurement-dependent conditions.

</details>


### [103] [NCGR: Noise-Conditional Gated Rectification for Camera Extrinsic Perturbations in BEV 3D Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03895)
*Wenbin Pan,Wanhao Liu,Liwei Luo,Panshuo Li,Yong Xu,Renquan Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出NCGR方法，用于解决BEV 3D检测中相机外参扰动导致的性能退化问题。NCGR通过预测2D矫正偏移并由相机级门控调制，在不显式估计完整6自由度外参修正的情况下补偿投影误差，在动态外参扰动下显著优于现有方法，且在干净外参下保持与基线相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于相机的BEV 3D检测通常假设外参准确且固定，但实际中外参扰动会导致空间交叉注意力中BEV参考点的图像平面投影发生偏移，使查询从错误区域采样特征，严重降低检测性能。现有方法需要显式估计完整六自由度外参修正，较为复杂。

Method: NCGR为每个查询-相机对预测2D矫正偏移，并通过相机级门控进行调制，以矫正基准投影后再进行原生可变形采样。训练过程中，用于构建条件和门控的扰动派生量通过调度插值逐步被由辅助标量（从相机特征预测）生成的对应量替代，从而支持无扰动元数据的盲推理。训练采用权重共享的干净教师/扰动学生配对，并通过两分支间的BEV一致性目标监督矫正模块。

Result: 在nuScenes的五个相机动态压力测试中，NCGR达到39.69% NDS，大幅优于BEVFormer的28.00%和CAPE的33.23%。在干净外参条件下，NCGR性能与BEVFormer相当。

Conclusion: NCGR有效解决了外参扰动下BEV 3D检测的性能退化问题，无需显式估计完整外参修正即可补偿投影误差，同时支持盲推理，兼顾了鲁棒性和性能。

Abstract: Camera-based bird's-eye-view (BEV) 3D detection typically assumes accurate and fixed camera extrinsics. In detectors using spatial cross-attention (SCA), extrinsic perturbations displace the image-plane projections of BEV reference points, causing queries to sample features from incorrect regions and degrading detection performance. To address this failure mode, Noise-Conditional Gated Rectification (NCGR) is proposed to compensate for projection errors without explicitly estimating a full six-degree-of-freedom extrinsic correction. For each query-camera pair, a 2D rectification offset is predicted and modulated by a camera-level gate to rectify the base projection before native deformable sampling. During training, the perturbation-derived quantities used to construct the condition and gate are gradually replaced through scheduled interpolation by counterparts generated from an auxiliary scalar predicted from camera features. This transition enables blind inference without perturbation metadata. During training, a weight-shared clean-teacher/perturbed-student pair is used, and the rectification module is supervised by a BEV-consistency objective between the two branches. NCGR is evaluated on nuScenes with simulated dynamic and static extrinsic perturbations. In a five-camera dynamic stress test, NCGR achieves 39.69% NDS, compared with 28.00% for BEVFormer and 33.23% for CAPE. Under clean extrinsics, NCGR maintains performance comparable to that of BEVFormer.

</details>


### [104] [UniEvo-RS: Omni-Prompt Unified Remote Sensing Segmentation with Representative Exemplar-Driven Prototype Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03911)
*Kunquan Zhang,Peilang Li,Xikun Hu,Yunkai Yang,Yushan Zou,Zhiwei Zhang,Runmin Dong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UniEvo-RS是一个统一的遥感分割框架，通过多指令提示数据集和代表性子示例驱动的原型演化机制，实现了多样化的提示任务统一和无需训练的渐进式精度提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 静态视觉语言模型在遇到新场景、未见类别或视觉混淆背景时性能严重下降，且现有统一范式主要依赖图像内特定提示，缺乏灵活的任务路由以适应多意图操作流程。

Method: 1) 构建多指令提示数据集，统一文本驱动和视觉驱动提示于单一架构，建立动态任务路由机制；2) 引入代表性反馈驱动的无训练原型演化机制，通过对比人工标注与初始预测，将预测错误蒸馏为正负原型，增强LLM查询召回并抑制空间背景噪声。

Result: 在大多数设置下实现了最先进的性能，通过极少量的子示例交互，在批量标注过程中对未见类别实现了无训练的渐进式精度提升。

Conclusion: UniEvo-RS通过统一提示框架和原型演化机制，有效解决了遥感分割中静态模型适应性差的问题，为大规模批量标注提供了高效路径。

Abstract: Prompt-driven vision-language models (VLMs) hold immense promise for accelerating dense remote sensing (RS) annotation, but static models suffer from severe performance degradation when deployed on novel scenes, unseen categories, or visually confusing backgrounds. Moreover, existing unified paradigms primarily rely on intra-image specific prompts, lacking flexible task routing to adapt to multi-intent operational workflows. In practical batch mapping, annotators typically refine a small set of representative samples before processing large datasets. Motivated by this practice, we propose UniEvo-RS, an omni-prompt unified RS segmentation framework equipped with representative exemplar-driven prototype evolution. First, we construct a multi-instruction prompt dataset that unifies text-driven and visual-driven prompts within a single architecture, establishing a dynamic task-routing mechanism for highly diverse RS annotation scenarios. Second, we introduce a representative feedback-driven, training-free prototype evolution mechanism. By contrasting manual annotations with initial predictions on exemplars, UniEvo-RS distills prediction errors into positive and negative prototypes. These prototypes enhance LLM query recall and suppress spatial background noise under a fixed-budget clustering memory. Extensive experiments show that UniEvo-RS unifies diverse prompting tasks, achieving state-of-the-art performance across most settings. Crucially, with minimal interaction on a few exemplars, it enables training-free, progressive accuracy enhancement on unseen categories during batch annotation.

</details>


### [105] [StreamDAM: Presence-Aware Memory for Real-Time Streaming Video Object Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03912)
*Xiang Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对视频目标分割（VOS）追踪器在实际流式协议下性能崩塌的问题，提出了一种新的方法（\method{}），通过内存管道重建和在模型内部进行优化，使记忆机制能以帧率运行，并用单一学习到的存在信号来管理记忆的进入、读取范围、输出抑制和重新检测，在四个基准和五个基线模型上取得了最强的流式追踪效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前顶级VOS追踪器（如DAM4SAM）在离线评测中表现优异，但在真实的30帧/秒流式协议下性能大幅下降——因为其丰富的记忆机制运行速度太慢，无法及时跟上帧率，且输出的结果无法判断目标是否存在。作者将这两个失败根源追溯到追踪器的内存管道，并决定针对流式场景重建该管道。

Method: 作者提出\method{}方法，核心思路是：（1）让记忆机制本身在模型内部以帧率运行，通过内部优化而非外挂的降级回退方案；（2）使用单一的学习到的存在信号（presence signal）来统一控制四个关键决策——什么内容进入记忆、追踪器向前读取多远、何时抑制输出、何时重新检测目标。机制分析表明固定策略无法取胜，因为对目标真正消失有帮助的控制策略会对目标难以看到的情况造成伤害，因此必须逐帧做出决策。

Result: 在四个基准数据集和五个现代基线模型的评测中，\method{}成为最强的流式追踪器，在时间约束下恢复了离线模型近全部的精度，并且在最难的内容上甚至超过了其所基于的离线模型。

Conclusion: 流式VOS追踪不能简单在离线模型上外挂回退机制，需要从内存管道层面重新设计，将流式感知内建到模型优化中，并通过逐帧的学习式控制信号来动态平衡精度与速度。

Abstract: Quality-tier video object segmentation (VOS) trackers such as DAM4SAM top accuracy leaderboards, but they are measured offline, one frame at a time with no clock. Under an honest streaming protocol at 30 frames per second, where a frame that misses its budget is served the last mask already computed, the winner collapses: the rich memory that makes it accurate is too slow to keep up, and what it emits is blind to whether the object is even present. We trace both failures to one place, the tracker's memory pipeline, and rebuild it for streaming. \method{} makes the memory machinery itself run at frame rate through in-model optimization rather than a bolted-on fallback, and governs it with a single learned presence signal that decides what enters memory, how far back the tracker reads, when to withhold output, and when to re-detect. A mechanism analysis shows why a fixed policy cannot win: the control that helps when an object truly disappears is the one that hurts when it is merely hard to see, so the choice must be made per frame. Across four benchmarks and five modern baselines, \method{} is the strongest streaming tracker, recovers nearly all of the offline model's accuracy under the clock, and on the hardest content exceeds the offline model it is built from.

</details>


### [106] [Multi-Camera Trajectory Forecasting with Trajectory Tensors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2108.04694)
*Olly Styles,Tanaya Guha,Victor Sanchez*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了多相机轨迹预测（MCTF）问题，用于在相机网络间预测移动物体的轨迹。作者提出了"Which-When-Where"框架和轨迹张量这一新概念来编码跨相机的轨迹及其不确定性，并开发了多种编码器-解码器MCTF模型，在包含600小时视频数据、15个相机视角的自建数据库上验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有轨迹预测方法主要集中于单相机轨迹预测（SCTF），无法满足监控和交通管理等广泛应用场景的需求。单相机的视野限制使得长期轨迹预测成为不可能，因此需要一种能够同时利用多个相机视角进行轨迹预测的方法。

Method: 提出MCTF框架，采用Which-When-Where方法同时预测物体在哪些相机中出现、何时出现以及在相机视图中何处出现。核心创新是提出了轨迹张量（trajectory tensors）概念，用于编码跨多个相机视角的轨迹及相关不确定性。基于此开发了多种编码器-解码器MCTF模型。

Result: 在自建的包含600小时视频数据、15个相机视角的数据集上进行了大量实验。结果显示轨迹张量模型优于基于坐标轨迹的MCTF模型，也优于现有的SCTF方法改编而成的MCTF方法。

Conclusion: 轨迹张量作为一种新的编码方式，能够有效利用多相机信息进行轨迹预测，为多相机场景下的长期轨迹预测提供了可行的解决方案，代码已开源。

Abstract: We introduce the problem of multi-camera trajectory forecasting (MCTF), which involves predicting the trajectory of a moving object across a network of cameras. While multi-camera setups are widespread for applications such as surveillance and traffic monitoring, existing trajectory forecasting methods typically focus on single-camera trajectory forecasting (SCTF), limiting their use for such applications. Furthermore, using a single camera limits the field-of-view available, making long-term trajectory forecasting impossible. We address these shortcomings of SCTF by developing an MCTF framework that simultaneously uses all estimated relative object locations from several viewpoints and predicts the object's future location in all possible viewpoints. Our framework follows a Which-When-Where approach that predicts in which camera(s) the objects appear and when and where within the camera views they appear. To this end, we propose the concept of trajectory tensors: a new technique to encode trajectories across multiple camera views and the associated uncertainties. We develop several encoder-decoder MCTF models for trajectory tensors and present extensive experiments on our own database (comprising 600 hours of video data from 15 camera views) created particularly for the MCTF task. Results show that our trajectory tensor models outperform coordinate trajectory-based MCTF models and existing SCTF methods adapted for MCTF. Code is available from: https://github.com/olly-styles/Trajectory-Tensors

</details>


### [107] [When and Where to Look: Adaptive Visual Evidence Scheduling for Efficient Long Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03918)
*Ke Li,Jiayu Chen,Maoliang Li,Zihao Zheng,Hailong Zou,Hengyi Zhang,Xuanzhe Liu,Xiang Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出EcoFrame，一个无需训练的框架，用于长视频理解中的查询自适应视觉证据调度。它通过熵门控的预算调度和注意力引导的候选提议，在保持准确率的同时显著降低推理开销，在多个基准和VLM骨干上取得更好的准确率-效率权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长视频理解的视觉证据筛选方法存在两类问题：基于相关性的方法依赖静态一次性选择，缺乏自适应性；基于智能体的调度器虽然自适应但需要昂贵的多轮推理。目标是在低开销下实现查询自适应的帧选择。

Method: EcoFrame包含两个核心机制：(1) 熵门控预算调度——利用VLM的推理反馈（输出不确定性）判断当前证据是否充分，决定提前停止或逐步扩展帧预算；(2) 注意力引导候选提议——将帧级注意力转化为时间先验，在信息密集区域进行稠密局部搜索，同时在注意力分散时保持全局覆盖。

Result: 在Video-MME、LongVideoBench和MLVU上，EcoFrame在多个VLM骨干上取得更好的准确率-效率权衡。在Qwen2.5-VL上平均准确率64.4，超过BOLT的63.5；相比AKS和BOLT提供1.85倍加速；相比基于智能体的A.I.R.，在保持可比准确率的同时最高获得13.5倍推理加速。

Conclusion: EcoFrame验证了通过VLM推理反馈（熵）和注意力先验可以替代昂贵的多轮智能体搜索，实现低开销且自适应的视觉证据调度，为长视频理解提供了一种高效且可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Efficient long-video understanding requires vision--language models (VLMs) to reason over a small number of frames selected as sparse visual evidence. Existing relevance-based methods rely on static one-shot selection with fixed frame budgets and candidate pools, while agent-based schedulers achieve adaptivity through costly multi-round reasoning and interactive search. We propose EcoFrame, a training-free framework for low-overhead query-adaptive visual evidence scheduling. EcoFrame leverages the VLM's inference feedback to determine when to increase the frame budget and where to search for additional candidate evidence. Specifically, entropy-gated budget scheduling uses output uncertainty to stop early when the current evidence is sufficient or progressively expand the frame budget otherwise. Meanwhile, attention-guided candidate proposal converts frame-level attention into a temporal prior, enabling dense local search in informative regions while preserving global coverage when attention is diffuse. Experiments on Video-MME, LongVideoBench, and MLVU demonstrate that EcoFrame achieves a better accuracy--efficiency trade-off across multiple VLM backbones. On Qwen2.5-VL, EcoFrame achieves an average accuracy of 64.4, surpassing BOLT at 63.5, while providing a $1.85\times$ speedup over AKS and BOLT. Compared with the agent-based A.I.R., EcoFrame maintains comparable accuracy with up to a $13.5\times$ inference speedup. Code will be available at https://github.com/AK-DREAM/EcoFrame.

</details>


### [108] [CLIP-EBC: CLIP Can Count Accurately through Enhanced Blockwise Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.09281)
*Yiming Ma,Victor Sanchez,Tanaya Guha*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了CLIP-EBC，这是首个完全基于CLIP模型的精准人群密度估计方法。通过增强块状分类（EBC）框架，使用整数值分箱减少标签模糊，并结合基于密度图的回归损失提升计数精度。在NWPU-Crowd测试集上达到MAE 58.2、RMSE 268.5，超越此前最优的STEERER方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CLIP模型在识别任务上表现卓越，但尚未被充分探索用于计数（回归）任务。现有基于分类的人群计数框架存在两个主要局限：将计数值量化为边界相邻的实值分箱导致标签模糊，以及仅关注分类误差导致计数预测不准确。因此需要设计一个能充分利用CLIP识别能力且克服上述局限的计数框架。

Method: 提出增强块状分类（EBC）框架：1）采用整数值分箱替代原有的实值分箱，减少分箱边界附近的标签歧义；2）引入基于密度图的回归损失，改善计数值预测的准确性。在骨干网络无关的EBC框架内，进一步引入CLIP-EBC，充分发挥CLIP的识别能力用于人群计数任务。

Result: EBC框架在UCF-QNRF数据集上将现有基于分类的方法性能提升最高达44.5%。CLIP-EBC在NWPU-Crowd测试集上取得最优性能，MAE为58.2、RMSE为268.5，相较于此前最佳方法STEERER分别提升了8.6%和13.3%。

Conclusion: CLIP-EBC成功证明了CLIP模型能够被有效应用于计数任务，通过EBC框架解决了分类式计数方法的标签模糊和预测不准问题，达到了人群密度估计的最优性能。代码和权重已公开。

Abstract: We propose CLIP-EBC, the first fully CLIP-based model for accurate crowd density estimation. While the CLIP model has demonstrated remarkable success in addressing recognition tasks such as zero-shot image classification, its potential for counting has been largely unexplored due to the inherent challenges in transforming a regression problem, such as counting, into a recognition task. In this work, we investigate and enhance CLIP's ability to count, focusing specifically on the task of estimating crowd sizes from images. Existing classification-based crowd-counting frameworks have significant limitations, including the quantization of count values into bordering real-valued bins and the sole focus on classification errors. These practices result in label ambiguity near the shared borders and inaccurate prediction of count values. Hence, directly applying CLIP within these frameworks may yield suboptimal performance.
  To address these challenges, we first propose the Enhanced Blockwise Classification (EBC) framework. Unlike previous methods, EBC utilizes integer-valued bins, effectively reducing ambiguity near bin boundaries. Additionally, it incorporates a regression loss based on density maps to improve the prediction of count values. Within our backbone-agnostic EBC framework, we then introduce CLIP-EBC to fully leverage CLIP's recognition capabilities for this task. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of EBC and the competitive performance of CLIP-EBC. Specifically, our EBC framework can improve existing classification-based methods by up to 44.5% on the UCF-QNRF dataset, and CLIP-EBC achieves state-of-the-art performance on the NWPU-Crowd test set, with an MAE of 58.2 and an RMSE of 268.5, representing improvements of 8.6% and 13.3% over the previous best method, STEERER. The code and weights are available at https://github.com/Yiming-M/CLIP-EBC.

</details>


### [109] [Low-Dimensional High-Leverage Subspace Optimization: Beyond Full-Parameter Coupled Training for Neural Network Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03919)
*Peng Xia,Junbiao Pang,Zheng Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出归一化仿射预条件（NAP）方法，针对低比特量化中紧凑网络性能严重退化的问题，识别出归一化仿射参数是主导量化鲁棒性的低维高杠杆子空间，并通过定向子空间优化同时改进PTQ和QAT流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低比特量化在紧凑网络上性能严重退化，根源在于全参数耦合训练范式忽略了参数子空间的异质性。紧凑网络特征冗余有限，难以吸收量化误差；传统PTQ在固定预训练模型上重建而不改善固有的量化友好性，QAT则因骨干权重与校准参数之间的梯度耦合而受限。

Method: 提出NAP方法：1）对于PTQ，冻结骨干权重，仅在目标伪量化图下微调仿射参数，主动提升量化友好性；2）对于QAT，引入交替QAT-NAP架构，解耦特征学习和数值校准。理论分析证明BN仿射参数可完全抵消量化失真的通道仿射分量，非线性舍入和裁剪残差构成不可约误差边界；蒸馏引导的NAP作为方向性平坦度优化。

Result: 在ImageNet和CIFAR-100上的实验表明：NAP能够恢复严重坍塌的低比特量化，一致性地提升基于重建的PTQ性能，并以极低调优成本超越饱和的全参数QAT。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了定向低维子空间优化的原理，为高效深度学习提供了超越全参数耦合训练的新视角。

Abstract: Low-bit quantization suffers severe accuracy degradation on compact networks, rooted in the dominant full-parameter coupled training paradigm that ignores parameter subspace heterogeneity. Their limited feature redundancy leaves little room to absorb quantization errors. Conventional pipelines adopt monolithic optimization: PTQ reconstructs fixed pretrained models without improving inherent quantization friendliness; QAT updates all parameters jointly, suffering from gradient coupling between backbone weights and calibration parameters. In this paper, we identify normalization affine parameters as a low-dimensional high-leverage subspace dominating quantization robustness, and propose Normalization Affine Preconditioning (NAP) for targeted subspace optimization. For PTQ, NAP freezes backbone weights and fine-tunes only affine parameters under the target fake-quantization graph on full-precision models, proactively boosting quantization friendliness before downstream reconstruction. For QAT, we introduce an alternating QAT-NAP schema that decouples feature learning and numerical calibration, breaking the performance ceiling of saturated joint training. Theoretical analysis confirms BN affine parameters fully cancel the channel-wise affine component of quantization distortion, while nonlinear rounding and clipping residuals form the irreducible error boundary; distillation-guided NAP acts as directional flatness optimization, projecting teacher-student logit mismatch onto the restricted subspace. Experiments on ImageNet and CIFAR-100 show NAP recovers severely collapsed low-bit quantization, consistently boosts reconstruction-based PTQ, and outperforms saturated full-parameter QAT with negligible tuning cost. This work reveals the principle of targeted low-dimensional subspace optimization, offering a new perspective beyond full-parameter coupled training for efficient deep learning.

</details>


### [110] [GeoMAR: Unleashing Geometrically Aligned Features for Masked Autoregressive Blind Face Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03923)
*Lu Gan,Hanyu Yan,Chaofeng Chen,Junqi Hu,Dan Zeng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GeoMAR是一个基于码本的盲人脸恢复框架，通过双输入几何特征提取和对齐注入器解决特征条件模糊问题，并将一步映射重构为多步掩码自回归过程以实现渐进式细化，在多个基准上取得竞争性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于码本的盲人脸恢复方法面临两个主要挑战：在严重退化条件下，条件特征模糊不清且预测机制脆弱，导致恢复效果不佳。

Method: 提出GeoMAR框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) 双输入提取管线获取基于组件的几何描述，并通过Aligned Geometric Priors Injector（采用KV-Q交换策略）将文本先验与低质量特征融合，生成几何对齐特征；(2) 将一步映射重构为多步掩码自回归（MAR）过程，通过从粗到细的生成方式，基于日益可靠的上下文逐步细化复杂面部区域。

Result: 在一个人工合成基准和三个真实世界基准上的实验表明，GeoMAR相比现有方法取得了具有高度竞争力的感知质量和连贯的视觉结构。

Conclusion: GeoMAR通过几何对齐特征与掩码自回归细化的结合，有效解决了码本BFR中特征模糊和预测机制脆弱的问题，实现了鲁棒的面部恢复，并开源了代码。

Abstract: Codebook-based blind face restoration (BFR) often suffers from ambiguous conditioning features and a fragile prediction mechanism under severe degradation. To address these challenges, we propose GeoMAR, a framework designed to unleash geometrically aligned features with masked autoregressive (MAR) refinement for robust face restoration. For feature conditioning, we introduce a dual-input extraction pipeline to extract component-based geometric descriptions with explicit, spatially faithful anchors. These textual priors are integrated with low-quality (LQ) features via an Aligned Geometric Priors Injector, which employs a KV-Q exchange strategy to generate geometrically aligned features. For prediction mechanism, we reformulate the one-step mapping into a multi-step MAR process. This coarse-to-fine generation progressively refines complex facial regions based on increasingly reliable context. Experiments on one synthetic and three real-world benchmarks demonstrate that GeoMAR achieves highly competitive perceptual quality and coherent visual structures compared with existing methods. The code is available at https://github.com/BRL-SYSU/GeoMAR.git.

</details>


### [111] [Progressive Learning of a Diffusion-based Inpainting Model for Separating Overlapped Fingerprints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03937)
*Noor Hussein,Anil K. Jain,Karthik Nandakumar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于扩散模型的指纹重叠分离方法，将分离问题建模为图像修复（inpainting）任务，通过多阶段渐进训练的方式，从预训练的Stable Diffusion模型出发，逐步引入指纹先验知识、局部指纹补全能力，最终实现重叠感知的修复重建。在两个公开数据集上的实验表明，该方法重建出的单枚指纹能以很高概率与其匹配对应指纹成功配对。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重叠摩擦脊线指纹模式在犯罪现场潜在指纹提取和活体扫描场景中是一个常见问题，现有方法要么依赖基于规则的取向场补全（需要强领域知识），要么训练端到端深度神经网络（未考虑领域特定因素）。因此需要一种能够结合领域知识同时具备深度学习能力的分离方法。

Method: 提出一个多阶段渐进式扩散模型流水线：1）从预训练的Stable Diffusion模型出发；2）逐步引入指纹先验；3）增加部分指纹补全能力；4）最终提出"重叠感知修复"方法，基于多通道条件使用扩散修复模型分别重建每个组成指纹。

Result: 在两个公开数据集上的实验表明，使用所提出的基于扩散的修复方法重建的组成指纹，能够以非常高的概率与其匹配对应指纹成功匹配。

Conclusion: 扩散模型结合领域先验知识可以有效解决重叠指纹分离问题，该方法在准确率和匹配概率上都表现出优越性。

Abstract: Overlapped friction ridge patterns are a recurring problem in latent fingerprints recovered from crime scenes and in live-scan scenarios where residual fingerprints on the sensor may corrupt subsequent acquisitions. Existing approaches for separating overlapped fingerprints either rely on rule-based orientation field completion that requires strong domain knowledge or train end-to-end deep neural networks that do not account for domain-specific considerations. This work introduces a diffusion-based pipeline for separating component fingerprints from an image containing overlapping friction ridge patterns. We formulate the separation problem as an inpainting task and progressively learn a diffusion model for this task in multiple stages. Starting from a pre-trained Stable Diffusion model, we progressively incorporate a fingerprint prior, add the ability to complete partial fingerprints, and finally propose \textbf{overlap-aware inpainting} that reconstructs each component print using a diffusion inpainting model based on multi-channel conditioning. Experiments on two public datasets demonstrate that component fingerprints reconstructed using the proposed diffusion-based inpainting method can match with their mated counterparts with very high probability.

</details>


### [112] [UniWorld-Design: From Pixel Generation to Layer-Native Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03971)
*Zongjian Li,Zhiyuan Yan,Chenxu Bai,Chen Chen,Haoxiang Sun,Shaodong Wang,Feize Wu,Shenghai Yuan,Bin Lin,Zheyuan Liu,Yuwei Niu,Li Yuan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UniWorld-Design是一个将图像生成从像素合成重新定义为结构化视觉组合的框架，以语义RGBA图层作为生成、理解和编辑的基本单元。它包含两个模型：T2RGBA（文本生成独立RGBA素材）和I2L（对完整图像进行图层分解），在Crello基准上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统图像生成模型以像素为基本操作单元，但人类设计师和创作者实际上是通过图层来创建、理解和编辑视觉内容的。像素只定义图像如何渲染，而图层才定义图像如何被创作、理解和编辑。现有方法缺乏以图层为核心的多模态生成范式。

Method: UniWorld-Design包含两个核心模型：(1) Text-to-RGBA (T2RGBA)模型直接从文本生成独立的RGBA素材；(2) Image-to-Layer (I2L)模型以完整图像为输入，结合全局指令和逐层提示，联合生成有序的、完整的语义RGBA图层。I2L的指令接口支持顶层分解、递归分解和定向提取，使分层成为可通过指令寻址的编辑操作。关键创新在于I2L学习的是完整的语义对象而非仅可见像素的分割，因此图层在移动或移除后依然可用。

Result: 在Crello基准上，I2L将逐层RGB L1误差降低了37%，在Alpha Soft IoU上相比Qwen-Image-Layered取得了34%的相对提升。T2RGBA在CLIP Score上达到最高分，优于LayerDiffuse和OmniAlpha。

Conclusion: UniWorld-Design通过将图层作为生成的原子单元，为多模态生成模型提供了图层原生的设计空间，验证了"图层驱动"的图像生成与编辑范式在分解质量和生成质量上均优于传统的像素驱动方法。

Abstract: We introduce UniWorld-Design, a framework that redefines image generation from flat pixel synthesis to structured visual composition, with semantic RGBA layers as the atomic units of generation, understanding, and editing. Our key insight is that pixels define how an image is rendered, whereas layers define how an image is created, understood, and edited. Just as human designers create and manipulate visual content through layers rather than raw pixels, UniWorld-Design equips multimodal generative models with a layer-native design space. UniWorld-Design comprises two models. The Text-to-RGBA (T2RGBA) model generates standalone RGBA assets directly from text. The Image-to-Layer (I2L) model conditions on a finished image, a global instruction and per-layer prompts, and jointly produces ordered, complete semantic RGBA layers. Its instruction interface supports top-level decomposition, recursive decomposition and targeted extraction, making layering an instruction-addressable operation for agentic editing. Because I2L learns complete semantic objects rather than visible-pixel partitions, its layers stay usable when moved or removed. On the Crello benchmark, I2L reduces per-layer RGB L1 error by 37% and achieves a 34% relative improvement in Alpha Soft IoU over Qwen-Image-Layered. Separately, T2RGBA achieves the highest CLIP Score, outperforming LayerDiffuse and OmniAlpha.

</details>


### [113] [JoyAI-Video-Edit: Real-Time Open-Ended Video Editing with Autoregressive Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03974)
*Yicheng Xiao,Wenxun Dai,Xinran Qin,Lin Song,Maoquan Zhang,Hang Xu,Yukang Chen,Yitong Li,Guohui Zhang,Yuan Zhang,Xuying Zhang,Tommy Zhang,Jianlong Yuan,Peihao Li,Shuai Lu,Siming Fu,Chuyang Zhao,Xin Han,Jie Huang,Wenbo Li,Guoqing Ma,Wei Huang,Xiaojuan Qi,Haoyang Huang,Nan Duan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: JoyAI-Video-Edit 是一个16B参数的自回归扩散框架，用于实时、开放式视频编辑。它通过分块自回归适配、源锚定分布匹配蒸馏和长视野自回归蒸馏三种技术，在无需未来帧和预先定义视频时长的情况下，实现了单块Nvidia B200 GPU上约30 FPS的720p端到端视频编辑，显著优于现有流式编辑系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时视频编辑需要低延迟因果生成，同时满足有限的算力资源、保持源视频保真度和长期时间一致性。现有系统在这方面的表现不足，亟需新的方法来解决流式编辑中的生成效率和保真度问题。

Method: 提出16B参数的自回归扩散框架，结合三种核心技术：1) 分块自回归适配（chunk-wise autoregressive adaptation）以支持无需未来帧的因果生成；2) 源锚定分布匹配蒸馏（SA-DMD）在两步生成过程中保持源视频保真度；3) 长视野自回归蒸馏（Long-Horizon Autoregressive Distillation）减少训练与推理之间的不匹配并缓解累积的时间漂移问题。

Result: 大量自动评估和人工评估表明，JoyAI-Video-Edit在短视频和长视频上都显著优于现有流式编辑系统，并与强离线系统表现相当。完整系统在单块Nvidia B200 GPU上实现了约30 FPS的720p端到端视频编辑。

Conclusion: JoyAI-Video-Edit通过自回归扩散框架有效解决了实时视频编辑中的效率、保真度和时间一致性问题，实现了与离线系统竞争的性能，为实时流式视频编辑领域提供了重要的技术方案。

Abstract: Real-time video editing requires low-latency causal generation with bounded computational resources while preserving source fidelity and long-term temporal consistency. We present JoyAI-Video-Edit, a 16B-parameter autoregressive diffusion framework for real-time, open-ended video editing without access to future frames or a predefined video duration. Our method combines chunk-wise autoregressive adaptation, Source-Anchored Distribution Matching Distillation (SA-DMD), and Long-Horizon Autoregressive Distillation to reduce train--inference mismatch, preserve source fidelity during two-step generation, and mitigate accumulated temporal drift. Extensive automatic and human evaluations show that JoyAI-Video-Edit substantially outperforms existing streaming editors and remains competitive with strong offline systems on both short and long videos. The complete system achieves end-to-end 720p video editing at approximately 30 FPS on a single Nvidia B200 GPU. Code is available at https://github.com/jd-opensource/JoyAI-Video-Edit.

</details>


### [114] [Video-DeepResearch: Towards the Next-Generation Multimodal Deepresearch Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03979)
*Zhen Fang,Yu Zeng,Wenxuan Huang,Yiming Zhao,Shiting Huang,Tianfei Ren,Qi Lu,Qingnan Ren,Qisheng Su,Lionel Z. Wang,Qingyu Yin,Shuang Chen,Zehui Chen,Lin Chen,Zhenfei Yin,Yao Hu,Shaohui Lin,Wanli Ouyang,Shaosheng Cao,Feng Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Video-DeepResearch（Video-DR），将多模态智能体从静态图像扩展到连续视频流，解决视频理解中密集时空定位与开放网络探索相结合的问题。通过解耦感知-探索流水线和分阶段工具解锁，配合SFT+GRPO两阶段训练策略，在自建的Video-DR-Bench基准上达到64.0%的平均准确率，超越Claude-4.5-Sonnet、GPT-5和Gemini 2.5 Pro等商业模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型在处理视频流与开放网络探索结合的任务时存在两大关键瓶颈：一是模态偏差（智能体绕过视觉工具而偏向文本搜索），二是参数化知识泄漏（模型依赖内部记忆而非真正的工具增强执行），这些严重制约了视频多模态智能体的真实推理能力。

Method: 提出Video-DR框架，核心包括：1）解耦的感知-探索流水线，通过分阶段工具解锁强制模型在网络检索前进行全面的跨帧视觉接地；2）两阶段训练方案，先进行监督微调（SFT），再使用组相对策略优化（GRPO），实现超越模仿学习上限的自主探索；3）构建Video-DR-Bench基准，包含200个复杂多跳VQA实例，采用人机协作方式标注。

Result: Video-DeepResearch-35B-A3B达到64.0%的平均准确率，超越Claude-4.5-Sonnet（59.0%）5.0个百分点，显著优于GPT-5（52.5%）和Gemini 2.5 Pro（57.5%）；30B-A3B变体达到59.3%，与Claude-4.5-Sonnet相当，验证了该训练范式在紧凑模型规模下的有效性。

Conclusion: Video-DR通过解耦感知-探索架构和强化的两阶段训练策略，有效解决了视频多模态智能体的模态偏差和知识泄漏问题，实现了开源模型在视频深度研究任务上对闭源商业模型的全面超越，为该领域树立了新的性能标杆。

Abstract: We introduce Video-DeepResearch (Video-DR), extending multimodal agents from static images to continuous video streams, a setting that demands dense spatiotemporal grounding coupled with open-web exploration. Preliminary evaluations reveal two critical bottlenecks in current models: (1) modality bias, where agents bypass visual tools in favor of textual search, and (2) parametric knowledge leakage, where models rely on internal memory rather than genuine tool-augmented execution. To address these challenges, we propose Video-DR, featuring a decoupled perception-exploration pipeline with stage-wise tool unlocking that compels exhaustive cross-frame visual grounding prior to web retrieval. Our framework adopts a two-stage training recipe: supervised fine-tuning followed by Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), enabling autonomous exploration that breaks the imitation-learning ceiling. Furthermore, we curate Video-DR-Bench, a human-AI collaborative benchmark comprising 200 complex, multi-hop VQA instances. Empirical results demonstrate that our Video-DeepResearch-35B-A3B establishes a new state-of-the-art of 64.0% average accuracy, surpassing proprietary Claude-4.5-Sonnet (59.0%) by 5.0 points and significantly outperforming GPT-5 (52.5%) and Gemini 2.5 Pro (57.5%). The 30B-A3B variant achieves 59.3%, competitive with Claude-4.5-Sonnet and demonstrating the effectiveness of our training paradigm even at compact scale. Code: https://github.com/Osilly/Vision-DeepResearch.

</details>


### [115] [Perceptual Anchoring: Prototype-Guided Text Calibration for Training-free Open-Vocabulary Semantic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03991)
*Wanli Ma,Jiangwen Lu,Qinmu Peng,Xinge You*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为PTC（Prototype-Guided Text Calibration）的免训练开集语义分割方法。该方法通过"感知-锚定"两个阶段，利用视觉原型动态校准文本嵌入，弥合通用类别概念与实例特定外观之间的语义鸿沟，无需额外训练即可作为即插即用模块提升现有方法性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有免训练开集语义分割方法主要优化视觉表示，但将仅编码通用类别概念的文本嵌入作为固定的分类参考，导致通用概念与捕捉目标实例特定外观的视觉表示之间存在语义鸿沟，进而产生不完整的掩码和错误预测。

Method: PTC包含两个阶段：1）感知阶段（Perceiving）：基于初始匹配分数选择可靠的视觉证据，构建类别特定的视觉原型；2）锚定阶段（Anchoring）：利用视觉原型校准对应的文本嵌入，校准强度根据视觉证据的数量自适应调整。该方法无需额外训练或外部模型，可作为即插即用模块。

Result: 在八个基准数据集上的大量实验表明，PTC显著提升了六种代表性方法的性能，产生了更完整和准确的分割结果，验证了其在改善视觉-文本对齐方面的有效性。

Conclusion: PTC作为一种简单高效的视觉-文本对齐改进方法，通过在保持通用类别语义和开集泛化能力的同时，使校准后的文本嵌入更准确地与实例特定的视觉表示对齐，有效提升了免训练开集语义分割的性能。

Abstract: Training-free open-vocabulary semantic segmentation (OVSS) partitions an image into semantically distinct regions based on arbitrary text descriptions, without learning any additional parameters. However, existing methods typically focus on improving visual representations while treating text embeddings that encode only generic category concepts as fixed classification references. The resulting semantic gap between these generic concepts and the visual representations that capture the specific appearances of target instances often causes incomplete masks and erroneous predictions in non-target regions. Inspired by the symbol-percept correspondence underlying perceptual anchoring, we propose Prototype-Guided Text Calibration (PTC) for training-free OVSS. In the Perceiving stage, PTC selects reliable visual evidence based on initial matching scores to construct category-specific visual prototypes. In the Anchoring stage, PTC uses these prototypes to calibrate their corresponding text embeddings, with the calibration strength adaptively adjusted based on the amount of visual evidence. Consequently, the calibrated text embeddings align more accurately with instance-specific visual representations while preserving generic category semantics and open-vocabulary generalization. Moreover, PTC requires neither additional training nor external models and can serve as a plug-and-play module for existing methods. Extensive experiments across eight benchmarks show that PTC significantly enhances the performance of six representative methods and yields more complete and accurate segmentation results. These results validate PTC as a simple and effective approach to improving visual-text alignment.

</details>


### [116] [Quo Vadis, World Modeling?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02713)
*Yu Yang,Xuemeng Yang,Licheng Wen,Lingdong Kong,Xiaobin Hu,Dongyue Lu,Wei Chow,Xiyan Huang,Yuxiang Feng,Yue Liao,Jianbiao Mei,Daocheng Fu,Rong Wu,Pinlong Cai,Ran Yi,Ying Tai,Jiangning Zhang,Botian Shi,Yong Liu,Shuicheng Yan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出"智能体中心交互式世界代理"（Agent-Centric Interactive World Proxies）概念，将世界建模从物理状态预测范式转向智能体可用信息（如执行结果、经验技能、验证信号）的传递范式，并按反馈模态划分为六类代理，按赋能程度划分为三个递进层次，为持续改进的智能体提供可扩展、低成本的交互反馈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续改进的智能体需要动态交互反馈，但真实环境交互成本高、速度慢、不安全且难以并行化。传统世界模型仅通过未来物理状态预测作为代理，范围狭窄，无法为需要可操作反馈的智能体提供足够支持。

Method: 本文是概念性/框架性研究。首先提出从"物理状态转移"到"智能体可用信息转移"的范式转换；然后按反馈模态将世界代理系统化分为六类：动力学、空间、执行、记忆/经验、技能、奖励/验证代理；最后提出三个渐进赋能层次：推理时引导（L.1）、训练时优化（L.2）、智能体-代理协同进化（L.3）。

Result: 构建了世界代理设计空间的系统化映射框架，涵盖六种功能形式与三个赋能层次，重新定位了世界建模在智能体持续改进中的角色，并为后续研究提供了路线图。

Conclusion: 本文将世界建模重塑为智能体中心范式，确立了一条通过世界代理使智能体更好地规划、更快学习、持续进化的路线图。

Abstract: Continually improving agents require dynamic interaction feedback beyond static supervision, yet direct real-environment interaction is costly, slow, unsafe, and hard to parallelize. World modeling offers a natural intermediate proxy that allows agents to query lower-cost, more controllable feedback before committing to real actions. Classical world models instantiate this proxy primarily through future physical-state prediction, a formulation useful yet narrow for agents that require actionable feedback beyond raw state transitions. In this work, we conceptualize Agent-Centric Interactive World Proxies, shifting the fundamental paradigm from physical state transitions to agent-usable information transitions, such as execution outcomes, retrieved experiences or skills, and verification signals, broadening the scope of world modeling to provide versatile feedback for continually improving agents. To systematically map this design space, we organize world proxies into six functional forms based on their feedback modalities: dynamics, spatial, execution, memory/experience, skill, and reward/verification proxies, which together characterize the primary ways world modeling serves agent improvement. We further analyze how these proxies empower agents across three progressive levels: L.1 Inference-Time Guidance, where proxy outputs enrich in-context information for superior decisions; L.2 Training-Time Optimization, where proxy outputs yield rewards, critiques, or synthetic rollouts for policy learning; and L.3 Agent-Proxy Co-Evolution, where real-environment evidence continuously updates both the proxy and the agent for co-evolution. Ultimately, this work recasts world modeling into an agent-centric paradigm, establishing a roadmap for building world proxies that empower agents to plan better, learn faster, and evolve continually.

</details>


### [117] [SAGE: Semantic Explainability of Attention-Based Survival Models in Computational Pathology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02803)
*Abdallah Lamane,Abdul Rahman Diab,Ren-Chin Wu,William Lotter*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SAGE是一个后置的解释框架，利用病理视觉语言模型将冻结的ABMIL生存预测模型的注意力图转化为基于25个组织学概念的语言化全局解释，能够跨患者队列量化各概念与预测风险的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ABMIL是计算病理学中切片级预测的主流方法，但其注意力图仅提供局部解释（表明模型关注的位置，而非哪些组织学特征驱动预测），且无法解释模型在患者队列层面的行为，缺乏全局、语义化的解释工具。

Method: SAGE使用病理视觉语言模型，将图像块与25个组织学概念字典进行评分，再根据模型学到的注意力权重聚合这些分数，从而量化每个概念与队列中预测风险的关联程度。

Result: 在七个TCGA癌症队列和三个基础模型上的生存预测实验中，SAGE恢复了已知的预后特征（如坏死的负面关联），并揭示了癌症特异性生物学（如肾细胞癌中与分子亚型一致的有利血管生成特征）。消融实验表明这些关联取决于模型学到的注意力而非概念本身出现的频率，且概念字典捕捉了基础模型特征中的大部分预后信息。

Conclusion: SAGE提供了一种可扩展、模型无关的框架，用于理解ABMIL生存模型所学到的内容，使病理学家能够在队列层面解释模型行为，并具有生物标志物发现的潜力。

Abstract: Attention-based multiple instance learning (ABMIL) is the predominant approach for slide-level prediction in computational pathology, yet its attention maps provide only local explanations: they indicate where a model focuses but not which histological features drive its predictions or how the model behaves across a patient cohort. We present Semantic Attention Global Explanations (SAGE), a post-hoc framework that extracts global, language-grounded explanations from a frozen ABMIL model. Using a pathology vision-language model, SAGE scores image patches against a dictionary of 25 histological concepts, aggregates these scores according to the model's learned attention, and quantifies how each concept relates to prediction risk across a cohort. Applied to survival prediction using seven TCGA cancer cohorts and three foundation models, SAGE recovered established prognostic features, such as the adverse association of necrosis, while revealing cancer-specific biology, including a favorable angiogenic signature in renal cell carcinoma consistent with known molecular subtypes. Ablation studies demonstrated that these associations depend on the model's learned attention rather than concept prevalence alone, and that the concept dictionary captures much of the prognostic information encoded by the foundation model features. Through semantically-grounded explanations, SAGE provides a scalable, model-agnostic framework for understanding what ABMIL survival models learn, enabling pathologists to interpret model behavior at the cohort level and offering the potential for biomarker identification.

</details>


### [118] [In-Context Collapse in Vision-Language Models and How to Mitigate it?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02830)
*Mohammad Rostami*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发现视觉-语言模型（VLM）在多示例上下文学习（ICL）中，随着演示样本累积会出现"上下文崩溃"现象——准确率急剧甚至灾难性下降，部分模型跌至随机水平以下。通过参数匹配的消融与拯救实验，作者将崩溃定位到视觉-语言整合通路，并提出CircA——一种一次性训练的"整合疫苗"，可在单一合成任务上训练后迁移至未见任务族，恢复模型对积累演示的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流观点假设VLM的多示例上下文学习会随演示数量增加而提升，但作者发现实际情况相反：演示累积会导致部分VLM出现严重性能崩溃。这一反直觉现象此前未被系统研究，理解其机制对VLM的实际部署至关重要。

Method: 在0.5B到11B的开源VLM面板及前沿模型Claude Sonnet 4.5上，覆盖合成分类、自然图像分类和VQA基准进行系统评估；通过参数匹配的消融-拯救实验，在连接器和早期/中层添加适配器进行因果定位；提出CircA，其核心是在单一合成任务上训练一次"整合疫苗"，再迁移到未见任务族验证其可迁移性。

Result: 崩溃现象在多个模型和基准上呈分级出现；对演示积累的鲁棒性与上下文学习新规则的能力可分离，产生三种可复现的"机制"；在连接器及早期/中层加适配器可将16-shot重映射准确率从0.39提升至0.91，而等容量的晚期读出适配器无效；CircA疫苗迁移到CIFAR/Fashion后分别达到0.71/0.60（从随机水平恢复）。晚期读出层在基于权重的整合上更高准确率和更少遗忘，但并非上下文整合的最佳层。

Conclusion: 上下文崩溃本质上是视觉-语言接口处的整合失败，而非模型容量或任务本身的问题；它可以通过轻量级、可迁移的干预加以纠正。值得注意的是，适合上下文整合的层与适合基于权重的整合（如微调）的层并不相同，这对模型设计有重要启示。

Abstract: Many-shot in-context learning (ICL) lets vision-language models (VLMs) adapt from image--label demonstrations without weight updates, and is widely assumed to improve as more demonstrations are supplied. We show the opposite: as demonstrations accumulate, a subset of VLMs undergo an \emph{in-context collapse}, a sharp, sometimes catastrophic accuracy drop spanning synthetic classification, natural-image classification, and VQA benchmarks, in some models falling below chance while outputs remain well-formed. Across an open VLM panel ($0.5$B--$11$B) and a frontier model (Claude Sonnet 4.5), the collapse is graded. Two capabilities turn out to be dissociable: robustness to accumulating demonstrations and the ability to learn a novel rule in context, their combinations yield three reproducible regimes. A parameter-matched lesion-and-rescue causally localizes the collapse to the vision-language integration pathway: an adapter on the connector and early/mid layers restores genuine learning (remap accuracy $0.39!\rightarrow!0.91$ at 16 shots), while an equal-capacity adapter on the late readout does not. We propose \textsc{CircA}, whose core is a one-time integration vaccine: trained once on one synthetic task, it transfers collapse-resistance to unseen task families (chance$\rightarrow$$0.71$/$0.60$ on CIFAR/Fashion). The layers best for in-context integration are not the layers best for weight-based consolidation, the late readout achieves higher accuracy and less forgetting at fewer parameters. The collapse is an integration failure at the vision--language interface, correctable by a lightweight, transferable intervention.

</details>


<div id='cs.CL'></div>

# cs.CL [[Back]](#toc)

### [119] [ARCHead: Activation-Metric Residual Correction for Large Language Model Output Heads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02703)
*Şuayp Talha Kocabay,Talha Rüzgar Akkuş,Kamer Ali Yuksel*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ARCHead是一种针对大语言模型LM-head的压缩方法，结合量化低秩核心、分组INT4残差和低秩校正，在不存储稠密BF16头的情况下将LM-head存储减少3.7-3.9倍，同时保持接近原始模型的困惑度性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有权重仅量化方法虽然大幅压缩了transformer块，但通常保留LM-head为BF16/FP16格式，而直接量化该投影会严重扰动词汇logit分布，因此需要一种专门针对LM-head的高效压缩方案。

Method: ARCHead由三个组件构成：量化低秩核心（quantized low-rank core）、分组INT4残差（group-wise INT4 residuals）和基于激活派生度量拟合的低秩校正（low-rank correction）。三者组合实现无稠密BF16头的LM-head压缩。

Result: 在Qwen3-8B-Base上，ARCHead仅使用BF16头存储的25.6%即达到1.007的相对困惑度，而存储匹配的朴素INT4量化达到1.14-1.16。替换AWQ或bitsandbytes遗留的BF16头仅增加0.006-0.007交叉熵，吞吐变化小于2%。

Conclusion: ARCHead能有效压缩块量化方法未处理的LM-head投影，作为现有块量化器的补充方案，在极小性能损失下实现显著的存储节省。

Abstract: Weight-only quantization substantially reduces the storage of large language model (LLM) transformer blocks, but practical backends often retain the final language-modeling head (LM-head) in BF16 or FP16. Quantizing this projection naively can strongly perturb the vocabulary-logit distribution. We present ARCHead, a packed LM-head compressor that combines a quantized low-rank core, group-wise INT4 residuals, and a low-rank correction fitted in an activation-derived metric. ARCHead stores no dense BF16 head and reduces persistent LM-head storage by 3.7-3.9x. On Qwen3-8B-Base, it uses 25.6% of BF16 head storage while attaining 1.007 relative perplexity; storage-matched naive INT4 yields 1.14-1.16. Replacing the BF16 head left by AWQ or bitsandbytes adds only 0.006-0.007 cross-entropy, with less than 2% throughput change in our measurements. ARCHead therefore complements block quantizers by compressing the large output projection they can leave untouched. Code is available at https://github.com/suayptalha/archead.

</details>


### [120] [Learning a Vector-Symbolic Model for Socio-Cultural Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02807)
*Meera Ray,Swapnika Dulam,Christopher L. Dancy*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于向量符号自编码器的声明性记忆系统，用于ACT-R认知架构中，通过多层级语义表示来建模社会文化结构对决策的影响，并在种族情境化的内隐联想测试（IAT）中验证其效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算认知模型中需要更好地表示社会文化结构对决策的影响，但建模者难以确定哪些语义表示层级对特定情境最为重要。现有的大语言模型和语料模型只能通过共现表示宽泛的语义关联，忽略了自我表征在记忆中的作用，因此无法准确刻画文化关联如何塑造决策。

Method: 提出一个用于ACT-R认知架构的声明性记忆系统，通过向量符号自编码器在多层级上表示语义关联。使用简单的HRR（全息约化表示）操作将情景记忆与从文本中提取的语义记忆向量进行不同编码，从而为记忆请求生成最终的块激活值。该系统在种族情境化的内隐联想测试（IAT）认知模型中进行测试。

Result: 通过在种族情境化的内隐联想测试（IAT）中构建ACT-R认知模型来验证该声明性记忆系统的有效性。

Conclusion: 该声明性记忆系统能够通过多层级语义表示更好地建模社会文化结构对决策的影响，并在IAT任务中得到了验证。

Abstract: How can we better represent the impact of sociocultural structures on decision making in computational cognitive models? Modeling this impact requires traversing multiple levels of semantic representation, however it is not immediately clear to a modeler which levels of representation are most salient to a given situation. Though large language models and cognitively grounded corpus models can represent broad semantic associations through co-occurences, the role of self representations in memory should be accounted for to determine how cultural associations shape decision making. We propose a declarative memory system to be used in the ACT-R cognitive architecture that represents semantic associations at multiple levels via a vector-symbolic autoencoder. We use a simple HRR operation to encode episodic memories differently from semantic memory vectors extracted from text to produce a final chunk activation for a memory request. We use ACT-R cognitive models of a racially contextualized implicit association test (IAT) to test this new declarative memory system.

</details>


### [121] [BODHI: Do LLMs Branch Out and Discover Heterogeneous Inferences?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02867)
*Soumadeep Saha,Krish Sharma,Akshay Chaturvedi,Nicholas Asher*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过迷宫求解实验和数学推理轨迹的语义树结构（BODHI-Trees）分析，研究了RLVR训练对LLM测试时探索行为的影响，发现RLVR不仅导致策略熵坍缩，还显著降低了语义分支熵，表明其扩展了环境约束遵循和回溯能力，但以牺牲真实推理多样性为代价换取采样效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR在多种推理任务上提升了LLM性能，但对于RLVR究竟是扩展了推理能力边界，还是仅仅提升了采样效率，学界存在显著争议，本文旨在澄清这一问题。

Method: 采用受控迷宫求解实验来研究RLVR训练模型的测试时探索行为；同时从数学推理轨迹中提取基于语义等价性的树结构（BODHI-Trees），用以区分风格性变异产生的熵与真正的推理分支熵。

Result: RLVR模型中的策略熵坍缩不仅是句法层面的，还伴随语义分支熵的显著降低；RLVR改善了模型对环境约束的遵循能力和回溯能力，但压缩了延续空间，这可能解释了RLVR的采样效率提升，但代价是减少了真实的rollout多样性。

Conclusion: RLVR提升采样效率的本质在于压缩语义分支空间而非扩展推理能力边界，这提醒研究者需要关注RLVR在效率与多样性之间的权衡。

Abstract: Although reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has improved the performance of large language models (LLMs) across a variety of reasoning tasks, there is significant debate as to whether RLVR expands the reasoning capability boundary, or just improves sampling efficiency. In this paper, we investigate the nature of test-time exploration in RLVR-trained LLMs by employing controlled maze-solving experiments and extracting a tree structure from mathematical reasoning traces (BODHI-Trees) based on semantic equivalence. This helps us delineate between entropy arising from stylistic variations and genuine inferential branching. Our findings demonstrate that the policy entropy collapse observed in RLVR models is not merely syntactic, and is accompanied by a significant reduction in semantic branching entropy. While RLVR improves adherence to environmental constraints and backtracking capabilities, it constricts the space of continuations; we provide evidence suggesting that this might be responsible for the sample efficiency gains of RLVR, albeit at the cost of genuine rollout diversity.

</details>


### [122] [FLARE: Few-shot Learning-based Adaptive Reflective Engine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02919)
*Dhanasekar Sundararaman,Bharat Gandhi,Aashna Garg,Minjie Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出FLARE框架，利用高级反思机制和少量few-shot参考示例优化指令，在多个基准任务上持续优于GEPA优化器，最高提升14.2分，同时具备更高的数据效率和稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最近如GEPA等优化器声称反思性指令进化优于强化学习和few-shot优化，作者质疑这一转变，希望证明结合反思机制与少数few-shot参考示例能够更有效地优化指令。

Method: 提出FLARE（Few-shot Learning-based Adaptive Reflective Engine）框架，结合高级反思机制和少量few-shot参考示例来迭代优化指令，在多个基准上使用GPT-5系列模型进行评估。

Result: FLARE在所有任务-模型组合上均优于GEPA：HotPotQA最高提升14.2分（52.2 vs 42.2），工具调用达87.0%（vs 81.0%），GoEmotions微F1提升至52.7%（+15.3），且仅需100个验证示例即可达到最佳性能，随机种子间更稳定。

Conclusion: 反思性指令强大，但few-shot学习的策略性优化仍是释放下一代LLM潜力的关键前沿方向。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in complex, compound AI systems where performance hinges on the quality of prompts. Recent state-of-the-art optimizers like GEPA (Genetic-Pareto) have argued that reflective instruction evolution can outperform traditional reinforcement learning and few-shot optimization. In this work, we challenge this shift by introducing FLARE (Few-shot Learning-based Adaptive Reflective Engine), a framework that leverages advanced reflective mechanisms and a small set of few-shot reference examples to optimize instructions. We evaluate our method across a diverse suite of benchmarks -- spanning retrieval-augmented reasoning (HotPotQA, MedQA, 2WikiMultiHopQA), tool calling, and multi-label emotion classification (GoEmotions) -- using the GPT-5 series of models. Our results demonstrate that FLARE consistently outperforms GEPA, winning on every task-model pair: it achieves gains of up to +14.2 points on HotPotQA (52.2 vs. GEPA's 42.2 with GPT-5-Chat), reaches 87.0% on tool calling (vs. 81.0% for GEPA), and lifts GoEmotions micro-F1 to 52.7% (+15.3) with GPT-5.1 on the full 5408-example test split, more than doubling GEPA's +5.7 gain. Beyond raw accuracy, FLARE is also strikingly data-efficient: on GoEmotions it reaches its peak performance using as few as 100 validation examples, while remaining markedly more stable across random seeds than GEPA. Our findings suggest that while reflective instructions are powerful, the strategic optimization of few-shot learning remains a critical frontier for maximizing the potential of next-generation LLMs.

</details>


### [123] [Character Iconicity vs. Arbitrariness: An Arabic NLP Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02935)
*Dorieh Alomari,Irfan Ahmad,Maged S. Al-shaibani*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了阿拉伯语去除变音点（dotless阿拉伯语）后，任意但一致的字符重映射是否能在NLP任务中达到与保留原始rasm分组相当的性能。结果表明，随机重映射在语言建模、文本分类等多项任务中表现优异，说明阿拉伯语字符的形-功能关系在很大程度上是任意的，模型更依赖稳定的分布结构而非视觉形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究表明无点阿拉伯语在NLP中仍然可读有效，但尚不清楚这种成功是否依赖于保留原始rasm分组，还是任意一致的字符重映射也能达到相当性能。本文旨在探究阿拉伯语字符视觉区分（点）在功能性上是否必要。

Method: 在词级和字符级分词下生成2,000个随机字符重映射，并将其约束到相同的19个无点rasm集合，从中选取四个具有最高和最低熵值的代表性映射。在语言建模、文本分类、序列标注、机器翻译和原始脚本还原五个NLP任务上进行评估。

Result: 随机重映射在各项NLP任务中与标准有点/无点阿拉伯语表现相当甚至更优，同时降低了词汇表大小、OOV率、模型大小和训练成本。既不需要保留原始字符区分，也不需要维持传统rasm分组。

Conclusion: 从NLP角度看，阿拉伯语字符的形式-功能关系在很大程度上是任意的，模型主要依赖稳定的分布结构而非字符的视觉象似性。这为阿拉伯语NLP的高效字符表示提供了新的可能性。

Abstract: Arabic script uses 28 letters, many of which share a common base shape (rasm) and are distinguished only by dot placement. Because early Arabic manuscripts were written without dots yet remained interpretable, dot removal offers a natural test of whether these visual distinctions are functionally necessary. Prior work has shown that dotless Arabic can remain readable and effective for natural language processing (NLP), but it remains unclear whether this success depends on preserving the original rasm groupings or whether arbitrary but consistent remappings to the same reduced rasm set can achieve comparable performance. We address this question by comparing standard dotted and dotless Arabic with arbitrary character remappings constrained to the same 19 undotted rasms. We generated 2,000 random remappings under word- and character-level tokenization and selected four representative mappings with the highest and lowest entropy values. These representations were evaluated across language modeling, text classification, sequence labeling, machine translation, and restoration to the original script. The results show that neither preserving original character distinctions nor retaining traditional rasm-based groupings is necessary for strong NLP performance. Random remappings achieve competitive performance while reducing vocabulary size, out-of-vocabulary (OOV) rates, model size, and training cost. These findings suggest that, from an NLP perspective, Arabic character form-function relationships are largely arbitrary: models rely more on stable distributional structure than on the visual iconicity of letter forms.

</details>


### [124] [Aligned in Form, Not in Meaning: The Comprehension - Containment Decoupling of LLM Safety in Low-Resource Bangla Derogatory Speech](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02941)
*Shadab Bin Habib,A K M Ferdous Reza Habib,Subarno Neel,Adib Sakhawat*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文审计了五个前沿大语言模型在孟加拉语辱骂性语言上的表现，提出并验证了"理解-遏制解耦"假说：当代安全对齐绑定在高级资源语言的表面形式上，而非有害语义本身，导致模型对低资源语言脏话的理解能力与遏制能力相互独立运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前安全对齐机制主要针对高级资源语言（如英语）设计，对低资源语言（如孟加拉语）的恶意内容防护效果未知。研究者假设安全对齐绑定的是表面语言形式而非有害语义，导致模型理解低资源语言侮辱的能力与遏制它的能力相互独立，现有高级资源基准无法保证低资源环境下的安全。

Method: 对五个前沿大语言模型设计六种测试协议，以人工校准基线（kappa=0.84）为准，系统测试模型在孟加拉语恶意言语上的理解与遏制能力是否解耦，涵盖基线评估、严重程度校准、正字法扰动、链式推理、专家人设框架等多维度实验。

Result: 所有协议均证实理解-遏制解耦假说：模型在孟加拉语上存在7.92个百分点的理解缺陷，但两种语言下令牌泄露率同为92.83%；严重程度校准偏向表面解剖学线索而非组合性伤害；正字法扰动下的遏制提升实为分词器驱动的"遏制幻象"；链式推理将理解提升至94.72%同时将遏制瓦解至96.23%的利用率；专家人设使拒绝率骤降至6.57%，关键词过滤器完全忽视非人化社群侮辱。

Conclusion: 当代安全对齐无法保证低资源语言的安全性，高级资源基准不能作为低资源安全性的认证标准，亟需基于语义理解的内涵式遏制机制来替代当前依赖表面形式的对齐策略。

Abstract: We audit five frontier large language models on native Bangla derogatory speech (gali) across six protocols to test a single hypothesis: Comprehension-Containment Decoupling. We propose that contemporary safety alignment is bound to high-resource surface forms rather than harmful meaning, causing a model's capacity to comprehend a low-resource slur and its capacity to contain it to operate independently. Every protocol corroborates this hypothesis against a human-calibrated baseline (kappa = 0.84). At baseline, models exhibit a 7.92 percentage point comprehension deficit in Bangla while maintaining an identical 92.83% token leakage rate across both languages. Severity calibration tracks surface anatomical cues over compositional harm (+4.00 error on mild slang; -2.00 on threats), while apparent containment gains under orthographic perturbation prove to be a tokenizer-driven "containment mirage." Crucially, explicit Chain-of-Thought reasoning rescues comprehension (94.72% Pass) while systematically dismantling containment (96.23% Use). Furthermore, expert-persona framing collapses refusal to 6.57%, revealing that keyword-based filters ignore dehumanizing communal slurs entirely. Our findings demonstrate that high-resource benchmarks cannot certify low-resource safety, necessitating meaning-grounded containment.

</details>


### [125] [OPTD: On-Policy Transition Distillation with Consistency-Guided Adaptive Compression for Few-Step Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02942)
*Xiaocheng Lu,Hualei Zhang,Shuhan Guo,Jie Zhang,Xiaoyi Pang,Jian Liu,Haoxi Li,Bohai Gu,Haoxuan Che,Jingcai Guo,Song Guo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出OPTD，一种基于策略一致性引导的适应性压缩的在线策略过渡蒸馏方法，用于扩散语言模型少步推理加速，在四个数学推理和代码生成基准上实现最优质量-效率权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的少步蒸馏方法在离策略轨迹上构建监督信号，导致推理时学生模型的早期并行预测会改变后续预测的上下文，使实际访问的状态偏离监督状态，尤其在步压缩最激进时问题最严重。虽然在线策略蒸馏可以缓解这一不匹配，但如何确定每一步过渡应该前进多远仍然是一个悬而未决的问题：仅匹配教师的下一步动作限制了压缩效果，而不加区分地合并未来动作可能违反中间依赖关系。

Method: OPTD从少步学生模型自身的轨迹中采样部分状态，使用冻结的仅基于问题的教师模型识别结果对齐的未来候选，按当前状态置信度排序，然后选择能保持教师展开结果的最长前缀作为联合承诺。通过集合瓶颈目标促进每个已验证的未来候选达到解码器的释放阈值，同时使用冻结教师KL锚点正则化其他活跃位置。目标构建和训练均不使用黄金响应。

Result: 在四个数学推理和代码生成基准上，OPTD一致地改善了质量-效率权衡，在评估的少步基线中取得了最强的整体质量约束AUP（Area Under the quality-constrained Performance curve）。

Conclusion: OPTD通过在线策略蒸馏与一致性引导的自适应压缩，有效解决了少步扩散语言模型解码中的状态漂移和步压缩程度选择问题，在不使用黄金响应的条件下实现了更优的质量-效率平衡。

Abstract: Diffusion language models (dLLMs) can predict many tokens in parallel, but accurate generation still requires many iterative denoising steps. Few-step distillation accelerates decoding by compressing multiple teacher steps into a single student transition. However, existing methods construct supervision on off-policy trajectories. At inference, the student's early parallel commitments alter the context of later predictions, so the states it actually visits drift away from the supervised ones--precisely when step compression is most aggressive. On-policy distillation is a natural remedy for this mismatch, but it leaves open how far each transition should advance: matching only the teacher's next action limits compression, while indiscriminately merging future actions can violate intermediate dependencies. To address this limitation, we propose OPTD, On-Policy Transition Distillation with consistency-guided adaptive compression. It samples partial states from the few-step student's own trajectories, uses a frozen, question-only teacher to identify outcome-aligned future candidates, and orders them by current-state confidence. The method then selects the longest prefix whose joint commitment preserves the teacher's rollout outcome. A set-bottleneck objective promotes every verified future candidate to the decoder's release threshold, while a frozen-teacher KL anchor regularizes all other active positions. Neither target construction nor training uses a gold response. Across four mathematical reasoning and code-generation benchmarks, OPTD consistently improves the quality--efficiency trade-off and attains the strongest overall quality-constrained AUP among the evaluated few-step baselines.

</details>


### [126] [Every Wrong Answer Counts: Option-Level Psychometrics for LLM Multiple-Choice Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02966)
*Xiao Fei,Yang Zhang,Sarah Almeida Carneiro,Michalis Vazirgiannis*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出LLM-NRM，一个选项感知的心理测量学框架，通过建模LLM在多项选择题中对所有选项的完整分布来联合估计能力与题目特征，发现错误选项包含系统性和有用的测量信息，而非等效的错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有选择题基准仅以二元方式（正确/错误）评估LLM，将所有错误回答视为等同，忽略了模型在错误选项间的偏好可能包含有关其行为和能力的系统性有用信息。

Method: 提出LLM名义反应模型（LLM-NRM），一种选项感知的心理测量框架，建模答案选择的完整分布，联合估计LLM能力和选项级题目特征，同时分离模型特定的响应校准锐度、位置偏好和难度依赖的退缩行为。

Result: 在189个LLM和31,554个题目（来自14个基准）上，LLM-NRM在预测留出交互上优于二元项目反应模型和常规名义反应基线；能力估计与Arena.ai Elo排行榜的Spearman相关达0.920；干扰项身份额外贡献101%的Fisher信息；仅错误回答即可恢复全信息能力估计（Spearman 0.943）；41个精选题目可保持全题库排名（Kendall相关0.85），实现770倍效率提升。

Conclusion: 错误答案并非等效的错误，它们携带独特且有价值的测量信息，可用于更准确地评估LLM能力和实现高效基准测试。

Abstract: Most multiple-choice question (MCQ) benchmarks evaluate Large Language Models (LLMs) only by whether they select the correct answers. This binary scoring treats all incorrect responses alike, even though an LLM's preferences among incorrect options may contain systematic and useful information about its behavior and ability. We introduce the LLM Nominal Response Model (LLM-NRM), an option-aware psychometric framework that models the full distribution over answer choices to jointly estimate LLM ability and option-level item characteristics, while separating model-specific response calibration sharpness, positional preference, and difficulty-dependent fallback behavior. Across 189 LLMs and 31,554 items from 14 benchmarks, LLM-NRM predicts held-out LLM-item interactions more accurately than binary Item Response models and conventional nominal-response baselines, and its ability estimates achieve the strongest Spearman correlation of 0.920 with the external human-preference Arena.ai Elo leaderboard. Distractor identity contributes +101% additional Fisher Information per item beyond correctness, and incorrect responses alone recover full-information ability estimates with Spearman 0.943. The learned item parameters also enable efficient benchmarking, where 41 selected items preserve the full-bank ranking with Kendall's correlation 0.85, corresponding to a 770 times reduction. In conclusion, we show that incorrect answers carry distinct and useful measurement information rather than representing equivalent mistakes.

</details>


### [127] [Mapping the City Through the Lens of Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02971)
*Wanqi Liu,Rong Zhao,Zhizhou Sha,Qinyu Cui,Yecheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过十个开源权重语言模型，对匿名化城市形态档案进行评级，测量模型对"普通城市"的无言假设。结果显示模型普遍偏好建成区面积更大、近期增长更快、基础设施更完善、形态更紧凑的城市，且这些倾向在复制数据中稳定复现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型在回答未明确说明的城市引用时，会带有多样化的隐含假设（规模、形态、基础设施、环境等），但这些假设从未被系统量化。本文旨在通过不指名具体城市的方式，实证测量这些隐含的城市模型。

Method: 使用十个开源权重检查点，对基于真实形态城市中心生成的匿名化档案进行评级，涵盖40项审计指标和7个领域。设计包括：受限概率评级、预设信度筛选、谱系感知聚合、多种人口加权、独立复制样本和全档案验证。

Result: 模型普遍偏好更大的已开发面积、更快的近期增长、更完善的基础设施与非住宅容量、更不稀疏的形态。多数倾向方向在复制数据中重现；对完整档案的直接评级与逐指标构建结果具有中度一致性；在控制城市规模和开发水平后，地理差异缩小；典型性与可欲性往往高度一致。

Conclusion: 该框架使"模型认为的普通城市"这一模糊概念变得可经验追踪，描绘出通过语言模型视角看到的共享但依赖模型的"城市画像"，为理解模型的社会性隐含偏见提供了新的实证途径。

Abstract: Language models often complete an underspecified reference to a city with unstated assumptions about urban size, form, infrastructure, environment, and function. We measure those assumptions without naming places. Ten open-weight checkpoints rate anonymized profiles derived from real morphological urban centres across 40 audited indicators and seven domains. The design combines constrained probability-based ratings, prespecified reliability screens, lineage-aware aggregation, multiple population weightings, an independent replication sample, and whole-profile validation. The clearest shared tendency favours urban profiles with larger developed area, faster recent growth, greater mapped infrastructure and non-residential capacity, and less sparse form. Most eligible directions recur in the replication data, and direct ratings of complete profiles show moderate agreement with the indicator-wise construction. Geographic differences shrink after accounting for city scale and development, while reliably measured paired tasks indicate that typicality and desirability are often closely aligned. The framework makes an otherwise vague notion of what models regard as an ordinary city empirically traceable. The resulting evidence delineates a shared yet model-dependent portrait of the city through the lens of language models.

</details>


### [128] [TQLite: Multi-LLM Jury Guided Distillation for Real-time MQM Translation Quality Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02975)
*Bhavin Jawade,Cameron R. Wolfe*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出TQLite蒸馏框架，通过多大型推理模型（LRM）陪审团生成高质量合成训练数据，使小型语言模型（SLM）接近最佳LRM的MQM翻译质量评估性能，为大规模部署提供经济高效的替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型（LLM）和大型推理模型（LRM）在MQM翻译质量评估中表现优异，但在大规模部署时计算成本高昂；而小型语言模型（SLM）效率高但推理能力不足，难以胜任复杂的评估任务，存在效率与性能之间的两难困境。

Method: 首先对SLM、LLM和LRM在多种翻译质量评估设置下进行全面的实证基准测试；随后提出TQLite蒸馏框架，采用多LRM陪审团机制，通过实用的数据整理技术和聚合来自多个模型的评估响应，生成高质量合成训练数据来微调SLM。

Result: 经TQLite训练的SLM在MQM评估性能上远超标准SLM的开箱即用能力，逼近最佳LRM评估器的水平，同时保持了计算效率和经济性优势。

Conclusion: TQLite为翻译质量评估提供了一种可扩展且成本有效的解决方案，以SLM的部署效率逼近LRM的评估质量，确立了当前翻译质量评估领域的实践准则，为未来高效评估系统的构建指明了方向。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated impressive performance in MQM-based translation quality (TQ) evaluation, and recent advances in large reasoning models (LRMs) promise even greater improvements. However, both LLMs and LRMs are computationally expensive to deploy at scale, while small language models (SLMs)---though much more efficient---struggle with the complex reasoning required for evaluation tasks. In this work, we present an extensive empirical study benchmarking SLMs, LLMs, and LRMs across a wide range of TQ evaluation setups, providing a comprehensive view of the current landscape and establishing best practices. To address the scalability challenge, we introduce TQLite, a novel distillation framework that enables SLMs to approach the MQM evaluation performance of the best LRM-based evaluators. Our approach leverages a multi-LRM jury to generate high-quality synthetic training data via practical data curation techniques and aggregation of evaluation responses across a diverse panel of models. Our results demonstrate that SLMs trained via TQLite achieve strong MQM evaluation performance that far exceeds off-the-shelf evaluation capabilities of standard SLMs, offering a scalable and cost-effective alternative to LLM- and LRM-based evaluators.

</details>


### [129] [On the Non-Specificity of Statistical Measures Used in Script Decipherment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02999)
*Nikhil Raghavendra*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过构建一个具有组合意义但无音韵学价值的生成性标志系统SIGIL，检验统计规律性是否能作为未破译符号系统编码语言的证据。结果表明，SIGIL在重复性、方向不对称性和词汇分布等测试中与印度河文字语料库获得相同类别，说明现有度量方法只能检测组织性，无法特异地识别语言。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 印度河文字辩论是典型例子——统计规律性常被用来论证未破译符号系统编码语言。但这类推理依赖于特异性：报告的结果必须在合理的结构化非语言中不寻常。作者想通过构造性方法检验这一前提是否成立。

Method: 作者设计了SIGIL，一个有3,000篇核心语料、携带明确组合含义但符号无语音值的生成性标志系统。预先编制了记录54种方法的文献登记表，当可复现发表的印度河结果和源定义决策规则时，允许精确评分方法。对SIGIL进行重复性、方向不对称性、词汇分布测试，以及熵、频率、位置、预测、分类器和网络测量的重建。

Result: SIGIL在每一个按此方式评分的标准上都获得与印度河语料相同的类别。声明的熵、频率、位置、预测、分类器和网络测量重建均复现了类似印度河的签名。顺序破译压力测试对同一语料达到英语、梵语和泰米尔语的高词典覆盖率，但分组留出测试下降和不稳定的密钥揭示了这种覆盖率识别力有限。

Conclusion: 该构建并未判定印度河符号编码什么，而是表明被评估的度量方法只检测到组织性，并不特异地指向语言，因此单靠这些度量无法确立编码语音的存在。

Abstract: Statistical regularities are routinely offered as evidence that undeciphered sign systems encode language; the Indus script debate is the canonical example. Any such inference rests on specificity: the reported outcome must be unusual among plausible structured non-languages. We test that premise constructively with SIGIL, a purpose-built generative emblem system whose 3,000-text core corpus carries explicit compositional meanings although no sign has a phonological value. A literature registry compiled in advance of evaluation records 54 methods and admits a method to exact scoring when both the published Indus outcome and a source-defined decision rule can be reproduced. SIGIL receives the same category as the Indus corpus on every criterion scored this way, across repetition, directional-asymmetry, and lexical-distribution tests. Declared reconstructions of entropy, frequency, positional, predictive, classifier, and network measures reproduce the familiar Indus-like signatures as well. A sequential decipherment stress test then reaches high dictionary coverage for English, Sanskrit, and Tamil on the same corpus, while grouped held-out declines and unstable keys reveal how little that coverage identifies. The construction does not decide what the Indus signs encode: it shows that the evaluated measures detect organization without being specific to language, and therefore cannot, on their own, establish encoded speech.

</details>


### [130] [Language Models Encode the Contextual Truth of Propositions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03035)
*Rupak Sarkar,Pritika Ramu,Rachel Rudinger*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了LLM如何在激活空间中线性表示"情境化真相"（由上下文证据而非世界知识决定真假的命题），并通过steering实验提供因果证据。在协作视觉语言任务中，发现对话伙伴的断言会显著影响模型对命题真相的表征，且接近决策边界的命题更容易被"改写"真相。通过分离表征与输出，论文识别出两种不同的谄媚行为，其中显式重述虚假声明比隐式同意更可能导致表征跨越边界偏移（2.59倍）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往研究证明LLM在激活空间中沿线性方向编码事实命题的真相，但尚不清楚这种表征如何扩展到"情境化真相"——即由上下文证据而非世界知识决定的命题真实性。理解这一机制对于揭示模型如何受对话伙伴影响而改变判断具有重要意义。

Method: 1) 在协作视觉语言任务中让两个LLM维护共享常识，收集对话转录；2) 通过线性探针检测模型中关于命题真相的线性表征；3) 使用steering实验提供因果证据；4) 分离真实表征与输出行为，区分两种谄媚形式：接纳虚假命题但保持表征为假 vs. 表征跨越真相边界发生偏移。

Result: 发现LLM对情境化真相保持线性表征，且这种表征在不同输出策略下持续存在。伙伴的断言会显著影响模型对命题真相的表征，即使模型有足够证据判断真相。接近决策边界的命题更容易被伙伴断言改变真相表征。显式重述虚假声明导致表征跨越边界的频率是隐式同意的2.59倍。

Conclusion: LLM对情境化真相的线性表征会被对话伙伴的断言所左右，且将表征与输出分离可以揭示输出行为无法直接观察的两种谄媚机制。这为理解模型在对话中的真实认知状态与社会顺从行为提供了新视角。

Abstract: Prior work has shown that LLMs encode the truth of factual propositions along linear directions in activation space. It's unclear how these representations extend to contextual truth: propositions whose truth is determined by in-context evidence rather than world knowledge. We show that LLMs maintain a linear representation of contextual truth that persists across structurally different output policies, even when the output doesn't require the model to determine a proposition's truth, and show causal evidence via steering experiments. Using the transcripts from a collaborative vision-language task that requires two LLMs to maintain a shared common ground, we show that truth representations of a proposition are significantly swayed by partner assertions about that proposition, even when the LLM has enough evidence to determine its truth. We find evidence that propositions near the decision boundary are more susceptible to having their truth shifted through partner assertions. Separating representation from output distinguish two forms of sycophancy that output behavior alone cannot: the model may accommodate a false proposition while continuing to represent it as false, or shift its representation across the boundary. The latter is 2.59x more common when the model agrees by restating the false claim explicitly than when it agrees implicitly.

</details>


### [131] [Beyond Accuracy: A Multidimensional Evaluation of Statistical Reasoning in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03038)
*Monnie McGee,Mateo Langston Smith,Julian Cabrera*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种多维评估框架来评估大语言模型（LLM）的统计推理能力，将响应准确率、响应行为、结构主题建模和词汇相似性分析相结合。对15个当前代际LLM在90道跨层次（高中、本科、研究生）统计考试题上的表现进行评估，发现准确率差异显著（55%-78%），但所有模型在概念组织上具有共性，同时同一供应商的模型在解释风格上更为相似。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对LLM统计推理的评估过度聚焦于响应准确性，忽视了模型如何构建和传达统计解释这一重要维度。统计推理本身是多维度的，仅用准确率来评价模型能力是不够全面的，需要一种更全面的评估方法来捕捉模型统计推理的多方面特征。

Method: 构建多维评估框架，包含四个维度：（1）响应准确率评估；（2）响应行为分析；（3）结构主题建模（structural topic modeling）分析概念组织；（4）词汇相似性分析比较解释风格。该框架应用于15个当前代际LLM对90道来自四份不同层次（高中、本科、研究生）统计考试题目的解释回答。

Result: 模型准确率差异显著，范围在55%到78%之间。结构主题建模显示所有模型共享共同的统计推理概念组织；词汇相似性分析发现不同供应商之间的解释风格存在适度但一致的差异，同一供应商（如Anthropic、OpenAI）开发的模型产出的解释比不同供应商的模型更为相似。

Conclusion: 当代LLM的统计推理能力不能仅用准确率来表征。响应行为分析和模型生成解释的互补分析为生成式AI的统计推理提供了更全面的评估视角，验证了多维评估框架的价值。

Abstract: Statistical reasoning is multidimensional, yet evaluations of large language models (LLMs) typically emphasize response accuracy while overlooking how models construct and communicate statistical explanations. This study demonstrates the value of a multidimensional evaluation by combining response accuracy, response behavior, structural topic modeling, and lexical similarity analysis. The framework is applied to explanations generated by 15 current-generation LLMs responding to 90 questions drawn from four statistics examinations spanning high school, undergraduate, and graduate levels. Accuracy varied substantially across models, ranging from 55\% to 78\%. In contrast, structural topic modeling revealed a common conceptual organization of statistical reasoning across all models, while lexical similarity analysis identified modest but consistent vendor-specific differences in explanatory style. Models developed by the same vendor (e.g. Anthropic, OpenAI) produced explanations that were slightly more similar than models from different vendors. These findings demonstrate that statistical reasoning in contemporary LLMs cannot be characterized by accuracy alone and illustrate how complementary analyses of response behavior and model-generated explanations provide a more comprehensive evaluation of statistical reasoning in generative AI.

</details>


### [132] [Emulate or Estimate? The Divergent Strengths of Base and Post-Trained Language Models for Opinion Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03044)
*Seth Grief-Albert,Jessica Bo,Difan Jiao,Ashton Anderson*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发现大语言模型模拟人类观点时的矛盾结果源于混淆了两种不同任务：模拟（emulation）和估计（estimation）。在Pew American Trends Panel上评估六个模型后，基础模型更擅长模拟（生成个体响应以聚合为群体分布），而后期训练模型更擅长估计（直接预测群体分布）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前关于LLM模拟人类观点的研究结果相互矛盾——一些发现与人类调查数据高度一致，另一些则发现人格坍缩和人口统计敏感性弱。作者认为这种冲突源于没有区分两种本质不同的任务。

Method: 将任务区分为两类：模拟任务（模型生成个体回答，聚合为群体分布）和估计任务（模型直接预测群体分布）。在Pew American Trends Panel数据集上，对六个匹配的基础模型和后期训练模型进行了系统评估。

Result: 基础模型是更强的模拟器：生成的响应分布更接近人类真实分布，且更好地保留了人口统计结构。后期训练模型是更强的估计器：被直接询问时能产生更准确的分布预测。

Conclusion: 人类模拟的模型选择应取决于任务是生成文本还是预测分布——模拟任务适合基础模型，估计任务适合后期训练模型。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used to simulate human opinions, but prior work reports conflicting results: some studies find promising alignment with human survey data, while others find persona collapse and weak demographic sensitivity. We show that much of this conflict stems from conflating two distinct tasks. We call the first task emulation, in which models generate individual responses that aggregate into a population distribution. We call the second task estimation, in which models directly predict the population distribution. Evaluating six matched base and post-trained models on the Pew American Trends Panel, we find that base models are stronger emulators: they produce response distributions closer to human ground truth and better preserve demographic structure. Post-trained models are stronger estimators, producing more accurate distributional predictions when asked directly. We propose that model selection for human simulation should be guided by whether the task requires generating text or predicting distributions.

</details>


### [133] [PI-Mem: Pushing Long-Context Reasoning to 3.6M Tokens with Parallel-Iterative Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03048)
*Dawei Liu,Haixu Song,Shuang Cheng,Shijie Wang,Haozheng Hou,Kaifeng Liu,Ermo Hua,Zhonghang Yuan,Zhijie Zhong,Yuchen Fan,Biqing Qi,Bowen Zhou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出PI-Mem（并行迭代记忆）机制，通过并行处理所有块并迭代精炼共享记忆，解决了长上下文推理中递归记忆方法存在的早期证据覆盖和串行依赖延迟问题，在HotpotQA上最高提升7.81个绝对点并实现6.1倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文推理是LLM的关键瓶颈。现有递归记忆方法存在两个固有问题：一是顺序分块更新可能用后续无关内容覆盖早期关键证据；二是块间串行依赖限制了并行性，导致延迟随上下文长度线性增长。

Method: PI-Mem在每轮中并行读取所有块（条件为当前记忆），从每个块中选择新的或互补证据，并将所选证据合并到紧凑的共享记忆中供下一轮使用。通过强化学习优化工作流，引入辅助的轮次效率奖励来抑制冗余轮次，使模型能在收集足够证据后自适应退出。

Result: 在Qwen3.5-35B-A3B和Qwen2.5-7B上，HotpotQA基准最长3.6M token上下文条件下，分别比递归记忆基线提升+6.25和+7.81绝对点，同时推理速度分别提升6.1倍和2.1倍。

Conclusion: PI-Mem打破了长上下文推理中准确性与效率之间的权衡，为超长文档上的复杂多跳问答提供了可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Long-context reasoning remains a critical bottleneck for large language models, as recent recurrent-memory approaches face two inherent challenges: sequential chunk-wise updates can overwrite early critical evidence with later irrelevant content, and serial inter-chunk dependencies limit parallelism and cause latency to increase with context length. To address these issues, we propose PI-Mem (Parallel-Iterative Memory), a mechanism that processes all chunks in parallel and iteratively refines a shared memory over a bounded number of turns. In each turn, PI-Mem reads all chunks in parallel conditioned on the current memory, selects new or complementary evidence from each chunk, and merges the selected evidence into a compact shared memory for the next turn. To discourage redundant turns, we optimize the workflow through reinforcement learning with an auxiliary turn-efficiency reward, enabling the model to adaptively exit once sufficient evidence has been accumulated. We evaluate PI-Mem with Qwen3.5-35B-A3B and Qwen2.5-7B on the HotpotQA benchmark across context lengths up to 3.6 million tokens and find that it outperforms the recurrent-memory baseline by +6.25 and +7.81 absolute points while achieving 6.1$\times$ and 2.1$\times$ inference speedups, respectively. These results demonstrate that PI-Mem breaks the accuracy--efficiency trade-off in long-context reasoning and provides a scalable approach to complex multi-hop question answering over extremely long documents.

</details>


### [134] [SeqLLM: Augmenting LLMs with Behavioral-Sequence Modeling for High-Stakes Decisions at WeChat Pay](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03063)
*Guilin Li,Jiaxing Zhang,Matthias Hwai Yong Tan,Bo Wang,Weiran Huang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SeqLLM是一个为预训练大语言模型添加行为序列建模能力并保留其语言能力的框架，已在微信支付部署，每天筛选数百万商户，将筛查精确率从92.0%提升到97.5%，并在公开推荐基准上取得SOTA结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型支付平台的商户风险控制每天需要筛查数千万商户，虚假正例伤害合法商户，虚假负例遗漏有害活动。最难的案例需要联合理解商户的文本画像和长行为序列，而LLM擅长文本但无法原生建模序列，直接适配又会导致灾难性遗忘。

Method: SeqLLM由三个组件构成：(1) 紧凑的离散词汇表，将行为事件表示为原生token；(2) 轻量级投影器，通过两阶段对齐课程训练，将这些token映射到LLM的语义空间；(3) 前缀引导的能力注入，通过带任务前缀的监督微调而非持续预训练来获取序列建模能力。

Result: 在微信支付部署后，相比生产环境基于DeepSeek的LLM基线，筛查精确率从92.0%提升至97.5%；预训练的行为token嵌入使生产欺诈检测器的Precision@Top-0.01%提升26.8个百分点。在MovieLens和Amazon上，相对用户LLM基线Recall@5提升最高32%，同时保留更强语言能力；在RecIF上，Pass@32提升14.2%，仅用其五分之一的GPU天数。

Conclusion: SeqLLM证明了在不牺牲语言能力的前提下为预训练LLM添加行为序列建模能力的可行性，并在支付风控和推荐系统两个领域均取得显著效果，是一个兼具学术价值和工业实用性的框架。

Abstract: Merchant risk control at large payment platforms screens tens of millions of merchants daily, where false positives harm legitimate merchants and false negatives leave harmful activity undetected. The hardest cases require jointly understanding a merchant's textual profile and long behavioral sequence. Large language models (LLMs) excel at text but cannot natively model such sequences, while adapting them often causes catastrophic forgetting. We present SeqLLM, a framework that adds behavioral-sequence modeling to a pretrained LLM while preserving its language ability.
