TL;DR: 本文提出一个模块化的"生成-选择"框架，用于句子预算受限的摘要生成。通过预训练生成器产生多个候选摘要，再经句子级分解和组合选择器在预算约束下平衡相关性、事实性和冗余度，无需重训练生成器即可提升事实一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的抽象式摘要模型在事实一致性、冗余性和长度控制方面仍然存在缺陷，需要一种模型无关的机制来改善摘要的事实基础。

Method: 采用模块化的生成-选择框架：预训练生成器生成多个候选摘要，将其分解为句子级候选，然后通过组合选择器（支持MMR、ILP和DPP风格的log-determinant目标函数）在显式预算约束下平衡相关性、事实性和冗余度来构建最终摘要。

Result: 在CNN/DailyMail、Multi-News、FaithBench和TofuEval等数据集上，该方法在事实性和源文本接地指标上取得一致改进，尤其在多文档摘要任务中效果显著，但参考重叠分数有所下降。人工评估显示感知一致性、相关性、清晰度和简洁性更高，连贯性略有下降。

Conclusion: 将生成与选择解耦提供了一种模型无关的机制来改善事实接地，同时无需重新训练生成器。

Abstract: Abstractive summarization models remain vulnerable to factual inconsistency, redundancy, and weak length control. We propose a modular generation-and-selection framework for sentence-budget-constrained summarization. A pretrained generator produces multiple candidate summaries, which are decomposed into sentence-level candidates. A combinatorial selector then constructs the final summary by balancing relevance, factuality, and redundancy under an explicit budget. The framework supports MMR, ILP, and a DPP-inspired log-determinant objective without retraining the generator. Experiments on CNN/DailyMail, Multi-News, FaithBench, and TofuEval show consistent improvements in factuality and source-grounding metrics, especially for multi-document summarization, at the cost of lower reference-overlap scores. Human evaluation further indicates higher perceived consistency, relevance, clarity, and conciseness, with a small reduction in coherence. These results show that decoupling generation from selection provides a model-agnostic mechanism for improving factual grounding. Code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/bcfs-D05E/.

</details>


### [172] [How Closely Do LLM Reviews Align with Human Peer Review?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03659)
*Abraham Camelo-Guerrero,Jairo Diaz-Rodriguez*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究在300篇ICLR 2026论文（口述/海报/拒稿各100篇）上，对OpenAI GPT-5.4、Google Gemini 3.1 Pro和Anthropic Claude Opus 4.6三大LLM的审稿结果与人类评审及最终录用决定进行了对照分析。结果显示所有模型都能区分录用与拒稿论文，但均无法复现人类对口头报告与海报的细粒度区分；各模型评分模式存在显著差异（Gemini系统性偏高，OpenAI和Claude更接近人工），且LLM与人类在弱点关注重点上不同（LLM更常指出基线比较缺失，人类更关注计算效率）。研究表明宽泛的决策对齐并不等于与人类细粒度判断及审稿优先事项一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对LLM生成科学评审的评估很少在同一受控环境中考察不同模型提供商是否与会议决定和人类审稿优先事项同时对齐，缺乏跨提供商的系统对比。

Method: 选取300篇主题匹配的ICLR 2026投稿（口述/海报/拒稿各占100篇），由三家LLM在移除决策信息后使用相同指令和评分量表对每篇论文进行评审，从三个维度分析：决策类别对齐（宽泛与细粒度）、推荐评分使用差异、识别弱点的主题一致性。

Result: 三家LLM均能区分录用与拒稿论文，但都无法复现人类对口头与海报的区分；Gemini系统性给出更高评分，OpenAI和Claude在拒稿与海报论文上更接近人类但对口头论文更严格；LLM更频繁识别缺失基线比较，人类更常提出计算效率问题。

Conclusion: 宽泛的决策对齐并不意味着模型与人类在细粒度判断和审稿优先事项上一致，LLM审稿在细粒度区分和关注重点上仍与人类存在显著差距。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to generate scientific reviews, yet existing evaluations rarely examine whether different providers align with both conference decisions and human reviewing priorities within the same controlled setting. We compare reviews from OpenAI GPT-5.4, Google Gemini 3.1 Pro Preview, and Anthropic Claude Opus 4.6 with human reviews and final decisions for 300 topic-matched ICLR 2026 submissions, equally divided among oral, poster, and rejected papers. Each model reviewed every paper using identical instructions and rating scales after decision information was removed. Our study contributes a cross-provider analysis of three complementary dimensions: alignment with broad and fine-grained decision categories, differences in recommendation-scale usage, and thematic agreement in identified weaknesses. All three LLMs distinguished accepted from rejected papers, but none reproduced the oral versus poster distinction present in human ratings. Scoring patterns were provider-specific: Gemini assigned systematically higher ratings, while OpenAI and Claude were closer to humans for rejected and poster papers but more critical of oral papers. Human and LLM reviews also differed in emphasis, with LLMs more frequently identifying missing baseline comparisons and humans more often raising computational-efficiency concerns. These results show that broad decision alignment does not imply agreement with finer human judgments or reviewing priorities.

</details>


### [173] [VetScore: Risk-Weighted Fact Verification for Veterinary Long-Form QA with Citations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03675)
*Ivan Kartáč,Jan Tovarys,Mateusz Lango,Ondřej Dušek*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了VetScore，一种用于兽医领域长问答的多步评估方法，通过将输出分解为独立声明、评估其致病风险及对源摘录的忠实度，最终计算风险调整后的总体分数，实现对生成答案可靠性的综合评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高风险领域（如人类和兽医医学）中，引用摘录虽可提高生成输出的可靠性，但并不能保证生成声明与摘录内容完全一致。因此需要一种专门的方法来系统评估生成声明对源摘录的忠实度，并考虑不同声明的潜在危害程度。

Method: VetScore采用多步评估流程：(1)将输出分段并分解为独立声明；(2)根据每个声明的危害潜力打分；(3)评估每个声明对源摘录的忠实度；(4)计算总体风险调整分数。研究者收集了专家标注的元评估数据集，并使用多种评判模型进行验证。

Result: 实验表明VetScore即使使用较小的评判模型也能与兽医专家的评估达到高度相关性，同时在不同维度上提供可解释性。

Conclusion: VetScore为兽医领域长问答提供了一种忠实于来源且可解释的评估方法，在小型模型上也表现优异，具有较高的实用性和部署价值。

Abstract: Citation excerpts can be used to increase the reliability of generated outputs and their faithfulness to cited sources, which is especially important in high-stakes domains such as human and veterinary medicine. However, this does not guarantee that generated claims are faithful to the provided excerpts. We present VetScore, a multi-step evaluation method for veterinary long-form question answering, designed to assess how well are generated claims supported by the provided excerpts, weighing this information by each claim's harm potential. VetScore first segments the output and decomposes it into individual claims, then scores each claim with respect to its harm potential and evaluates its faithfulness to source excerpts, and finally calculates the overall risk-adjusted score. We collect an expert-annotated meta-evaluation dataset, evaluate our approach with a range of judge models, and show that it achieves high correlations with veterinary experts even with small judge models, while offering explainability across multiple dimensions.

</details>


### [174] [Predicting Deep Neural Network Training Outcomes from Early Training Telemetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03709)
*Ranjita Naik,Anh D. Nguyen,Pankaj Kumar Singh*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了能否仅利用单个训练运行自身的前几个epoch遥测数据（每轮损失、训练准确率、梯度信噪比、权重范数增长和激活饱和快照）以及采样的超参数，来预测该运行的最终结果，无需参考其他运行。在23,788个训练运行、六种架构/数据集组合上，仅用前5个epoch的遥测数据，梯度提升树在最终准确率回归上达到R²=0.92-0.99，相对分类上ROC-AUC=0.983-0.998，且单epoch后即可获得有用预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模超参数搜索中，大量计算被浪费在从最初几个epoch就注定失败的配置上。作者希望利用训练早期的遥测数据提前识别这些注定失败的运行，从而节省计算资源。

Method: 使用梯度提升树模型，结合每轮损失、训练准确率、梯度信噪比、权重范数增长、激活饱和快照等早期遥测数据及采样超参数，在23,788个训练运行上评估三个预测任务：最终测试准确率回归、域内相对性能分类、训练动态失败（包括数值发散）检测。采用永久留出的超参数配置集进行验证，并进行配对消融实验分析各遥测类型贡献。

Result: 仅用前5个epoch的遥测数据，最终准确率回归R²=0.92-0.99，相对分类ROC-AUC=0.983-0.998；单个epoch后即可获得有效预测。梯度级和权重级遥测相比仅用损失和准确率曲线有统计上一致的改进，但实际增益因领域而异。相似架构间迁移性强，跨数据集迁移主要受准确率尺度差异限制而非底层关系丢失。

Conclusion: 早期训练遥测数据可为计算资源分配提供实用的决策支持信号，但任何自动化干预仍需人工监督。

Abstract: Large hyperparameter sweeps for deep neural networks spend substantial compute on configurations that are effectively doomed from the first few epochs. We study whether a single training run's own early telemetry - per-epoch loss, training accuracy, gradient signal-to-noise ratio, weight-norm growth, and an activation-saturation snapshot - together with its sampled hyperparameters, can predict that run's eventual outcome without reference to other runs. We evaluate three prediction tasks: final test accuracy, relative performance within a domain, and training-dynamics failure, including numerical divergence. Across 23,788 training runs spanning six architecture/dataset combinations, gradient-boosted trees using only the first five epochs of telemetry achieve R^2 = 0.92-0.99 for final-accuracy regression and ROC-AUC = 0.983-0.998 for relative classification on a permanently held-out set of hyperparameter configurations. Useful prediction is already available after a single epoch. A paired ablation shows that gradient- and weight-level telemetry provides a statistically consistent improvement over loss and accuracy curves alone, although the practical gain varies by domain. Transfer is strong between similar architectures, while cross-dataset transfer is limited mainly by differences in accuracy scale rather than loss of the underlying relationship. These results suggest that early-training telemetry can provide a practical decision-support signal for compute allocation while motivating human oversight for any automated intervention.

</details>


### [175] [Detecting Hallucinations and Recovering Verified Answers in Arabic Islamic Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03720)
*Khaled Ziani*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了参加HalluScoring 2026任务2.1（伊斯兰幻觉检测与真相寻找）的系统。该系统基于微调的google/gemma-4-12B-it模型，对LLM生成的阿拉伯语回答进行幻觉检测，并从六个候选项中选出已验证答案。系统在600条数据上取得Macro-F1 0.928、标签准确率0.935、选项准确率0.895，综合得分0.912。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在回答伊斯兰教相关问题时可能生成难以识别的事实性错误，需要自动化检测LLM输出的幻觉内容并从相似候选中找出正确回答。

Method: 基于微调的google/gemma-4-12B-it模型，使用确定性解码进行推理，对生成输出进行归一化处理以提取幻觉标签和候选选项。

Result: 幻觉检测Macro-F1为0.928，标签准确率0.935，选项选择准确率0.895，综合得分0.912。

Conclusion: 系统在两个任务阶段均表现良好，但从相似候选中区分已验证答案比检测幻觉回答更具挑战性，选项选择准确率相对较低。

Abstract: Large language models can generate fluent responses to Islamic questions while introducing factual errors that are difficult to identify. This paper presents our system for \textsc{HalluScoring 2026} Task 2.1, \textit{Islamic Hallucination Detection and Find the Truth}. The task requires a unified two-step prediction: determining whether an Arabic answer generated by an LLM is hallucinated and selecting the verified answer from six closely related candidate options. We use the Islamic knowledge dataset provided by the shared task, which contains 600 question--answer instances, including 341 hallucinated and 259 non-hallucinated answers. Our system is based on the fine-tuned \texttt{google/gemma-4-12B-it} model and uses deterministic decoding during inference. The generated outputs are normalized to extract the hallucination label and the selected option. The system achieves a Macro-F1 score of 0.928 and a label accuracy of 0.935 for hallucination detection, together with an option accuracy of 0.895 for answer selection. These results yield a combined score of 0.912, demonstrating strong performance across both stages of the task. The lower option-selection accuracy indicates that distinguishing the verified answer from plausible alternatives remains more challenging than detecting hallucinated responses.

</details>


### [176] [GPTKB 2.0: Direct Construction of Disambiguated Knowledge Bases from Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03729)
*Yujia Hu,Tuan-Phong Nguyen,Simon Razniewski*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: GPTKB 2.0提出了一种直接从大语言模型构建消歧知识库的方法论，通过实时消歧实体、关系和类别，成功构建了包含超过100万个消歧实体和3840万条三元组的大规模知识库，这是首个百万级别的LLM原生知识库。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM原生知识库存在实体表示缺失的问题，导致重复条目和概念混淆，影响知识库质量。

Method: 采用on-the-fly（实时）消歧机制，对实体、关系和类别进行动态消歧，并精心设计以兼顾可扩展性和消歧准确性；同时分析关键设计决策并权衡准确性、规模与成本。

Result: 构建了包含超过100万消歧实体和3840万条三元组的物化知识库，是首个百万规模的LLM原生知识库，实现了实体、关系和类别的显式内部规范化。

Conclusion: GPTKB 2.0代表了从传统Wikimedia中心化知识库构建方式的显著转变，为直接从LLM构建大规模消歧知识库提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Automated Knowledge Base Construction (AKBC) is a core NLP task, and recent work proposes generating knowledge bases directly from large language models (LLMs), treating the model itself as the knowledge source. However, LLMs natively possess no representation of entities, leading to duplicate entries as well as conflations. We propose GPTKB 2.0, a methodology for constructing disambiguated KBs directly from LLMs. GPTKB 2.0 incorporates on-the-fly disambiguation of entities, relations and classes, and is meticulously designed to satisfy both scalability and disambiguation accuracy. We analyze the central design decisions and characterize the trade-offs between accuracy, scale, and cost. We execute GPTKB 2.0 at scale, obtaining a materialized KB containing over 1M disambiguated entities and 38.4M triples. This represents the first million-scale LLM-native KB with explicit internal canonicalization of entities, relations, and classes, a significant departure from prior Wikimedia-centric works. GPTKB 2.0 is available at https://gptkb.org/.

</details>


### [177] [MDLMPE: Distribution Aware Positional Encoding for Masked Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03769)
*Tong Ling,Hang Lei,Feng Xiao,Changhui Sun,Jiahang Xie,Hao Liu,Lu Liu,Yanlong Du*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出MDLMPE，一种专为掩码扩散语言模型设计的位置编码方法。MDLMPE首次将token的可见/掩码状态感知引入位置表示，通过二进制序列表示可用性、高斯加权和余弦基投影生成分布感知的位置特征，并调制RoPE相位。实验证明其在LLaDA和DREAM上优于传统位置编码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 掩码扩散语言模型（MDLMs）的并行生成和双向上下文建模机制使其位置上下文与自回归（AR）模型有本质不同。AR解码暴露连续的prefix，而MDLM去噪产生动态、非连续的可见/掩码token配置。传统位置编码（如RoPE）仅捕捉序列顺序和两两距离，无法感知这种不断演化的token可用性结构。

Method: MDLMPE将token可用性表示为二进制序列，应用距离感知的高斯加权，通过余弦基投影获得分布感知的位置特征。这些特征被添加到token嵌入中，并由轻量级MLP映射为角偏移量来调制标准RoPE相位，使位置表示显式感知可见/掩码配置的演化。

Result: 在LLaDA和DREAM上的大量实验表明，MDLMPE在监督微调、预训练、零样本评估和块扩散设置中普遍优于传统位置编码方法。消融实验进一步证明，可用性状态、高斯局部性、光谱基和嵌入注入的完整组合能取得最佳效果。

Conclusion: 演化的token可用性分布是掩码扩散语言模型中有用的位置信号，MDLMPE是该方向首个方法，为掩码扩散模型的位置编码设计提供了新思路。

Abstract: Masked diffusion language models (MDLMs) enable parallel generation and bidirectional context modeling, but their positional context differs fundamentally from that of autoregressive (AR) models. Whereas AR decoding exposes a contiguous prefix, MDLM denoising produces dynamic, non-contiguous configurations of revealed and masked tokens. Conventional positional encodings such as RoPE capture sequence order and pairwise displacement but remain insensitive to this evolving token-availability structure. To address this limitation, we propose MDLMPE, a positional encoding designed specifically for masked diffusion. To the best of our knowledge, MDLMPE is the first method to make positional representations explicitly aware of the changing revealed/masked configuration. It represents token availability as a binary sequence, applies distance-aware Gaussian weighting, and projects the resulting pattern through a cosine basis to obtain distribution-aware positional features. These features are added to token embeddings and mapped by a lightweight MLP to angular offsets that modulate the standard RoPE phases. Extensive experiments on LLaDA and DREAM demonstrate that MDLMPE generally outperforms conventional positional encoding methods across supervised fine-tuning, pretraining, zero-shot evaluation, and block-diffusion settings. Further ablations show that the complete combination of availability state, Gaussian locality, spectral basis, and embedding injection yields the strongest result. These results establish the evolving token-availability distribution as a useful positional signal for masked diffusion language models.

</details>


### [178] [Efficient Knowledge Distillation for LLMs: Offline Top-K Logits and a Fused Chunked KL Loss](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03796)
*Bakbergen Ryskulov,Iker García-Ferrero,David Montero,David Jansen,Ali Hashemi,Jezabel R. Garcia,Antonio Tiene,Román Orús*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对小语言模型知识蒸馏训练昂贵的问题，提出了两项系统级优化：离线KD（缓存教师模型Top-K logits）和融合分块KL损失（不物化完整词表大小的logit张量）。离线KD相比在线蒸馏每迭代快约29%，在单张H200 GPU上吞吐量提升高达41%；分块KL损失将峰值内存降为序列长度的线性关系，支持在单GPU上以4倍上下文长度（32,768 tokens）训练。实验还包含损失设计和序列打包的消融研究，并开源了实现代码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小语言模型因延迟、成本和本地部署限制常是唯一选择，但很少从零训练，通常通过知识蒸馏（KD）从压缩模型恢复。这一恢复步骤在很大程度上决定最终质量，但训练成本高昂，因此需要提高蒸馏训练效率。

Method: 提出两项系统贡献：(1) 离线KD——缓存教师模型Top-K logits一次，学生模型仅对缓存训练，免去教师模型占用内存；(2) 融合分块KL损失——不物化完整词表大小的logit张量，使峰值内存与序列长度线性相关。此外使用仅输出头的玩具基准隔离损失核，验证其内存和迭代率从4K到256K token的扩展性。

Result: 离线KD与在线蒸馏训练损失几乎相同，但每迭代快约29%，在单张H200 GPU上吞吐量提升高达41%。分块KL损失消除了内存尖峰，支持在单GPU上以4倍上下文长度（32,768 tokens）训练。玩具基准确认了内存和迭代率在4K到256K token范围内的扩展性。

Conclusion: 离线KD与分块KL损失的组合使大规模修复和数百次消融实验变得更加经济可行，为小语言模型的蒸馏训练提供了实用的效率优化方案。

Abstract: Small language models are often the only option for deployment under tight latency, cost, and on-premises constraints, but they are rarely trained from scratch: a compressed model is usually recovered through knowledge distillation (KD). This recovery step largely decides the final quality, yet it is expensive. We present a practitioner's study of how to make distillation training efficient, organised around two systems contributions. First, we show that offline KD (caching the teacher's top-$K$ logits once and training the student against the cache) matches online distillation at near-identical training loss while removing the teacher from memory, running about 29\% faster per iteration, and reaching up to 41\% higher throughput on a single H200 GPU. Second, we introduce a \emph{fused, chunked KL loss} that never materialises the full vocabulary-sized logit tensor, making peak memory linear in the sequence length. This removes the memory spike that otherwise caps context length and lets us train at four times the context (32{,}768 tokens) on a single GPU. A separate output-head-only toy benchmark isolates the loss kernel and confirms its memory and iteration-rate scaling from 4K to 256K tokens. Together these make large-scale healing and hundreds of ablations affordable. We also report supporting ablations on loss design and sequence packing. We release our chunked-loss implementation: https://github.com/CompactifAI/Full-Chunked-KL-Loss.

</details>


### [179] [M-GATE: Multilingual Grammar, Accuracy in Translation, and Efficiency Benchmark for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03803)
*Tomáš Burkert,Angelika Peljak-Łapińska,David Zelený*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出M-GATE基准，用于评估多语言模型的真实语言能力（而非仅任务表现），涵盖30种语言、三个任务，发现语言流利度与熟练度可显著分离，且翻译质量与预训练数据占比高度相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多语言基准主要测试模型能否在某种语言中完成任务，混淆了"流利度"（能否用该语言）与"熟练度"（是否真正掌握该语言）。作者希望构建一个能区分这两种能力的评测基准，从而更准确地评估多语言模型的真实语言水平。

Method: 构建M-GATE基准，包含三个任务：（1）基于语言学家手工构造的对抗性句子的语法错误检测；（2）将共享英文源文本经29种目标语言进行往返翻译，由三供应商LLM评审团评分（经专业标注员验证）；（3）补充的分词器效率测量。在50多个模型、80多种配置上进行了评估。

Result: 流利度与熟练度显著分离：翻译能力强的模型在对抗性语法项上接近随机水平，最佳MCC仅为0.36，且错误偏向少报（漏报而非误报）。翻译质量与预训练数据占比密切相关（r=0.86 vs log Common Crawl占比），低资源语言存在严重性能惩罚但正在缩小。推理能力的启用能稳定提升翻译质量，但对错误检测影响较小甚至对某些模型为负。

Conclusion: M-GATE揭示了多语言模型在翻译与语法理解能力上的显著不对称性，表明两者应采用不同的优化配置。基准通过私有测试集和持续更新的公共排行榜来抵抗数据污染，并公开了示例。

Abstract: Multilingual language models are deployed across a hundred or more languages, yet most benchmarks test whether a model can perform a task _in_ a language rather than whether it commands the language itself, conflating fluency with proficiency. We introduce M-GATE (Multilingual Grammar, Accuracy in Translation, and Efficiency), a benchmark of linguistic proficiency spanning 30 typologically diverse languages from high- to low-resource. M-GATE comprises three tasks: grammatical error detection on linguist-crafted, adversarially selected sentences that turn on hard, language-specific phenomena; round-trip translation of shared English sources across 29 target languages, scored by a three-provider LLM judge panel validated against professional annotators; and a supplementary tokenizer-efficiency measure. We evaluate over 50 models in more than 80 configurations. Fluency and proficiency come apart sharply: models that translate competently sit near chance on the adversarial grammar items, the best reaching a Matthews correlation coefficient (MCC) of only 0.36, and their errors lean systematically toward under-flagging, accepting ungrammatical text rather than raising false alarms. Translation quality closely tracks a language's share of pretraining data (r = 0.86 against log Common Crawl share), producing a steep low-resource penalty that is nonetheless narrowing with successive model releases. Enabling reasoning reliably improves translation, while its effect on error detection is smaller and for some models negative, so the best configuration is task-dependent. To resist contamination, test items are kept private behind a continuously updated public leaderboard, with illustrative examples released (https://m-gate.ai).

</details>


### [180] [VIBE: A VAD-Informed Benchmark for Entity-Centered Affective Profiling of Large Language Model Outputs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03810)
*Andrei Chetvergov,Alexander Evseev,Timofei Sivoraksha,Stepan Ukolov,Mikhail Solovev,Danil Sazanakov,Sergey Bolovtsov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍VIBE基准，用于在效价-唤醒-支配（VAD）空间中对大语言模型输出进行实体中心的情感画像分析。其核心是提出"测量契约"：将生成与外部评分分离，区分标量好感度、响应级VAD和目标指向VAD，并通过"情感护照"格式报告画像。三个实证层验证了该契约的合理性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作通过情感、好感度和情绪基准覆盖了LLM对敏感社会目标（政治人物、国家、宗教、组织等）情感编码的部分空间，但没有工作能同时结合目标指向的VAD归因、明确的评分者契约和护照报告格式。这构成了一个方法论空白。

Method: VIBE基准提出三个关键设计：（1）测量契约——将文本生成与外部评分分离，区分三种不同层级的评分类型；（2）三个实证假设验证——H1验证标量好感度无法涵盖唤醒和支配维度，H2验证响应级与目标指向VAD是不同契约，H3进行协议漂移诊断，考察诱导条件对画像的影响；（3）情感护照报告格式——要求附带评分者身份、覆盖范围、协议和解释限制等元数据。

Result: H1：效价维度交叉验证良好（rV=0.944人机相关，rV=0.954评分者间相关），但唤醒和支配仅是单评分者方向性估计（人类标注者间rA=0.495，rD=0.702），说明这些轴难度较大。H2：同一文本的整体情感语气与目标指向的情感画像可能不同。H3：诱导条件会改变情感画像，需要记录协议元数据。

Conclusion: VIBE验证了实体中心情感画像应作为有文档记录的做法：情感画像发布时需附带评分者身份、覆盖范围、协议及解释限制。这为LLM输出中社会敏感目标的情感分析提供了更严谨、可复现的测量框架。

Abstract: Large language models routinely describe socially salient targets, including political figures, countries, religions, organizations, historical events, and social groups, encoding affective framing alongside factual content: a target may appear favorable or threatening, calm or conflictual, powerful or vulnerable. Existing work captures parts of this space through sentiment, favorability, and emotion benchmarks, but none combines target-directed VAD attribution, an explicit scorer contract, and a passport reporting format. We introduce VIBE, a benchmark for entity-centered affective profiling of LLM outputs in Valence-Arousal-Dominance (VAD) space. Its core contribution is a measurement contract: VIBE separates generation from external scoring, distinguishes scalar favorability, response-level VAD, and target-directed VAD, and reports profiles through an Affective Passport. Three empirical layers support the contract. H1 shows scalar favorability does not subsume arousal and dominance: valence findings are cross-validated (rV = 0.944 judge-human, rV = 0.954 inter-scorer); arousal and dominance are single-scorer directional estimates, not point-precise, consistent with known inter-annotator difficulty on these axes (rA = 0.495, rD = 0.702 among human annotators). H2 shows whole-response and target-directed VAD are different contracts: the same text can carry one affective tone overall while representing the named target differently. H3 is a protocol-drift diagnostic: elicitation conditions shift profiles, motivating context metadata in every affective report. These results motivate entity-centered affective profiling as a documented practice: profiles should be released with scorer identity, coverage, protocol, and interpretation limits.

</details>


### [181] [Sensitivity, Causality, and Repair Dissociate: A Layer-Wise Analysis of Perturbation Robustness and Its Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03842)
*Nathan Labiosa,David Buff,Ena Nayak,Erica Donno*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究探讨语言模型在表面扰动输入（拼写错误、OCR噪声、同音词）下失败时，"哪一层负责"的三种操作化定义——敏感性、因果性和补偿能力——在层间是分离的。识别出两种传播模式（尖峰-抑制和晚累积），并证明诊断标记的层位实际上是最差的适配器放置位置，提出了级联破坏机制作为解释。规模扩展会加强晚累积特征，固定框架的层扫描在GSM8K思维链任务上验证了核心预测。同时警示了在思维链任务上评估干预方法可能存在的截断惩罚偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当语言模型在表面扰动输入（如拼写错误、OCR噪声、同音词）上失败时，实践中需要回答"哪个层负责"这个问题，但该问题缺乏统一的操作化定义。作者希望厘清三种可能的定义（敏感性、因果性、补偿能力）是否一致，以及它们对模型修复策略的指导意义。

Method: 在五个模型的测试面板上，对三种层定位方法（表征发散最大的敏感性、恢复干净激活的因果性、小适配器修复的补偿能力）进行系统比较。使用80%身份补丁门控筛选模型，计算敏感性与因果性之间的相关性。通过Qwen2.5家族（1.5B到14B）的规模扩展实验验证晚累积特征随规模单调增强。提出级联破坏机制假设，并通过固定框架层扫描在GSM8K思维链和多项选择控制任务上验证预测。

Result: 三种层定位方法在层间分离（dissociate）。识别出两种传播模式：尖峰-抑制（Phi-3.5、Gemma-2-9B）和晚累积（Llama-3、Mistral、Qwen2.5-7B）。在满足80%身份补丁门控的两个模型上，敏感性与因果性呈负相关（rho = -0.72至-0.88）。Qwen2.5家族扩展实验中晚累积特征随规模单调增强。固定框架层扫描确认因果标记层是最差的适配器放置窗口，在多项选择控制上信号减弱但与生成长度相关的破坏一致。

Conclusion: 因果标记的早期层位放置适配器会破坏完整的下游计算，产生级联破坏，因此诊断标记的层位是最差的适配器位置。实用的修复策略包括训练无关的LRD预筛选和默认最深层放置规则，但绝对增益有限。另一个重要警示是，在思维链任务上评估干预方法时，截断的思维链被计为空会导致虚假的增益——在充足的生成预算下，表示稳定性损失的表面收益会反转，这提示任何在思维链任务上的干预评估都需要注意生成预算设置。

Abstract: When a language model fails on surface-perturbed input (typos, OCR noise, homophones), "which layer is responsible" has three natural operationalizations: where representations diverge most (sensitivity), where restoring clean activations recovers the prediction (causality), and where a small adapter can repair the damage (compensatory capacity) - and we show these three layer maps dissociate. Across a five-model panel we identify two propagation regimes - spike-and-suppress (Phi-3.5, Gemma-2-9B) and late-accumulation (Llama-3, Mistral, Qwen2.5-7B) - and on the two models meeting an 80% identity-patch gate, sensitivity and causality are anti-correlated (rho = -0.72 to -0.88). Within-family scaling on Qwen2.5 (1.5B to 14B) shows the late-accumulation signature strengthening monotonically with scale, corroborated on a second family. We propose cascade disruption as the mechanism behind the dissociation: adapters placed at causally implicated early layers break intact downstream computation, making diagnostic-flagged sites the worst adapter placements. A fixed-harness layer sweep across four models (3.8-8B) confirms the core prediction on chain-of-thought GSM8K - the flagged sites are the most damaging adapter windows on every adjudicable model - and is sign-consistent but strongly attenuated on a multiple-choice control, consistent with damage that compounds with generation length. The sweep yields practical guidance: a training-free LRD pre-screen and a default-deepest placement rule, though absolute gains over no-adapter baselines remain small. Finally, apparent gains from a representation-stability loss reverse under an adequate generation budget - truncated chain-of-thought had been scored as empty - a methodological warning for any intervention evaluated on chain-of-thought tasks.

</details>


### [182] [Beyond Representational Similarity: Source-Conditioned Description-Length Gain for Generative Plagiarism Detection and Candidate Source Reranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03859)
*Peijia Guo,Wenxuan Xie,ZiGuang Li,Ming Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为SCDG（Source-Conditioned Description-Length Gain）的无需训练的检测框架，通过对比可疑文档在有/无候选源条件下的描述长度差异，来检测大语言模型生成的剽窃内容，在PAN基准测试上取得了优于所有基线的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型对学术诚信和同行评审构成挑战，但现有的生成式剽窃检测方法尚未解决这一难题。以往方法要么只检测AI参与度（可能被允许），要么基于相似度的方法在广泛改写和多源综合后失效。

Method: 基于概率预测的描述长度视角，引入SCDG框架，该框架是有方向性的、无需训练的，通过对比冻结语言模型在有无候选源S条件下对可疑文档P的描述长度差异，获得token级别的对数似然增益，以此衡量源S提供的增量预测证据。

Result: 在PAN 2025派生配对基准上达到0.92精确率、0.97召回率和0.94 F1值，优于所有基线；在PAN 2026多源检索任务上达到0.83 nDCG@10和0.96 Recall@100；在Multi-News同主题同事件测试中仅对0.125%的配对预测为源复用。

Conclusion: SCDG被确立为一种统一且可token分解的信号，能够检测经过大量变换后的特定源内容复用，为生成式剽窃检测提供了新方法。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) pose challenges to academic integrity and peer review. Yet generative plagiarism detection remains an underexplored and largely unresolved challenge. Prior work on LLM-generated-text detection targets AI involvement, which may be permissible, rather than source reuse, while similarity-based methods struggle after extensive rewriting and multi-source synthesis. Motivated by the description-length view of probabilistic prediction, in which relevant side information can reduce a target sequence's code length, we introduce Source-Conditioned Description-Length Gain (SCDG), a directional, training-free framework that contrasts a frozen language model's description length of a suspicious document $P$ with and without a candidate source $S$. This contrast yields token-level log-likelihood gains that measure the incremental predictive evidence supplied by $S$. We evaluate SCDG on the PAN at CLEF benchmarks for generative plagiarism. On a PAN 2025-derived pairwise benchmark, SCDG achieves 0.92 Precision, 0.97 Recall, and 0.94 F1, outperforming all baselines; on PAN 2026's multi-source retrieval task, it reaches 0.83 nDCG@10 and 0.96 Recall@100, surpassing all baselines. On a same-topic, same-event Multi-News test, the calibrated gain-distribution SCDG classifier predicts source reuse for only $0.125\%$ of pairs, supporting robustness to topical overlap under this evaluation protocol. These results establish SCDG as a unified and token-decomposable signal for source-specific content reuse under extensive transformation.

</details>


### [183] [SciRet: A Compute-Aware Empirical Study of Retrieval and Reranking for Scientific RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03860)
*Kaysarul Anas Apurba,Md. Hasibul Hasan,Rofiqul Alam Shehab,Asab Azad*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了SciRet，一个针对CORD-19科学问答任务的计算感知型检索增强生成（RAG）实证研究，在三个语料规模（1K、5K、15K论文）上评估固定的科学RAG流水线，发现混合检索比单一稀疏或稠密检索更稳健，但MS MARCO训练的交叉编码器重排序器因领域不匹配而降低精度，生成忠实度随语料规模增加而提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG研究缺乏对科学领域问答系统的系统性实证分析，尤其是在不同语料规模下各组件（检索、重排序、生成）的鲁棒性和交互效应尚不清楚，需要计算感知的、可复现的对照研究。

Method: 固定一条科学RAG流水线，包含句子窗口分块、BM25稀疏检索、BGE-M3稠密检索、倒数排名融合、可选交叉编码器重排序和基于grounding的答案生成，在1K/5K/15K三个语料规模上进行系统评估。

Result: 混合检索在1K和15K规模下Recall@10达到1.000，优于单一稀疏或稠密检索；MS MARCO训练的交叉编码器在科学语料上反而降低精度；生成忠实度（RAGAS）随语料规模增加而提升。

Conclusion: SciRet提供了科学领域RAG流水线的可控对照证据，揭示领域不匹配会削弱重排序优势，并发布了代码、索引和评估输出以支持复现，但需注意检索评估基于伪相关标签，不构成基准性能声明。

Abstract: We introduce SciRet, a compute-aware empirical study of retrieval-augmented generation for scientific question answering over CORD-19. Rather than proposing a new model, we evaluate a fixed scientific RAG pipeline across three corpus scales: 1,034 chunks (1K papers), 5,160 chunks (5K papers), and 15,480 chunks (15K papers). The pipeline combines sentence-window chunking, BM25, BGE-M3 dense retrieval, reciprocal rank fusion, optional cross-encoder reranking, and grounded answer generation. Across these settings, hybrid retrieval is more robust than either sparse-only or dense-only retrieval in our setting, reaching Recall@10 of 1.000 at 1K and 15K. In contrast, an MS MARCO-trained cross-encoder reranker reduces precision on the scientific corpus, suggesting that domain mismatch can outweigh the benefits of stronger query-passage interaction. Generation faithfulness measured with RAGAS increases with corpus scale in our setup. Retrieval evaluation uses pseudo-relevance labels derived from the hybrid system, so we treat the results as controlled comparative evidence rather than a benchmark claim. We release code, indexes, and evaluation outputs to support replication and follow-up studies.

</details>


### [184] [MultiGlobeQA: A Multilingual and Globally Diverse Benchmark for Geospatial Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03882)
*Martin Böckling,Elizaveta Nosova,Heiko Paulheim,Andreea Iana*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出MultiGlobeQA，一个包含46,060个问答对的多语言地理空间推理基准，覆盖14种空间函数家族、15种答案格式、201个国家和地区，并跨越17种语言。研究发现LLM在网格索引和形状计算任务上表现糟糕，拓扑关系和方向任务表现较好；检索和工具使用虽有提升，但即使在提供黄金事实的情况下性能仍有瓶颈，表明计算能力而非知识获取是主要限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有地理空间推理基准存在合成数据、规模小、单语主导、地理覆盖控制有限等问题，无法全面定位LLM在地理空间推理中的失败。需要构建一个大规模、多语言、地理覆盖严谨的基准来系统评估LLM的空间推理能力。

Method: 构建MultiGlobeQA基准，包含46,060个问答对，涵盖14种空间函数家族和15种答案格式，基于三个知识图谱生成执行级真值。通过收入分层和密度分层采样覆盖201个国家和地区，提供英语及另外16种高资源和低资源语言的平行问题。在参数化、推理和智能体三种设置下评估LLM。

Result: LLM在网格索引和形状计算任务上表现极差，拓扑关系和方向任务表现最好。检索和工具使用带来显著提升，但即使提供黄金事实，性能也低于三分之二，表明计算瓶颈而非知识瓶颈。模型在低收入地区表现不佳，且提供黄金事实反而扩大了这一差距。

Conclusion: LLM尽管存储了大量地理知识，但其空间计算能力是核心瓶颈。未来的地理空间推理系统应侧重于增强计算能力而非仅仅扩展知识访问，同时需要关注低收入地区的性能公平性问题。

Abstract: Geospatial reasoning, i.e., computing distances, containment, and other spatial relations over real-world entities, is central to navigation and logistics, yet large language models (LLMs) struggle with the required geometric and topological computation despite storing considerable geographic knowledge. Existing benchmarks localize these failures only partially: they are synthetic or smallscale, largely monolingual, and offer limited control over geographic coverage. We introduce MultiGlobeQA, a multilingual benchmark of 46,060 question-answer pairs spanning 14 spatial-function families and 15 answer formats, with execution-based ground truth over three knowledge graphs. It covers 201 countries and territories via income- and density-stratified sampling, with parallel questions in English and 16 additional high- and low-resource languages. Across parametric, reasoning, and agentic settings, LLMs collapse on tasks requiring grid indexing and shape computation, while topological relations and directions fare best. Retrieval and tool use yield considerable gains, yet performance plateaus below two thirds even when gold facts are supplied, indicating that computation, not access to knowledge, is the bottleneck. Models also underperform on low-income regions, a gap that gold facts widen rather than close.

</details>


### [185] [DS@GT-ARC at eRisk 2026 Task 3: Sparse, Semantic, and LLM Reranking for ADHD Symptom Sentences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03883)
*David Guecha*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍作者团队在eRisk 2026 Task 3（ADHD症状句子排序任务）中的系统提交方案。由于任务未提供标注训练数据，作者采用零样本实验、人工验证及无监督/弱监督检索管线，结合BM25稀疏检索、证据感知重打分、嵌入重排序、查询原型扩展和LLM重排序等多阶段方法，最终LLM重排序器取得最佳官方成绩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: eRisk 2026 Task 3首次提出ADHD症状句子排序任务，要求系统根据ASRS-v1.1量表中的18项症状对Reddit句子进行相关性排序。由于该任务为首次举办且未发布任何标注训练数据，如何在零样本条件下有效检索和排序相关句子成为核心挑战。

Method: 采用多阶段检索设计：(1) BM25稀疏检索在大规模候选中初步筛选；(2) 证据感知重打分处理自我指涉症状报告；(3) 基于嵌入的语义重排序；(4) 查询原型扩展增强检索表达；(5) LLM重排序作为最终精排阶段。整个系统在零样本设置下运行，辅以人工验证。

Result: 在所有提交系统中，LLM重排序器获得最强官方分数，其次为原型查询扩展方案。人工top-10分析结果与官方专家评分趋势一致，验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 多阶段重排序检索框架是ADHD症状句子排序任务的有前景方向，LLM重排序在该零样本任务中表现出色，值得进一步开发优化。

Abstract: This paper describes our submissions to eRisk 2026 Task 3, ADHD Symptom Sentence Ranking. The task requires systems to rank candidate Reddit sentences according to their relevance to each of the 18 symptoms in the Adult ADHD Self-Report Scale (ASRS-v1.1). Because no annotated training data were released for this first edition of the task, we relied on zero-shot experimentation, manual validation, and unsupervised or weakly guided retrieval pipelines. Our systems combine sparse BM25 retrieval, evidence-aware rescoring for self-referential symptom reports, embedding-based reranking, query-prototype expansion, and LLM-based reranking. All submitted systems follow a staged retrieval design in which BM25 retrieves candidates at scale and semantic or LLM rerankers refine the final rankings. Among our submissions, the LLM reranker achieved the strongest official scores, followed by the prototype query-expansion run. Our manual top-10 analysis aligned with the official expert scoring trend, suggesting that staged reranking is a promising direction for further development.

</details>


### [186] [ANNOTARES: A Dataset for Extracting Logical Structures from German Statutory Texts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03898)
*Ronja Schwarz,Jannik Strötgen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了法律文本中"构成要件"（Tatbestand）与"法律后果"（Rechtsfolge）的自动识别与切分任务，并发布了包含德语法律条文跨度级标注的新数据集ANNOTARES，涵盖了三个不同的法律法典。作者对多种方法（规则基线、CRF、BiLSTM、BiLSTM-CRF、BERT变体及基于LLM的方法）进行了基准测试，结果表明BERT和基于LLM的模型在捕捉法律语言复杂句法结构方面表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 法律文本的结构化分析是法律技术的基石，但从法律条文中提取逻辑组成部分（如构成要件与法律后果）仍然是一个重大挑战，目前缺乏专门支持此任务的数据集和方法。

Method: 作者创建了ANNOTARES数据集（对德语法律文本进行跨度级标注，涵盖三个法律法典），并系统基准测试了多种方法：规则基线、CRF、BiLSTM、BiLSTM-CRF、BERT变体以及基于LLM的方法，以评估跨法令场景下的泛化能力。

Result: BERT和基于LLM的模型在识别法律条文中的构成要件与法律后果方面取得了最优性能，优于传统序列标注方法，能够更好地处理法律语言的复杂句法结构。

Conclusion: 基于Transformer的预训练模型在大规模法律文本的结构化分析中具有显著优势，同时数据集的公开发布将为自动化法律推理领域的进一步研究提供重要基础资源。

Abstract: The automatic structural analysis of legal texts is a cornerstone of legal technology, yet the extraction of their logical components remains a significant challenge. In this paper, we introduce the task of identifying and segmenting legal conditions (Tatbestand) and legal consequences (Rechtsfolge) within German statutory texts. To support this task, we present ANNOTARES (Annotations of Tatbestand-Rechtsfolge Sequences), a novel dataset comprising German law texts with span-level annotations. Spanning three distinct legal codes, the dataset is designed to evaluate both domain-specific performance and cross-statute generalizability. We benchmark diverse architectural approaches: a rule-based baseline, CRFs, BiLSTMs, BiLSTM-CRF, and modern Transformer-based models, including BERT variants and LLM-based methods. Our results demonstrate that BERT and LLM-based models achieve superior performance in capturing the complex syntactic structures of legal language. We release our dataset to facilitate further research in automated legal reasoning.

</details>


### [187] [Logic Before Language: Pre-pretraining on Formal Derivations Fosters Skill Acquisition and Compressibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03930)
*Jo-Ku Cheng,Nikolaos Aletras,Marco Valentino*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出逻辑预预训练（Logic-PPT）策略，通过形式化推导为语言模型注入更丰富的结构与语言先验知识。在100B token规模下评估发现，该策略可显著加速语言技能习得，比标准初始化节省360亿token即达80%准确率，并在机制上诱导低秩、谱集中的表征空间，使模型在约33%稀疏度下仍能匹配密集基线性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的预预训练任务（如Dyck语言和程序化算法）依赖过于狭窄的原语，无法捕捉自然语言的表达能力；且先前研究仅限于较小的token预算，对技能涌现和表征动态的洞察有限。

Method: 提出逻辑预预训练（Logic-PPT），利用形式化推导作为初始化策略。形式化推导需要抽象机制（如变量绑定、量词与关系依赖连接、长上下文中谓词-论元结构的组合），这些机制与自然语言核心能力高度相关。评估扩展到100B token规模。

Result: 逻辑预预训练显著加速技能习得：在语言任务上以比标准初始化少360亿token即达到80%准确率，优于其他预预训练基线。机制分析显示形式化推导诱导持续的结构重组织，表现为低秩、谱集中的表征空间。

Conclusion: 逻辑预预训练表征的内部几何特性使模型具有更好的可压缩性，通过剪枝在约33%稀疏度下仍能匹配密集基线的性能，说明形式化推导不仅加速学习，还能赋予模型更高效的结构化表征。

Abstract: Pre-pretraining language models (LMs) on symbolic data can accelerate and improve natural language acquisition. However, existing pre-pretraining tasks, such as Dyck and procedural algorithms, rely on narrow primitives that fail to capture the expressive capacity of natural language. Moreover, prior studies remain restricted to relatively small token budgets, offering limited insight into skill emergence and representational dynamics. To address these limitations, we propose logic pre-pretraining (Logic-PPT) as a principled initialization strategy, leveraging formal derivations to impart richer structural and linguistic biases. Formal derivations require abstract mechanisms that are central to natural language, simultaneously binding variables, connecting quantifiers and relational dependencies, and composing predicate-argument structures over long contexts. Scaling our evaluation to a 100B-token regime, logic pre-pretraining substantially accelerates skill acquisition in LMs, achieving 80\% accuracy on linguistic tasks with 36B fewer tokens than standard initialization, and outperforming alternative pre-pretraining baselines. Mechanistically, formal derivations induce persistent structural reorganization, distinctively characterized by a lower-rank, spectrally concentrated representation space. Crucially, we show that this internal geometry enables improved model compressibility via pruning, matching the dense baseline performance even at $\approx$33\% sparsity.

</details>


### [188] [HalluTruthQA-4K: A Fine-Grained Corpus and Annotation Process for Arabic Hallucination Detection and Truth Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03966)
*Salah Eddine Bekhouche,Abdessalam Bouchekif,Hichem Telli,Mohammed-En-Nadhir Zighem,Abdenour Hadid*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了HalluTruthQA-4K，一个包含4,000条阿拉伯语问答实例的扩展语料库，覆盖伊斯兰知识、历史、科学和地理四个知识密集型领域，用于检测和评估大语言模型在阿拉伯语中的幻觉现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在生成阿拉伯语回答时经常产生难以识别和验证的事实性错误，而现有阿拉伯语幻觉资源仅对整个回答给出二元标签，缺乏关于具体错误内容、错误原因和正确事实答案的详细信息。

Method: 通过问题选择、受控答案生成、干扰项构建、专家标注、独立验证、仲裁和质量控制等流程构建语料库，每条幻觉回答均标注字符级错误片段、人工撰写的解释和层级化幻觉类型。语料包含1,643条幻觉回答和2,357条非幻觉回答，共1,843个标注的错误片段。

Result: 构建了包含4,000条专家策展的阿拉伯语问答实例的HalluTruthQA-4K语料库，作为HalluScoring 2026共享任务Track 2的官方数据集，并记录了标注指南、分类体系、数据格式、标注者间一致性和语料统计等信息。

Conclusion: HalluTruthQA-4K为幻觉检测、片段级错误定位、解释生成、事实核查以及阿拉伯语大语言模型事实可靠性评估提供了可复用的资源。

Abstract: Large language models can generate fluent Arabic answers while introducing factual errors that are difficult to identify and verify. Existing Arabic hallucination resources often assign a binary label to an entire response, indicating whether it is hallucinated or non-hallucinated, but provide limited information about the exact erroneous content, the reason for the error, or the correct factual answer. We present HalluTruthQA-4K, an expanded version of the HalluTruthQA resource containing 4,000 expert-curated Arabic question-answering instances across four knowledge-intensive domains: Islamic knowledge, history, science, and geography. Serving as the official dataset for Track 2 of the HalluScoring 2026 shared task, HalluTruthQA-4K extends our original corpus to 4,000 instances. Each instance pairs an Arabic question with a model-generated response, a verified reference answer, and five plausible distractors. Hallucinated responses are additionally annotated with character-level erroneous spans, human-written explanations, and hierarchical hallucination types. The corpus contains 1,643 hallucinated and 2,357 non-hallucinated responses, with 1,843 annotated erroneous spans. We describe the resource construction and annotation methodology, including question selection, controlled answer generation, candidate construction, expert annotation, independent verification, adjudication, and quality control. We also document the annotation guidelines, taxonomy, data format, inter-annotator agreement, and corpus statistics. HalluTruthQA-4K provides a reusable resource for hallucination detection, span-level error localization, explanation generation, factual verification, and the broader evaluation of factual reliability in Arabic language models.

</details>


### [189] [string2string Studio: An Interactive, In-Browser Platform for String-to-String Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03984)
*Mirac Suzgun,James Zou,Stuart M. Shieber,Dan Jurafsky*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了string2string Studio，一个基于浏览器的交互式字符串到字符串分析平台，支持NLP、计算生物学和数字人文领域的六大模块，通过WebAssembly实现本地高速运行，提供可解释的评分证据，性能最高比Python版本快2500倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的字符串分析工具通常需要安装、上传数据或缺乏可解释性，且性能有限。作者旨在构建一个无需安装、数据本地处理、结果可检查和调试的交互式平台，同时提供显著的速度提升。

Method: 平台包含六大模块（对齐、距离、相似度、搜索、生成指标和BLAST同源搜索），在字符、词、token、行和残基多个粒度上运行。核心算法用C++编写并编译为WebAssembly，默认在本地执行，无需安装或数据上传。界面展示评分的证据（对齐、编辑路径、指标匹配、搜索命中和同源追踪），并包含讲解模式和精选展示。

Result: 内部基准测试显示，相比Python版本速度提升最高达2500倍；全局/局部对齐比通用原生C语言对齐器更快；在声明设置下与独立参考结果完全一致。同源搜索的客户端blastn路径在匹配参数下与NCBI BLAST+的排名和统计紧密吻合。

Conclusion: string2string Studio是一个开源、免费可用的高性能字符串分析平台，兼具可解释性、可调试性和可比较性，适用于多个跨学科领域。

Abstract: We present string2string Studio, an interactive in-browser platform for string-to-string analysis across natural language processing, computational biology, and the digital humanities. The system integrates six main modules (alignment, distance, similarity, search, generation metrics, and BLAST homology search), operating at character, word, token, line, and residue levels. Its C++-based algorithms compile to WebAssembly, so core operations run locally by default without any installation or data upload. The interface reports scores with their "evidence" (alignments, edit paths, metric matches, search hits, and homology traces), making methods inspectable, debuggable, and comparable on shared inputs. Internal benchmarks show speedups of up to 2,500x over the Python predecessor, faster global/local alignment than a general-purpose native C aligner, and exact agreement with independent references under declared settings. For homology search, the scoped client-side blastn path closely matches NCBI BLAST+ rankings and statistics under matched parameters. A curated showcase and Learn mode present canonical algorithms and metrics as reusable demonstrations. string2string Studio is open-source and freely available at string2string.org.

</details>


### [190] [When Attention Goes Blind: Numerical Failure in ALiBi Positional Encodings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03994)
*Christopher Schröder,Lukas Gienapp,Ferdinand Schlatt,Martin Potthast,Gerhard Heyer*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发现ALiBi位置编码存在一个被忽视的失败模式：其线性偏置缩放会因浮点精度不足而下溢，导致大量注意力权重归零，使部分注意力头"部分失明"。作者分析了该问题的影响，评估了四种缓解策略，并在148M参数的解码器模型上进行了全面预训练实验，发现该问题会显著损害token检索能力但对标准基准影响较小，日志缩放距离是最一致的改进方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ALiBi位置编码被广泛使用，但其线性偏置缩放在浮点精度下可能发生下溢，导致注意力权重被错误置零。这一失败模式此前未被识别，可能导致模型在特定任务上性能受损，因此有必要深入分析其影响并提出缓解方案。

Method: 作者首先分析ALiBi线性偏置在浮点精度下的下溢机制，然后在state-of-the-art预训练模型中验证该现象的存在性。接着使用148M参数的解码器模型进行全面的预训练实验，以将ALiBi失败模式与上下文外退化（out-of-context degradation）的影响分离开来。提出了四种训练时缓解策略（包括日志缩放距离等），并分别及组合评估这些策略的效果。

Result: ALiBi的失败模式会显著损害token检索（如passkey retrieval）能力，但对标准解码器基准影响较小。四种缓解策略中，日志缩放距离（log-scaled distances）在passkey检索上带来最一致的改进。值得注意的是，默认的ALiBi斜率仍然是相当强的基线，尤其在needle-in-a-haystack检索任务上。

Conclusion: 尽管ALiBi存在浮点下溢的失败模式，默认ALiBi斜率仍然是一个强有力的基线。作者基于实验结果提供了关于如何使用ALiBi训练模型的具体建议，指出日志缩放距离是缓解该问题的最有效策略。

Abstract: We identify a previously overlooked failure mode of ALiBi positional encoding: its linear bias scaling underflows floating-point precision, which zeroes out a large fraction of attention weights and renders the affected attention heads partially blind. We analyze this failure mode, characterize its impact, and examine four mitigation strategies. We further demonstrate its occurrence in state-of-the-art pretrained models based on ALiBi. Comprehensive pretraining experiments with 148M-parameter decoder models help us to disentangle its effects from out-of-context degradation. We find that ALiBi's failure mode can substantially impair token retrieval while having only a minor effect on standard decoder benchmarks. We propose four training-time mitigation strategies and evaluate them individually and in combinations, finding that log-scaled distances yield the most consistent improvements in passkey retrieval. Despite this problem, default ALiBi slopes remain a surprisingly strong baseline, particularly for needle-in-a-haystack retrieval. Based on these findings we provide concrete recommendations on how to train models with ALiBi.

</details>


### [191] [PAST-Bench: Benchmarking the Foundations of Recursive Self-Improvement in Personal Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04003)
*Shuhan Xue,Zixin Ding,Yichen Shen,Yinjie Wang,Zhenfei Yin,Yingcheng Wu,Yuxin Chen,Mengdi Wang,Ling Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出PAST-Bench基准，用于系统评估个人AI代理能否利用保留的经验实现跨会话改进，并基于发现开发了Hermes+框架，通过五项针对性干预提升代理从经验中学习的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管个人AI代理能跨会话保留偏好、任务历史和工具例程，但保留的经验是否真正改善其后续表现尚未被系统测试过，缺乏一个隔离该问题的评估基准。

Method: 构建PAST-Bench基准，包含26个场景和204个片段，覆盖记忆、程序复用、信息收集和更新四类能力；在匹配条件下对比保留经验开/关的表现，并检验是否遵循保存-检索-更新路径。基于发现开发Hermes+，在代理循环各阶段加入五项针对性干预。

Result: 在7个基础模型和4个代理框架上，改进真实存在但不均衡；相同整体增益的代理在路径证据上差异显著。Hermes+提升了平均增益并提供更清晰的路径证据，在需要替换过时状态的任务上改进最强，但效果仍依赖具体能力和模型。

Conclusion: PAST-Bench和Hermes+为研究持久代理如何从保留经验走向系统性改进提供了评估与诊断基础。

Abstract: Recursive self-improvement requires agents to turn accumulated experience into better future behavior. Personal AI agents offer a concrete setting for studying this capability because they retain preferences, task histories, tool routines, and learned skills across sessions. Yet whether retained experience actually improves them over time has not been systematically tested. We introduce PAST-Bench, a benchmark designed to isolate this question. Each agent runs through ordered sequences of fresh-session tasks under matched conditions that turn retained experience on and off. It spans 26 scenarios and 204 episodes across memory, procedural reuse, information gathering, and update. We report both later-task gains and whether those gains follow the intended save, retrieve, and update pathway. Across seven base models and four agent frameworks, improvement is real but uneven across capabilities. Agents with the same headline gain can differ markedly in whether that gain is supported by evidence of the intended pathway. Guided by these findings, we develop Hermes+, which extends Hermes with five targeted interventions across stages of the agent loop. Hermes+ raises the average gain from retained experience and provides clearer pathway evidence, with its strongest improvement on tasks requiring outdated state to be replaced, although the effect remains capability- and model-dependent. Together, PAST-Bench and Hermes+ provide an evaluation and diagnostic foundation for studying how persistent agents can progress from retaining experience to systematically improving through it. Code: https://github.com/Gen-Verse/PAST-Bench

</details>


### [192] [TurnSight: Turn-Level Hindsight Self-Distillation for Tool-Integrated Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04007)
*Changle Qu,Sunhao Dai,Hengyi Cai,Yuqi Zhou,Xinran Chen,Simon,Jun Xu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出TurnSight框架，通过回合级事后自我蒸馏（turn-level hindsight self-distillation）为工具集成推理（TIR）中的强化学习提供更精细的信用分配信号。该方法从执行条件的事后视角获取监督，构造多视野事后视角并通过跨视野方向一致性选择可靠信号，最终在兄弟rollout间归一化后自适应调制RL优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TIR中的强化学习方法依赖轨迹级监督，在长时程TIR场景下难以进行细粒度信用分配。已有的on-policy自我蒸馏方法从ground-truth答案或检索技能中获取教师上下文，无法反映agent实际访问的状态；且token级监督无法捕捉工具交互的回合级结构。

Method: 1）从执行条件的事后视角（execution-conditioned hindsight）直接获取监督信号；2）构造多个具有不同前瞻视野的事后视角，通过跨视野方向一致性（cross-horizon directional agreement）选择可靠监督；3）将选定的事后信号在兄弟rollout间进行归一化，自适应地调制RL优势值，同时保留原始优化方向。

Result: 在三个基准数据集上的大量实验验证了TurnSight的有效性。

Conclusion: TurnSight通过回合级事后自我蒸馏和跨视野一致性选择，有效解决了TIR中强化学习的细粒度信用分配问题，为长时程工具交互场景提供了更可靠的监督信号。

Abstract: Tool-Integrated Reasoning (TIR) enables LLMs to solve complex tasks through iterative tool interactions. However, existing reinforcement learning methods often rely on trajectory-level supervision, limiting fine-grained credit assignment in long-horizon TIR scenarios. On-policy self-distillation offers denser signals through teacher branches with privileged context, but existing approaches typically derive such context from ground-truth answers or retrieved skills, which may not reflect the states actually visited by the agent. Moreover, token-level supervision fails to capture the turn-level structure of tool interactions. To address this, we propose TurnSight, a turn-level hindsight self-distillation framework that derives supervision directly from execution-conditioned hindsight. It then constructs multiple hindsight views with different lookahead horizons and selects reliable supervision through cross-horizon directional agreement. Finally, the selected hindsight signal is normalized across sibling rollouts and used to adaptively modulate RL advantages while preserving their original optimization direction. Extensive experiments on three benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness of TurnSight. Our codes are available at https://github.com/quchangle1/TurnSight.

</details>


### [193] [WorldCup Arena: Prospective, Leakage-Free Evaluation of Frontier LLMs on a Live Tournament](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04008)
*Zhenran Wang,Zhonghan Bian,Jinsong Li,Zhangyang Qi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文报告了一个前瞻性而非回顾性的基准测试：在2026年FIFA世界杯的39天中，六个前沿LLM在每场比赛开球前需预测七项比赛市场结果，共涉及104场比赛加12个小组冠军和赛前冠军池，总计4,494个评分预测。由于问题提出时答案尚不存在，因此评测天然无泄漏。结果显示这些模型在比赛结果上平均准确率63.9%，与押注博彩公司热门持平；模型间高度一致，多数投票无增益；低估平局和进球，比分预测集中于单一原型结果；准确性跟随比赛悬殊程度而非信息丰富度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM预测能力的基准几乎都是回顾性的——事件已发生、答案已在网上，评测必须防御模型记忆。作者希望设计一种前瞻性评测：在问题提出时答案尚不存在，从而从根本上避免数据泄漏问题。

Method: 在2026年世界杯39天期间，六款前沿LLM（均具备扩展推理和原生服务端网络搜索）在每场开球前被要求填写七市场预测卡，覆盖全部104场比赛，外加12个小组冠军和赛前冠军池。所有预测被冻结存档，共4,494个评分预测，并使用官方赛果和评分代码进行评估。

Result: 模型平均比赛结果准确率63.9%，与押注博彩公司热门相当（事实上模型通常正是这样做）。模型之间的一致性远高于其正确性，多数投票无增益。模型低估平局和进球数，比分预测集中于单一原型结果。准确率与比赛悬殊程度相关而非信息量：在最接近的比赛（信息最丰富）中准确率崩溃，而关于整个赛事的问题回答良好。模型间区分度不高：顶部和底部稳定，中间名次波动，差距始终很小。

Conclusion: 当前一代前沿LLM在此预测任务上表现与简单基线（跟注博彩热门）相当，并未展现出超越性的预测能力；其行为存在系统性偏差（低估平局和进球、比分预测过于集中），且彼此高度同质化。该预测档案、赛程和官方赛果已作为基准连同评分代码一起发布。

Abstract: Benchmarks that measure the forecasting ability of large language models are almost always retrospective: the event has happened, the answer is somewhere on the Web, and the evaluation must defend itself against memorisation. We report the opposite design. Over the 39 days of the 2026 FIFA World Cup, six frontier LLMs -- all with extended thinking and native server-side web search -- were asked before every kickoff, one match at a time, to fill in a seven-market prediction card for all 104 matches, plus 12 group winners and a pre-tournament outright pool; no answer existed when the question was asked, so the evaluation is leakage-free by construction rather than by filtering, and the frozen archive holds 4,494 scored predictions. What the tournament establishes is a set of behaviours the six systems share. On match outcome they average 63.9%, level with backing the bookmaker's favourite -- which is in fact what they usually do. They agree with one another far more often than they are right, so a majority vote adds nothing. They under-commit to draws and to goals, and crowd their scoreline picks onto a single prototypical result. Accuracy tracks how lopsided a fixture is rather than how much is known about it: it collapses in the closest ties, where the dossiers are richest, while questions about the tournament as a whole are answered well. On this task the current generation of frontier systems is not sharply differentiated: the standings hold up at the top and the bottom across the run and churn in the middle, and the margins stay narrow throughout. The briefing dossiers, fixtures and official results are released as a benchmark, together with the scoring code.

</details>


### [194] [SocietyBench: Forecasting Counterfactual Social-World Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04009)
*Zhenran Wang,Zhonghan Bian,Jinsong Li,Zhangyang Qi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SocietyBench是一个端到端的社会事件预测基准，从新闻和社交媒体构建时间线并生成预测问题，通过实体替换和日期平移创建反事实社会世界，评估六个前沿LLM在概率校准和时间准确性两个维度上的表现，最强模型仅得75分（满分100），且两个能力维度可分离，多事件评估优于单事件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM基准主要衡量任务完成能力（修bug、操作浏览器/GUI），但缺乏对模型理解与预测真实社会事件演化方式的互补性社会能力的评估。

Method: 从一行事件主题出发，收集五个平台的新闻和社交媒体帖子，提炼为日期索引的时间线（事实层和舆论层分离），每个截止日期生成一组可审计的预测问题。通过三步流程替换所有命名实体并平移日期，构建反事实社会世界以消除预训练记忆的影响。在五个异构事件和125个预测点上，以中文和英文双版本评估，采用概率校准和时间准确性两个正交的100分制评分轴。

Result: 六个前沿LLM中最强者仅达75.0分（对照锚点为50分）。概率校准和时间准确性两个维度可分离（校准强但时间弱，或反之）。三个基于同一基础模型的智能体框架未能超越基础模型，两个无模型启发式方法均落后于所有LLM。单事件单轴性能差距最高达21.4分。

Conclusion: 当前的LLM社会预测能力仍然有限，且评价应覆盖多个事件而非单一事件。所有匿名化时间线、问题库、真实答案和评分代码均已发布。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs), and the agents built on top of them, are now benchmarked heavily on whether they can finish a task -- fix a bug, drive a browser, operate a GUI. A complementary social ability, namely how well a model understands and forecasts the way real social events unfold, has barely been measured. We introduce SocietyBench, an end-to-end benchmark that takes a one-line event topic, collects Web news and social-media posts across five platforms, distills them into a date-indexed timeline that keeps factual events and a public-opinion layer separate, and then turns every cutoff date on that timeline into an audited bank of forecasting questions. Questions are scored on two orthogonal 100-point axes: probability calibration and temporal accuracy. Before any model sees a timeline, a three-phase procedure replaces every named entity and shifts every date by a per-event constant, turning a real arc into a counterfactual social world -- structurally identical to what happened, but stripped of the surface labels a model could match against pre-training memory. On five heterogeneous events and 125 prediction points in Chinese and English editions, the strongest of six frontier LLMs reaches only 75.0 out of 100, against a trivial anchor of 50. The two axes come apart: a model can be calibration-strong but time-weak, or the reverse. Three agent frameworks built on a shared base model fail to improve on that base, and two model-free heuristics trail every LLM. Per-event gaps reach 21.4 points on a single axis, which is our main argument for evaluating on several events rather than one. All anonymized timelines, question banks, ground truth, and scoring code are released.

</details>


### [195] [MemArena: An Ego-Centric Benchmark for On-Device Agentic Personal Memory Assistants at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02613)
*Jiadong Zhang,Xiaosong Ma*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MemArena是一个面向边缘部署的个人记忆助手的对话基准，由MASim模拟器生成包含50个代理15天活动的单一世界对话数据（1030万token），并在六个评估维度上评估五种开放权重阅读器与多种记忆后端的组合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆基准未能充分结合活动密集交互、自我中心视角和连贯的多会话世界这三个关键维度，无法有效评测边缘端个人记忆助手在开放权重模型上的表现。

Method: 构建MASim代理模拟器生成单一世界的对话基准，覆盖50个代理15天活动（10.3M对话文本token，每代理每日24.1K自我中心观察token），协同生成六个评估维度的真值标注。评估五种开放权重阅读器与五种记忆后端（Vanilla、BM25-RAG、Oracle、Memobase、MemSearch）的组合。

Result: 三个关键发现：(1)记忆后端选择对内容准确性的影响大于阅读器缩放（Qwen3-0.6B上Memobase到MemSearch提升+32.5/+19.2pp，超过阅读器缩放带来的+10.6/+6.8pp）；(2)权限感知访问普遍失败，Oracle严重泄露而其他后端过于保守；(3)搜索延迟仅在极小型阅读器上产生明显影响，在Spark GB10边缘节点上固定附加延迟为87/7/48ms。

Conclusion: MemArena填补了记忆基准在活动密集交互、自我中心视角和连贯多会话世界方面的空白，揭示了记忆后端选择的重要性以及权限感知机制在现有模型中的普遍缺失。

Abstract: Edge-deployed personal memory assistants must handle private interpersonal conversations on-device with open-weight models. Yet, existing memory benchmarks often under-test the combination of activity-dense interaction, ego-centric perspective, and coherent multi-session worlds. MemArena fills these gaps with a single-world conversational benchmark built with its MASim agent simulator, for 50 agents over 15 days (10.3M dialog-text tokens, 24.1K text-only ego-observed tokens/agent/day). With the interaction history, it co-generates ground truth over six recall, reasoning, and trustworthiness evaluation dimensions. We evaluate five open-weight readers with Vanilla context, BM25-RAG, Oracle retrieval, Memobase, and MemSearch as memory backends. Three results stand out: (1) Memory-backend choice matters more for content accuracy: At Qwen3-0.6B, Memobase-to-MemSearch gains +32.5/+19.2 pp, exceeding MemSearch reader scaling (+10.6/+6.8 pp). (2) Permission-aware access fails universally, with Oracle leaking heavily and other backends too timid to disclose. (3) Search latency bites only at very small reader: on a Spark GB10 edge node, memory-search adds a moderate and fixed 87/7/48 ms (BM25-RAG/Memobase/MemSearch) that composes a small part of TTFT for most reader-backend combinations. Code, the MASim simulator, and the MemArena-L benchmark will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [196] [OncoTriad-QA: A Patient-Level Radiology-Pathology-Genomics Benchmark for Pan-Cancer Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02615)
*Ahnaf Munir,Dannong Wang,Michael W. McDonald,Mubarak Shah,Pegah Khosravi,Yu Tian*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出OncoTriad-QA，一个覆盖放射学、病理学、基因组学和临床元数据的患者级泛癌问答基准，包含86.1k个语义问题、9,281个TCGA患者案例和32个癌症队列。同时引入OncoVLM多模态模型，通过投影器将各模态证据映射到LLM接口。经过微调后，OncoVLM在MCQ准确率和BERTScore-F1上平均超过MedGemma-4B 10.7分，证明了该基准对整合癌症问答训练与评估的价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学LLM和VLM基准大多聚焦于单一模态或狭窄的图像-文本任务，缺乏对患者级、跨多种证据流（放射学、病理学、基因组学、临床元数据）的肿瘤学综合评估，导致多模态整合的癌症诊疗能力未被充分测试。

Method: 1) 构建OncoTriad-QA基准：从TCGA收集9,281个患者案例、32个癌症队列，整合CT/MRI放射学、全切片组织病理学、体细胞突变、拷贝数变异、DNA甲基化、bulk RNA-seq和临床元数据，生成86.1k个语义问题。2) 采用源接地LLM辅助管道构建案例特定标注，以策展标签、诊断报告、分子谱和模态衍生证据为真值来源，并附自动一致性检查和临床医生审查。3) 提出OncoVLM参考多模态模型，通过可学习投影器将模态原生放射学、病理学、DNA甲基化和RNA-seq证据映射到LLM接口。

Result: 现有通用和医学LLM在全面泛癌QA上表现有限，尤其在需要整合影像发现、肿瘤形态和分子证据的问题上。经过OncoTriad-QA微调后，OncoVLM在MCQ准确率和BERTScore-F1上平均超过MedGemma-4B 10.7分，在单选和开放问答、放射学单独、病理学单独及全部可用设置下均有一致提升。

Conclusion: OncoTriad-QA作为患者级多模态整合癌症QA基准，有效暴露了现有模型的局限，并为训练和评估整合癌症问答模型提供了有价值的资源；OncoVLM展示了在该基准上微调可显著提升多模态癌症问答性能。

Abstract: Cancer diagnosis and characterization require integrating complementary evidence from radiology, pathology, genomics, and clinical metadata. However, most medical large language model (LLM) and vision-language model (VLM) benchmarks focus on isolated modalities or narrow image-text tasks, leaving patient-level oncology assessment across multiple evidence streams largely untested. We introduce OncoTriad-QA, a patient-level radiology-pathology-genomics benchmark for pan-cancer question answering. OncoTriad-QA contains 86.1k semantic questions across 9,281 TCGA patient cases from 32 cancer cohorts, aligning CT/MRI radiology, whole-slide histopathology, somatic mutations, copy-number alterations, DNA methylation, bulk RNA-seq, and clinical metadata. Case-specific annotations are constructed through a source-grounded LLM-assisted pipeline using curated labels, diagnostic reports, molecular profiles, and modality-derived evidence as primary sources of truth, with automated consistency checks and clinician review. We also introduce OncoVLM, a reference multimodal model that maps modality-native radiology, pathology, DNA methylation, and RNA-seq evidence into an LLM interface through learned projectors. Experiments show that existing general-purpose and medical LLMs remain limited on comprehensive pan-cancer QA, especially when questions require integrating imaging findings, tumor morphology, and molecular evidence. After fine-tuning on OncoTriad-QA, OncoVLM exceeds MedGemma-4B by an average of 10.7 points when using MCQ accuracy and BERTScore-F1, with consistent gains across multiple-choice and open-ended questions under radiology-only, pathology-only, and all-available settings. These results demonstrate the benchmark's value for training and evaluating models for integrated cancer question answering.

</details>


### [197] [Evaluating OpenAI's Privacy Filter: Cross-Lingual, Cross-Domain PII Detection Across 42 Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02616)
*Rohith Uppala*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 首次对OpenAI Privacy Filter（OPF）进行独立系统评估，在22种语言、5个领域的42个合成基准上测试了其PII检测性能。OPF在结构化PII检测上表现优于现有工具，但在非拉丁语系和叙事文本中性能急剧下降，存在明显的领域与文化偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管OpenAI Privacy Filter作为1.5B参数的PII检测器已被部署，但其性能从未经过独立的系统性评估。作者希望通过跨多语言、多领域的全面基准测试，客观衡量OPF的实际能力、局限性和适用边界，为PII检测工具的选择提供实证依据。

Method: 构建42个合成基准数据集，覆盖22种语言和5个领域（医疗、法律、金融、客服、结构化PII）。在零样本设置下评估OPF，并与Presidio、XLM-RoBERTa和GPT-4o等基线进行对比，同时进行错误类型分析和精度-召回率平衡评估。

Result: OPF在AI4Privacy上F1=0.855，在SPY医疗上0.464，均优于Presidio和XLM-RoBERTa；但GPT-4o在医疗/法律/金融PII上领先。OPF对非拉丁语系（阿拉伯语0.04、西里尔语0.03）和叙事文本中嵌入的PII（F1=0.04-0.57）性能崩溃；对结构化PII（email 0.78、phone 0.76）表现最好，对人的姓名（0.40）和地址（0.49）表现最弱。

Conclusion: OPF是结构化、拉丁语系PII检测的强工具，但存在明显的语言和文本类型偏见——非拉丁语系和叙事文本环境中实用性极低。此外，其全局精度范围仅0.31-0.86，并表现出召回率偏向（P=0.31-0.54, R=0.70-0.85），说明用户需根据具体应用场景谨慎选择PII检测工具。

Abstract: We present the first independent, systematic evaluation of OpenAI's Privacy Filter (OPF), a 1.5B-parameter bidirectional PII detector, across 42 synthetic benchmarks spanning 22 languages and 5 domains. Zero-shot, OPF achieves F1=0.855 on AI4Privacy and 0.464 on SPY medical, outperforming Presidio (0.431, 0.273) and XLM-RoBERTa (0.269, 0.111) on PII-annotated benchmarks; on multilingual NER, XLM-RoBERTa leads OPF on all 13 Indic and non-Latin languages. GPT-4o leads on medical, legal, and financial PII (SPY: 0.643 avg, Gretel: 0.527), while OPF leads on structured synthetic PII (0.71 avg) and customer support (0.60). OPF degrades sharply when PII is embedded in narrative prose: F1=0.04--0.57 on NER benchmarks and collapse for non-Latin scripts (Arabic: 0.04, Cyrillic: 0.03). Error analysis shows OPF is strongest on structurally regular PII types (email: 0.78, phone: 0.76) and weakest on culturally variable ones (person: 0.40, address: 0.49), and is recall-biased on customer-support and medical/legal PII (P=0.31--0.54, R=0.70--0.85); global precision spans 0.31--0.86 across all domains.

</details>


### [198] [Preferred, Not Safer: Pairwise Preference Is a Poor Proxy for Clinical Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02617)
*Fay Elhassan,David Sasu,Alexandra Kulinkina,Lars Henning Klein,Mary-Anne Hartley*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究基于MOOVE平台（26,804条临床医生配对偏好判断，覆盖13个LLM、736+名临床医生、28+国家），发现临床医生的配对偏好在评估大语言模型的临床安全性时是不可靠的信号。高偏好排名的模型仍可能在无害性和准确性等安全关键维度上有大量临床失败（≤-1评分），且这些失败在不同专科间分布不均。表面特征比安全关键评分更能解释偏好差异。作者提出了一种"临床调整偏好排名"方法，将配对偏好与基于量规的反馈结合，比原始Bradley-Terry强度产生更安全感知的排序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在临床决策中的应用日益广泛，但现有的评估方法（尤其是基于配对偏好排名的做法）是否能够可靠反映临床安全性仍不清楚。作者希望通过大规模临床医生反馈数据检验"偏好的模型是否安全"这一假设。

Method: 利用MOOVE平台收集的26,804条配对偏好判断和多标准量规评分（-2到+2离散量表），对13个LLM的输出进行系统性评估。通过Bradley-Terry模型计算偏好排名，同时分离安全关键维度（如无害性、准确性）与表面特征（如提示长度、模型拒绝和升级行为）对偏好的贡献，并基于此提出临床调整的偏好排名方法。

Result: 临床医生配对偏好与安全关键性能之间存在显著脱节：偏好排名高的模型仍可能在高比例的安全失败（≤-1评分）上表现不佳；失败在不同医学专科间分布不均，形成聚合排名不可见的领域特定"禁区"；大量偏好投票不携带积极安全信号；特征分解显示表面特征对偏好变异的解释力略高于安全关键量规差异。

Conclusion: 评估临床LLM时应将偏好与安全性分开报告，直接报告安全关键失败率，并在排名LLM时纳入临床校准的调整机制。单纯依赖临床医生配对偏好来评估LLM的临床安全性是不够的，需要更严谨的多维度评估框架。

Abstract: We evaluate whether clinician pairwise preferences provide a reliable signal of clinical safety in large language model (LLM) evaluation using expert feedback from MOOVE (Massive Open Online Validation and Evaluation), a clinician-led platform collecting blinded pairwise preferences alongside multi-criterion rubric ratings. Clinicians assign scores on a discrete $[-2, +2]$ scale, where negative values indicate clinically unsafe or misleading content. Using 26{,}804 pairwise judgments across outputs from 13 LLMs, contributed by more than 736 clinicians across 28+ countries, we find that clinician preference is a poor proxy for safety-critical performance. Models ranking highly under pairwise preference can still exhibit substantial rates of clinically meaningful failures ($\leq -1$) on dimensions such as \emph{Harmlessness} and \emph{Accuracy}. These failures are unevenly distributed across specialties, creating domain-specific ``no-go zones'' not visible in aggregate rankings or single-number leaderboards. We further analyze contributing factors including prompt length, refusal and escalation behavior, and the relative contributions of safety-critical versus surface-level features. A substantial fraction of preference votes carry no positive safety signal, while feature decomposition shows that surface-level characteristics explain slightly more preference variation than safety-critical rubric differences. Finally, we introduce a clinically adjusted preference ranking combining pairwise preference with rubric-derived feedback, producing a more safety-aware ordering than raw Bradley--Terry strength alone. Our findings support evaluation practices that separate preference from safety, report safety-critical failure rates directly, and incorporate clinically grounded adjustments when ranking LLMs for clinical decision making.

</details>


### [199] [Knowing the Form, Not the Function: Automatically Auditing Answer--Authority Decoupling in Legal Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02621)
*Hsien-Jyh Liao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出法律基准测试中应联合评估"答案正确性"与"法条依据（权威引注）"两个维度，而非仅以最终答案打分。通过对台湾律考238道题目的实证分析，发现四个LLM在未要求引用法条的情况下自发产生权威标记，但答案正确与权威引用之间存在双向分离——有的答案正确但遗漏正确法条，有的答案错误却引用了正确法条。作者建议采用联合评估框架，并初步验证了PRC民法场景下的可迁移性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有法律基准测试仅以最终答案得分，即使模型陈述了法律权威（如法条依据）也不予评分。但答案正确性能否作为法条依据正确性的代理指标尚不清楚，这可能导致模型遗漏正确法条却被判定为完全成功。

Method: 使用238道台湾律师资格考试题目（每题均有已验证的管辖条文），在普通推理提示（不要求引用法条）下测试四个LLM，自动联合审计答案正确性与法条依据正确性；另设计法条检索探针实验和允许引用放弃的干预实验进行补充验证，并对中国民法场景做了初步扩展。

Result: 两个维度在双向均发生分离：刑法题目中24.0–42.4%的合法回答答案正确但遗漏了正确法条，15.2–21.7%答案错误但引用了正确法条。法条检索探针和引用放弃干预实验进一步表明答案与引注行为在输出层面可独立变动。该分离在无对抗或无一致性诱导的提示下自然发生。

Conclusion: 由于法条依据具有结构可提取性和外部可验证性，这一失败可以自动化测量。本文提出对基于法条的法律基准测试采用"答案+法条"联合评估方案，以更全面地衡量模型在法律领域的表现。

Abstract: Legal benchmarks typically score final answers even when models also state legal authority. We test whether answer correctness can serve as a proxy for authority grounding. Under ordinary reasoning prompts that did not request statutory citations, four LLMs spontaneously produced authority markers across 238 Taiwan bar-examination items. Because each item has a verified governing provision, we automatically audit answer correctness and authority grounding jointly. The two dimensions dissociate in both directions. In criminal law, 24.0--42.4\% of valid responses were answer-correct but missed the gold authority, while 15.2--21.7\% were answer-incorrect but cited it. A separate statutory-retrieval probe and a permissive citation-abstention intervention further show that answer and citation behavior can move separately at the output level. Because this mismatch arises without adversarial or inconsistency-inducing prompting, answer-only scoring treats naturally occurring gold-authority misses as complete benchmark successes. Because statutory authority is structurally extractable and externally verifiable, the failure can be measured automatically. A preliminary PRC civil-law extension also observes citation-unrequested authority marking, motivating a full cross-jurisdictional joint audit. We therefore propose joint answer--authority evaluation for statute-grounded legal benchmarks.

</details>


### [200] [Speculative Correction: Draft-then-Refine Decoding for Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02625)
*Brian K Chen,Chong Wu,Kenji Kawaguchi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了扩散语言模型（DLMs）的一种简单即插即用推理模式：先完整生成草稿，再用双向扩散进行全局精炼。实验表明，同模型草稿-精炼（Flash-Flash）可在GSM8K和MBPP上显著提升准确率且速度更快；而异构级联（Mini-Flash）虽不具备一致性质量优势，但提供了有价值的质量-延迟权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散语言模型虽然能够双向修正token，但标准解码方式往往被改写为从左到右的逐块生成，未能充分利用双向精炼这一核心优势。作者希望探索一种简单的训练无关的推理模式，以充分发挥DLM双向修正的能力。

Method: 提出两种配置：（1）Flash-Flash：同一模型既作为草稿生成器（块自回归）又作为精炼器（全局双向扩散），检验模型能否通过全局精炼改进自身输出；（2）Mini-Flash：借鉴投机解码思想，由Mini模型起草完整回复，Flash模型将草稿作为可编辑初始化进行修正（称为"投机修正"）。

Result: Flash-Flash在GSM8K-384上准确率从0.848提升至0.899，且比Flash块自回归基线快1.20倍；MBPP-384从0.545提升至0.693。因果消融表明完整草稿提供了有效初始化，全局精炼在GSM8K上带来明显增益，局部精炼在MBPP和MATH上可捕获大部分增益。Mini-Flash在MATH-384上达到0.294（Flash为0.300），但速度快2.17倍。

Conclusion: 同模型草稿-精炼表明双向精炼是DLM的有用解码原语；投机修正展示了无需训练的快速DLM生成路径。但结果支持Pareto前沿解释，而非异构级联在所有场景下均匹配Flash质量的论断。

Abstract: Diffusion language models (DLMs) can revise tokens bidirectionally, but standard decoding procedures often adapt them to left-to-right generation by producing text block by block. We study a simple plug-and-play inference pattern: first generate a complete draft, then refine the full response using bidirectional diffusion. Using LLaDA2.1-Flash and LLaDA2.1-Mini, we evaluate two configurations. In Flash-Flash, the same Flash model serves as both drafter and refiner, testing whether an existing model can improve its own block-autoregressive output through global refinement. In Mini-Flash, inspired by speculative decoding, we introduce speculative correction: Mini drafts a full response, and Flash revises it as an editable initialization. Flash-Flash improves GSM8K-384 accuracy from 0.848 to 0.899 while running 1.20 times faster than the selected Flash block-autoregressive baseline, and improves MBPP-384 from 0.545 to 0.693. Latency-window-matched Flash-only controls indicate that these gains persist after targeted tuning of block-autoregressive decoding. Causal ablations indicate that completed drafts provide useful initializations: refinement from a fully masked span performs poorly, full global refinement provides a clear additional gain on GSM8K, and local refinement captures much of the gain on MBPP and MATH. Mini-Flash provides useful quality-latency trade-offs, including MATH-384 performance of 0.294 versus 0.300 for Flash while running 2.17 times faster. These results support a Pareto-frontier interpretation rather than the claim that the heterogeneous cascade uniformly matches Flash quality. Overall, same-model draft-and-refine provides evidence that bidirectional refinement is a useful decoding primitive for DLMs, while speculative correction demonstrates a training-free route to fast DLM generation.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [201] [On the missing benchmarks layer and a potential solution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02996)
*Francis F Daniel,Mauro Ibañez,Francis Perelman,Marian Basti*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出拉丁美洲缺乏AI基准层（benchmark layer）这一基础性缺口，并通过EvalsHub和其首个区域实例LatamBoard来填补——一个开放、任务优先的基准基础设施，使大学、公共机构、专业社区和企业能够发布、执行、比较和维护跨模型、工作流及代理的评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拉丁美洲缺乏AI开发的基础层——基准层。该层承担两个不可替代的功能：审计AI系统是否符合区域社会要求，以及在经济相关环境中引导AI优化。没有它，公共机构无法独立评估外国AI系统，企业也无法优化AI以SOTA性能解决本地问题。缺失的代价是双重损失：可审计性的丧失和优化方向性的丧失。

Method: 提出EvalsHub作为开放的基准基础设施，以LatamBoard为首个区域实例。其设计原则包括：任务优先（task-first）、开放性设计、激励驱动构建，支持跨模型/工作流/代理的评估发布、执行、比较和维护。核心理念是一次构建、永久测量（Built once, measured forever），可被机构和新系统发布时重复运行。

Result: 该提案尚未呈现具体实验结果或性能数据，论文处于方案提出与基础设施设计阶段，重点展示了架构理念和区域部署策略。

Conclusion: 通过建立区域基准层，拉丁美洲可以同时恢复对AI系统的可审计性和优化方向性，使公共机构能独立评估外国AI系统，企业能针对本地经济环境优化AI，从而将AI从单向依赖的技术转变为区域可控的关键基础设施。

Abstract: Latin America is missing a foundational layer for native AI development: the benchmark layer. The benchmark layer does two things no other layer can - it audits AI systems against regional social requirements and it directs AI optimization in economically relevant environments. Without it, public institutions cannot independently evaluate foreign AI systems, and companies cannot optimize AI systems to solve local problems with SOTA performance. The cost of the missing layer is dual: a loss of auditability and a loss of optimization direction over a technology that is increasingly critical infrastructure. We propose an EvalsHub, with LatamBoard as its first regional instance - an open, task-first benchmark infrastructure where universities, public institutions, professional communities, and companies can publish, execute, compare, and maintain evaluations across models, workflows, and agents. Built once, measured forever - re-run by institutions as new AI systems ship and by industry teams after every system change. Open by design and incentive-driven by construction.

</details>


### [202] [UrbanAgent: A Tool-Augmented Agent for Cross-System Urban Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03018)
*Jiayu Cao,Xingyuan Zeng,feiyu Li,Zhijing Huang,Xujie Yuan,Rongxiang Chen,Shimin Di,Libin Zheng,Jian Yin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Urban-Agent，一个面向跨系统城市任务的大语言模型智能体框架，并配套引入Urban-Eval基准。Urban-Agent通过工具增强与自适应闭环（澄清缺失信息、实时观察驱动工具调用、证据对齐）将从自然语言请求到跨系统工作流的转换可靠落地，实验显示任务成功率71%，领先最强基线10个百分点，且在多个主流模型上均保持优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代城市依赖大量数字服务运行，但服务碎片化、互操作性差，居民日常需求难以得到满足。现有数字平台、城市基础模型和智能助手仅能解决城市任务的孤立环节，难以将复杂的自然语言请求可靠地转换为可执行的跨系统工作流，存在明显的评估与能力缺口。

Method: Urban-Agent采用工具增强的智能体框架，将大语言模型的认知推理能力与工具集（代码执行、API调用、模型上下文协议MCP）相结合。通过一个自适应的闭环流程：先澄清缺失信息再行动，将工具使用锚定在实时观察上，最终将响应与观察证据和任务约束对齐。同时构建Urban-Eval基准，评估任务结果与执行质量（工具覆盖率、依赖有效性、证据可追溯性）。

Result: Urban-Agent在Urban-Eval基准上达到71%的任务成功率，比最强基线高出10个百分点。该优势在GPT-5-mini、Gemini-2.5-flash、DeepSeek-V4-flash和Qwen3-235B-A22B等多个模型上均保持一致。

Conclusion: Urban-Agent有效解决了跨系统城市任务中自然语言请求到可执行工作流的转换难题，同时Urban-Eval填补了该领域缺乏综合评估基准的空白，为城市智能服务的落地提供了重要参考。

Abstract: Modern cities rely on an increasing number of digital services to operate, but residents' daily needs are still difficult to meet. Services are fragmented and have little interoperability, placing a heavy operational burden on users. Existing digital platforms, urban foundation models, and intelligent assistants each address only isolated aspects of an urban task. But they struggle to reliably convert complex natural-language requests into executable cross-system workflows. We propose Urban-Agent, a tool-augmented agent framework for cross-system urban tasks. It couples the cognitive and reasoning capabilities of a large language model with a tool-set supporting code execution, API calls, and Model Context Protocol. Through one adaptive closed loop, it clarifies missing information before acting, grounds tool use in live observations, and aligns the final response with observed evidence and task constraints. To address the evaluation gap, we introduce Urban-Eval, a benchmark specifically designed for cross-system urban request. Unlike prior benchmarks that assess either general tool use or urban knowledge and reasoning, Urban-Eval evaluates both task results and execution quality, including required tool coverage, dependency validity, and evidence traceability. Experimental results indicate that Urban-Agent reaches a 71% task success rate, 10 points above the strongest baseline. This lead holds across GPT-5-mini, Gemini-2.5-flash, DeepSeek-V4-flash, and Qwen3-235B-A22B.

</details>


### [203] [DiffImaginE: Imagine to Verify Entity Types with Diffusio](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03025)
*Feng Zhang,Feiyu Han,Rongxin Yang,Yang Liu,Yancheng Chen,Rui Wang,Yingguang Yang,Tian Xueyun,Chongyang Zhang,Hao Zheng,Xu Kefu,Congjing Ran,Fuhai Chen,Bin Chong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出DiffImaginE，将多模态命名实体识别（MNER）的类型验证任务形式化为条件潜空间扩散推理，利用类型条件降噪器预测注入噪声，通过降噪误差作为类型条件负对数似然的ELBO一致替代量，实现对候选实体类型的概率化排序与分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MNER验证方法采用"想象-比较"框架，将每个（实体片段，类型）对映射为单一预测视觉特征，将多样化的视觉表征压缩为单一原型，且仅提供兼容性分数而缺乏显式概率语义，无法对类型假设进行严格的似然比较。

Method: DiffImaginE将MNER类型验证建模为条件潜扩散推理：给定片段局部视觉证据，类型条件降噪器预测注入标准潜变量的噪声，降噪误差作为类型条件负对数似然的ELBO一致代理；采用无分类器引导的扩散评分器，使用Min-SNR加权训练，直接以逐类型扩散分数作为分类logits监督，学习跨噪声水平的聚合，并利用对偶抽样降低蒙特卡洛比较方差。

Result: 在Twitter-2015和Twitter-2017数据集上，DiffImaginE在相同编码器、辅助目标和评估协议下，相较匹配的确定性ImaginE基线取得一致性能提升，消融实验与配对显著性检验支持了结果的有效性。

Conclusion: 分类器自由引导可锐化类型后验分布，对偶配对在相同降噪器成本下能够降低方差，验证了基于扩散推理的概率化类型验证框架的有效性。

Abstract: Multimodal named entity recognition (MNER) determines whether each candidate span and entity-type hypothesis is supported by joint textual and visual evidence. Existing imagine-and-compare verifiers map each (span, type) pair to one predicted visual feature, compressing diverse visual realisations into a single prototype and providing a compatibility score without explicit probabilistic semantics. We introduce DiffImaginE, which formulates MNER type verification as conditional latent diffusion inference. Given span-localised visual evidence, a type-conditioned denoiser predicts noise injected into its standardised latent. The resulting denoising error provides an ELBO-consistent surrogate for type-conditional negative log-likelihood, allowing competing type hypotheses to be ranked by how well they explain the observation. DiffImaginE retains a standard multimodal encoder stack and replaces the deterministic verifier with a classifier-free-guided diffusion scorer trained using Min-SNR weighting. We directly supervise per-type diffusion scores as classification logits, learn aggregation across noise levels, and use antithetic sampling to reduce Monte Carlo comparison variance. Our analysis shows that classifier-free guidance sharpens the induced type posterior and characterises when antithetic pairing reduces variance at equal denoiser cost. Experiments on Twitter-2015 and Twitter-2017 show consistent gains over a matched deterministic ImaginE control under the same encoder, auxiliary objectives, and evaluation protocol, supported by ablations and paired significance tests.

</details>


### [204] [Evaluating Counterfactual Sensitivity to Patient Information in Medication-Safety Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03028)
*Zhitian Hou,Yuhang Liu,Pengkai Wang,Zeyu Liu,Guanghao Zhu,Zheng Liu,Shuo Cai,Congkai Xie,Zhijie Sang,Kun Zeng,Hongxia Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MedPIC-Bench基准，用于评估LLM在患者特定用药安全推理中的表现。通过配对反事实问题测试，发现所有模型在反事实问题上的准确率显著下降（从63.6%降至45.1%），表明模型普遍存在"记住规则但不会条件应用"的缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学评估使用孤立、固定的场景，模型可能仅通过记忆药物-风险关联就能答对，而无法证明其是否真正利用了患者信息来决定规则是否适用。需要一个能衡量条件规则应用能力的基准。

Method: 构建MedPIC-Bench基准，包含来源可验证的建议和专家验证的问题，结合指南遵循问题与配对反事实问题（通过受控改变患者信息来改变规则适用性），共467个问题，沿六个临床和推理维度进行标注，并评估28个医学专用、通用和专有LLM。

Result: 所有模型在反事实问题上表现更差，平均准确率从63.6%降至45.1%。当显式患者属性直接指向熟悉的禁忌症时模型表现良好，但当患者信息需要收窄或撤销安全警告时则表现挣扎。模型的推理过程常常承认了患者信息的改变，但最终答案仍保留之前的判断。医学专用LLM的平均反事实表现反而落后于通用LLM。

Conclusion: MedPIC-Bench使条件规则应用变得可测量，揭示了静态用药安全准确率无法反映患者特定可靠性的局限。模型在条件推理能力上存在系统性脆弱性，即使医学专用模型也不例外。

Abstract: Applying a valid medication-safety rule when its patient-specific conditions are not met can produce an incorrect decision. Existing medical evaluations largely use isolated and fixed scenarios. A model may therefore answer correctly by recalling a drug-risk association without showing that it used patient information to decide whether the rule applies. To address this gap, we introduce MedPIC-Bench, a benchmark of source-verifiable recommendations and expert-validated questions for patient-specific medication-safety reasoning.
